		Logger.warn("Features.Core.Guard", "identityAdopted", { country = countryName })
	end
	return resolved
end

---Evaluate the gate.
---@return table { canPlan, canSend, reason, country }
function Guard.check()
	local verdict = { canPlan = false, canSend = false, reason = nil, country = nil }

	if not Session.isCorrectPlace() then
		verdict.reason = string.format("not Conquer The World WW2 (PlaceId %s)", tostring(game.PlaceId))
	elseif not Session.replicatorPresent() then
		verdict.reason = "client disconnected (no ClientReplicator)"
	else
		local fresh, freshness = Tick.isFresh()

		if not fresh then
			verdict.reason = freshness
		else
			local country, countryReason = Session.ourCountry()

			if country == nil then
				verdict.reason = countryReason
			else
				-- [FIX-RENAME-1] Nama negara bisa BERGANTI saat formable. Ownership hanya
				-- di-rebuild pada ChildAdded/perubahan warna, jadi nama baru tak dikenal
				-- -> unit.owner tidak cocok dengan world.me -> world.ourUnits kosong -> AI diam.
				Guard.reconcileIdentity(country)

				if not Settings.get("ai.enabled") then
					verdict.reason = "AI switched off (ai.enabled)"
					verdict.country = country
				else
					verdict.canPlan = true
					verdict.country = country
					verdict.canSend = Settings.get("ai.sendOrders") == true

					if verdict.canSend then
						verdict.reason = "planning and sending"
					else
						verdict.reason = "dry-run: planning, orders logged but not sent (ai.sendOrders off)"
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	Guard.lastVerdict = verdict

	return verdict
end

---True when a remote may be sent right now (re-evaluates the gate).
---@return boolean, string
function Guard.canSend()
	local verdict = Guard.check()
	return verdict.canSend, verdict.reason
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Core.Guard", Guard)

]=]

-- >>> PART 2/3 START

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Core/Scheduler"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Core/Scheduler

	Decides when the AI thinks (DESIGN Â§3.3, user requirement: combat reactions are instant).
	- Combat cycles run in the same frame as the event that needs them.
	- Tick cycles run on each detected day tick.
	Each cycle runs under its own trace id; its cost goes to the Profiler.

	=== BUGFIX-C4 (2026-10-01) ===
	Scheduler.reactionLatencies is now bounded via Stats.pushBounded.
]]

local Scheduler = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Profiler
local Profiler = require("Utility/Profiler")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Utility.Stats
local Stats = require("Utility/Stats")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

---@module Game.Units
local Units = require("Game/Units")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Core.Guard
local Guard = require("Features/Core/Guard")

---@module Features.Front.WorldView
local WorldView = require("Features/Front/WorldView")

---@module Features.Front.FrontPlanner
local FrontPlanner = require("Features/Front/FrontPlanner")

---@module Features.Orders.OrderExecutor
local OrderExecutor = require("Features/Orders/OrderExecutor")

-- Constants.
local EMPTY_PATH_JSON = "[]"
-- [BUGFIX-C4] Cap sample windows so a long session does not leak memory.
local MAX_LATENCY_SAMPLES = 256

-- State.
Scheduler.hooks = {}
Scheduler.scheduled = false
Scheduler.lastCombatCycleClock = 0
Scheduler.MIN_COMBAT_INTERVAL = 0.15
-- [PERF-SCH-1] Adaptive throttle: interval dasar × multiplier menyesuaikan
-- biaya cycle terakhir. Saat cycle jadi berat (>40ms), kita melambat supaya
-- main thread tidak kehabisan budget render.
Scheduler.lastCycleCost = 0
Scheduler.MAX_ADAPTIVE_INTERVAL = 0.9
Scheduler.ADAPTIVE_HEADROOM = 1.6
Scheduler.tickPending = nil
Scheduler.combatTriggers = {}
Scheduler.lastStates = {}
Scheduler.cycleCount = 0
Scheduler.lastCycle = nil
Scheduler.reactionLatencies = {}
Scheduler.running = false
Scheduler.context = { capitalTile = nil, enemies = {} }

---True when a land tile is ours or touches ours.
---@param tileName string
---@param me string
---@return boolean
local function nearOurLand(tileName, me)
	if Ownership.tileOwner[tileName] == me then
		return true
	end

	local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]

	if record == nil then
		return false
	end

	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(record.landNeighbours) do
		if Ownership.tileOwner[neighbourName] == me then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

---Run the pending cycle.
local function runPending()
	Scheduler.scheduled = false
	local cycleStartClock = os.clock()

	if Scheduler.running then
		Scheduler.scheduled = true
		task.defer(runPending)
		return
	end

	local tickInfo = Scheduler.tickPending
	local triggers = Scheduler.combatTriggers
	Scheduler.tickPending = nil
	Scheduler.combatTriggers = {}

	local kind = "combat"
	if tickInfo ~= nil then
		kind = "tick"
	elseif #triggers == 0 then
		return
	else
		-- [PERF-SCH-1] Interval adaptif: maks(dasar, biaya-cycle-terakhir × headroom).
		local adaptiveInterval = math.min(
			Scheduler.MAX_ADAPTIVE_INTERVAL,
			math.max(Scheduler.MIN_COMBAT_INTERVAL,
				Scheduler.lastCycleCost * Scheduler.ADAPTIVE_HEADROOM)
		)
		if os.clock() - Scheduler.lastCombatCycleClock < adaptiveInterval then
			for _, t in ipairs(triggers) do
				table.insert(Scheduler.combatTriggers, t)
			end
			Scheduler.scheduled = true
			task.delay(adaptiveInterval, runPending)
			return
		end
		Scheduler.lastCombatCycleClock = os.clock()
	end

	Scheduler.running = true
	Scheduler.cycleCount += 1

	local label = kind .. "_" .. tostring(Scheduler.cycleCount)

	local cycleOk, failure = pcall(Tracer.withTrace, "cycle." .. kind, function()
		local traceId = Tracer.context()
		local verdict = Guard.check()

		if Scheduler.hooks.onVerdict ~= nil then
			Scheduler.hooks.onVerdict(verdict)
		end

		if not verdict.canPlan or not Settings.get("front.enabled") then
			Logger.debug("Features.Core.Scheduler", "cycleSkipped", { kind = kind, reason = verdict.reason })
			Formula.flushSuppressed(traceId)
			return
		end

		local world = Profiler.run("Cycle_WorldView", WorldView.build, verdict.country, label)
		world.tickNumber = (tickInfo ~= nil and tickInfo.tickNumber) or 0
		world.cycleKind = kind
		Scheduler.context = { capitalTile = world.capitalTile, enemies = world.enemies }
		OrderExecutor.pruneCommitments(world)

		local plan, analysis = Profiler.run("Cycle_FrontPlan", FrontPlanner.plan, world, OrderExecutor.blockedUnits(), OrderExecutor.commitments(), kind)
		local summary = Profiler.run("Cycle_Execute", OrderExecutor.execute, world, plan, verdict.canSend)

		if #triggers > 0 then
			local earliest = math.huge
			local names = {}

			for _, trigger in ipairs(triggers) do
				earliest = math.min(earliest, trigger.clock)
				names[trigger.event] = (names[trigger.event] or 0) + 1
			end

			local latency = os.clock() - earliest
			-- [BUGFIX-C4] bounded
			Stats.pushBounded(Scheduler.reactionLatencies, latency, MAX_LATENCY_SAMPLES)

			Logger.info("Features.Core.Scheduler", "reaction", {
				kind = kind,
				triggers = names,
				firstTrigger = triggers[1],
				latencySeconds = latency,
				orders = summary,
			})
		end

		if kind == "tick" and Scheduler.hooks.onTick ~= nil then
			Profiler.run("Cycle_TickHooks", Scheduler.hooks.onTick, world, analysis, plan)
		end

		if kind == "tick" then
			Formula.flushSuppressed("none")
		end

		Scheduler.lastCycle = {
			kind = kind,
			label = label,
			plan = plan:summary(),
			orders = summary,
			serverTime = world.serverTime,
		}

		if Scheduler.hooks.onPlanned ~= nil then
			Scheduler.hooks.onPlanned(world, plan, summary)
		end

		Formula.flushSuppressed(traceId)
	end)

	Scheduler.running = false
	-- [PERF-SCH-1] Simpan biaya cycle untuk adaptive throttle berikutnya.
	-- Rata-rata eksponensial: 70% cost lama + 30% cost baru.
	Scheduler.lastCycleCost = Scheduler.lastCycleCost * 0.7 + (os.clock() - cycleStartClock) * 0.3

	if not cycleOk then
		-- [FIX-H1] Logger adalah no-op, jadi error siklus harus muncul lewat warn/print.
		local cycleMessage = string.format("[CTW_WarAI] cycle %s (%s) aborted: %s",
			tostring(label), tostring(kind), tostring(failure))
		shared.CTW_CycleError = {
			label = label,
			kind = kind,
			message = tostring(failure),
			at = os.clock(),
		}
		warn(cycleMessage)
		print(cycleMessage)
		Logger.warn("Features.Core.Scheduler", "cycleAborted", { kind = kind, label = label, message = tostring(failure) })
	end
end

---Ask for a cycle at the end of this frame.
local function schedule()
	if not Scheduler.scheduled then
		Scheduler.scheduled = true
		task.defer(runPending)
	end
end

---Queue a combat trigger (hot path, untraced twin of Scheduler.requestCombat).
---@param event string
---@param tileName string?
local function requestCombat(event, tileName)
	table.insert(Scheduler.combatTriggers, { event = event, tile = tileName, clock = os.clock(), serverTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() })
	schedule()
end

---Request a tick cycle.
---@param info table
function Scheduler.requestTick(info)
	Scheduler.tickPending = info
	schedule()
end

---Request a combat cycle for an event (hot path: called from world signals).
---@param event string
---@param tileName string?
function Scheduler.requestCombat(event, tileName)
	requestCombat(event, tileName)
end

---True when a unit has no tiles of an order left (currentPath absent or "[]").
---@param record table
---@return boolean
local function pathFinished(record)
	local remaining = record.model:GetAttribute("currentPath")
	return remaining == nil or remaining == EMPTY_PATH_JSON
end

---Classify a unit attribute change into a combat trigger. Hot path.
---@param record table
---@param attributeName string
---@param value any
local function onUnitChanged(record, attributeName, value)
	local me = Units.ourCountryName

	if me == nil then
		return
	end

	if attributeName == "state" then
		local previous = Scheduler.lastStates[record]
		Scheduler.lastStates[record] = value

		local ours = record.owner == me
		local hostile = not ours and record.owner ~= nil and Scheduler.context.enemies[record.owner] == true

		if ours then
			if value == "Retreating" then
				requestCombat("ourRetreat", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Dead" then
				requestCombat("ourDeath", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Idle" and previous == "Waiting" then
				requestCombat("orderDropped", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Idle" and previous ~= nil and previous ~= "Idle" and pathFinished(record) then
				requestCombat("ourUnitIdle", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Defending" then
				requestCombat("ourUnitAttacked", record.tile)
			end
		elseif hostile and nearOurLand(record.tile, me) then
			if value == "Attacking" then
				requestCombat("enemyAttack", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Retreating" then
				requestCombat("enemyRetreat", record.tile)
			elseif value == "Dead" then
				requestCombat("enemyDeath", record.tile)
			end
		end
	elseif attributeName == "dieOnNextRetreat" and value == true and Scheduler.context.enemies[record.owner] and nearOurLand(record.tile, me) then
		requestCombat("enemyFlagged", record.tile)
	end
end

---Connect the triggers.
---@param maid table
---@param hooks table { onTick(world, analysis, plan), onPlanned(world, plan, summary), onVerdict(verdict) }
function Scheduler.attach(maid, hooks)
	Scheduler.hooks = hooks

	maid:give(Tick.dayTick:connect(function(info)
		Scheduler.requestTick(info)
	end))

	maid:give(Units.unitChanged:connect(onUnitChanged))

	maid:give(Units.unitMoved:connect(function(record, fromTile, toTile)
		local me = Units.ourCountryName

		if me == nil then
			return
		end

		if record.owner == me then
			if fromTile == Scheduler.context.capitalTile then
				requestCombat("capitalLeft", fromTile)
			end
		elseif Scheduler.context.enemies[record.owner] and nearOurLand(toTile, me) then
			requestCombat("enemyAdvance", toTile)
		end
	end))

	maid:give(Units.unitAdded:connect(function(record)
		local me = Units.ourCountryName

		if me ~= nil and Scheduler.context.enemies[record.owner] and nearOurLand(record.tile, me) then
			requestCombat("enemyAppeared", record.tile)
		end
	end))

	maid:give(Units.unitRemoved:connect(function(record, tileName)
		local me = Units.ourCountryName
		Scheduler.lastStates[record] = nil

		if me == nil or record.superseded then
			return
		end

		if tileName == Scheduler.context.capitalTile then
			requestCombat("capitalEmptied", tileName)
		elseif nearOurLand(tileName, me) then
			requestCombat("unitGone", tileName)
		end
	end))

	maid:give(Ownership.ownerChanged:connect(function(tileName, previous, current)
		local me = Units.ourCountryName

		if me ~= nil and previous == me then
			requestCombat("tileLost", tileName)
		end
	end))

	for _, key in ipairs({ "ai.enabled", "ai.sendOrders", "front.enabled", "front.attack" }) do
		maid:give(Settings.onChanged(key, function()
			Scheduler.requestTick({ reason = "setting " .. key })
		end))
	end

	Logger.info("Features.Core.Scheduler", "attached", {})
end

---Refresh the event filters' facts for our country (attach, country change).
---@param countryName string?
function Scheduler.refreshContext(countryName)
	if countryName == nil or not Ownership.isAlive(countryName) then
		Scheduler.context = { capitalTile = nil, enemies = {} }
		return
	end

	Scheduler.context = {
		capitalTile = Ownership.capitalTile(countryName),
		enemies = Ownership.enemiesOf(countryName),
	}
end

---Latency statistics of combat reactions (seconds from the first trigger to the orders).
---@return table
function Scheduler.latencyStats()
	local samples = Scheduler.reactionLatencies

	if #samples == 0 then
		return { count = 0 }
	end

	return {
		count = #samples,
		median = Stats.median(samples),
		max = Stats.max(samples),
	}
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Core.Scheduler", Scheduler)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Core/Settings"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Core/Settings

	The registry of every user-adjustable value (derivation tier T4).
]]

local Settings = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

-- Constants.
local SUPPORTED_KINDS = { toggle = true, slider = true, dropdown = true }

-- State.
Settings.definitions = {}
Settings.byKey = {}
Settings.values = {}
Settings.listeners = {}

---Declare a setting.
---@param definition table
---@return table
function Settings.define(definition)
	assert(type(definition.key) == "string", "Settings.define: key is required")
	assert(Settings.byKey[definition.key] == nil, string.format("Settings.define: '%s' is already defined", tostring(definition.key)))
	assert(type(definition.tab) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): tab is required", definition.key))
	assert(type(definition.section) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): section is required", definition.key))
	assert(type(definition.label) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): label is required", definition.key))
	assert(SUPPORTED_KINDS[definition.kind], string.format("Settings.define(%s): unsupported kind '%s'", definition.key, tostring(definition.kind)))
	assert(type(definition.unit) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): unit is required", definition.key))
	assert(definition.default ~= nil, string.format("Settings.define(%s): default is required", definition.key))
	assert(type(definition.defaultSource) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): defaultSource (provenance of the default) is required", definition.key))
	assert(type(definition.effect) == "string", string.format("Settings.define(%s): effect (what it changes) is required", definition.key))

	if definition.kind == "slider" then
		assert(type(definition.min) == "number" and type(definition.max) == "number", string.format("Settings.define(%s): slider needs min and max", definition.key))
		assert(type(definition.rounding) == "number", string.format("Settings.define(%s): slider needs rounding (decimal places)", definition.key))
		assert(definition.default >= definition.min and definition.default <= definition.max, string.format("Settings.define(%s): default outside [min, max]", definition.key))
	end

	if definition.kind == "dropdown" then
		assert(type(definition.choices) == "table" and #definition.choices > 0, string.format("Settings.define(%s): dropdown needs choices", definition.key))
		assert(table.find(definition.choices, definition.default), string.format("Settings.define(%s): default is not one of the choices", definition.key))
	end

	if definition.kind == "toggle" then
		assert(type(definition.default) == "boolean", string.format("Settings.define(%s): toggle default must be boolean", definition.key))
	end

	table.insert(Settings.definitions, definition)
	Settings.byKey[definition.key] = definition
	Settings.values[definition.key] = definition.default
	Settings.listeners[definition.key] = {}

	Logger.debug("Features.Core.Settings", "defined", {
		key = definition.key,
		kind = definition.kind,
		unit = definition.unit,
		default = definition.default,
		defaultSource = definition.defaultSource,
		min = definition.min,
		max = definition.max,
		choices = definition.choices,
	})

	return definition
end

---Read a setting. Unknown keys raise.
---@param key string
---@return any
function Settings.get(key)
	if Settings.byKey[key] == nil then
		return error(string.format("Settings.get: unknown setting '%s'", tostring(key)))
	end

	return Settings.values[key]
end

---Validate a candidate value against a definition.
---@param definition table
---@param value any
---@return string?
function Settings.validate(definition, value)
	if definition.kind == "toggle" and type(value) ~= "boolean" then
		return "toggle value must be boolean"
	end

	if definition.kind == "slider" then
		if type(value) ~= "number" then
			return "slider value must be a number"
		end

		if value < definition.min or value > definition.max then
			return string.format("slider value %s outside [%s, %s]", tostring(value), tostring(definition.min), tostring(definition.max))
		end
	end

	if definition.kind == "dropdown" and not table.find(definition.choices, value) then
		return string.format("'%s' is not one of the choices", tostring(value))
	end

	return nil
end

---Change a setting, validate it, log the change with its origin, and notify listeners.
---@param key string
---@param value any
---@param origin string
function Settings.set(key, value, origin)
	local definition = Settings.byKey[key]

	if definition == nil then
		return error(string.format("Settings.set: unknown setting '%s'", tostring(key)))
	end

	local rejection = Settings.validate(definition, value)
	if rejection ~= nil then
		return error(string.format("Settings.set(%s): %s", key, rejection))
	end

	local previous = Settings.values[key]
	if previous == value then
		return
	end

	Settings.values[key] = value

	Logger.info("Features.Core.Settings", "changed", {
		key = key,
		previous = previous,
		current = value,
		unit = definition.unit,
		origin = origin,
	})

	for _, listener in ipairs(Settings.listeners[key]) do
		listener(value, previous)
	end
end

---Register a listener for one setting.
---@param key string
---@param listener function
---@return function
function Settings.onChanged(key, listener)
	local listeners = Settings.listeners[key]

	if listeners == nil then
		return error(string.format("Settings.onChanged: unknown setting '%s'", tostring(key)))
	end

	table.insert(listeners, listener)

	return function()
		local index = table.find(listeners, listener)
		if index ~= nil then
			table.remove(listeners, index)
		end
	end
end

---Definitions grouped by tab, then by section.
---@return table array of { name, sections = array of { name, definitions } }
function Settings.layout()
	local tabList = {}
	local tabByName = {}

	for _, definition in ipairs(Settings.definitions) do
		local tab = tabByName[definition.tab]

		if tab == nil then
			tab = { name = definition.tab, sections = {}, sectionByName = {} }
			tabByName[definition.tab] = tab
			table.insert(tabList, tab)
		end

		local section = tab.sectionByName[definition.section]

		if section == nil then
			section = { name = definition.section, definitions = {} }
			tab.sectionByName[definition.section] = section
			table.insert(tab.sections, section)
		end

		table.insert(section.definitions, definition)
	end

	return tabList
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Core.Settings", Settings)

]=]

	end

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Economy/EconomyFormulas"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Economy/EconomyFormulas

	Declared economy arithmetic (economy.md Â§3, REFERENCE R10, R13, R15).
]]

local EconomyFormulas = {}

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.factoryPrice",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "CityInfoLocalScript:288 mirror, matched on 19/19 (rec A) and 104/104 (rec B) purchases (economy.md Â§3.4)",
	expression = "factoryPrice * (1 + (cityFactories + cityQueue) * factoryPriceMultiplier + (countryFactories + allQueued) * factoryTotalPriceMultiplier)",
	unit = "money",
	inputs = {
		factoryPrice = "Techs.factoryPrice",
		cityFactories = "city factories",
		cityQueue = "city factoryBuildQueue",
		factoryPriceMultiplier = "Techs.factoryPriceMultiplier",
		countryFactories = "country factories",
		allQueued = "sum of factoryBuildQueue over the country's cities",
		factoryTotalPriceMultiplier = "Techs.factoryTotalPriceMultiplier",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		return inputs.factoryPrice * (1 + (inputs.cityFactories + inputs.cityQueue) * inputs.factoryPriceMultiplier + (inputs.countryFactories + inputs.allQueued) * inputs.factoryTotalPriceMultiplier)
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { factoryPrice = 1000, cityFactories = 0, cityQueue = 0, factoryPriceMultiplier = 0.25, countryFactories = 0, allQueued = 0, factoryTotalPriceMultiplier = 0.1 }, expected = 1000, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { factoryPrice = 1000, cityFactories = 2, cityQueue = 0, factoryPriceMultiplier = 0.25, countryFactories = 10, allQueued = 0, factoryTotalPriceMultiplier = 0.1 }, expected = 2500, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.defensePrice",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "CityInfoLocalScript mirror, 11/11 observed (economy.md Â§3.5)",
	expression = "cityDefensesPrice * (1 + (defenses + queue) * cityDefensesPriceMultiplier)",
	unit = "money",
	inputs = {
		cityDefensesPrice = "Techs.cityDefensesPrice",
		defenses = "city defenses",
		queue = "city defenseBuildQueue",
		cityDefensesPriceMultiplier = "Techs.cityDefensesPriceMultiplier",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		return inputs.cityDefensesPrice * (1 + (inputs.defenses + inputs.queue) * inputs.cityDefensesPriceMultiplier)
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { cityDefensesPrice = 3000, defenses = 0, queue = 0, cityDefensesPriceMultiplier = 0.1 }, expected = 3000, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { cityDefensesPrice = 3000, defenses = 5, queue = 0, cityDefensesPriceMultiplier = 0.1 }, expected = 4500, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.trainingCost",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "TryIncreaseTarget charge, exact on 60/60 and 90/90 raises (ground-commands.md Â§3.7)",
	expression = "0 if target < count else ((costIncrease - 1) * target + 1) * baseCost",
	unit = "money",
	inputs = { target = "class target", count = "class count", costIncrease = "class costIncrease", baseCost = "class baseCost" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		if inputs.target < inputs.count then
			return 0
		end

		return ((inputs.costIncrease - 1) * inputs.target + 1) * inputs.baseCost
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { target = 3, count = 3, costIncrease = 1.15, baseCost = 2000 }, expected = 2900, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { target = 0, count = 1, costIncrease = 1.15, baseCost = 2000 }, expected = 0, tolerance = 0 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.manpowerPrice",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "ArmyManagementLocalScript mirror, 11/11 observed purchases (economy.md Â§3.6)",
	expression = "(1 + (divisions + manpower / 10000) * bonusCoef) * bonusBasePrice",
	unit = "money",
	inputs = { divisions = "country divisions", manpower = "country manpower", divisionSize = "ArmyCommon.divisionSize", bonusCoef = "Army.bonusCoef", bonusBasePrice = "Army.bonusBasePrice" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		return (1 + (inputs.divisions + inputs.manpower / inputs.divisionSize) * inputs.bonusCoef) * inputs.bonusBasePrice
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { divisions = 10, manpower = 5000, divisionSize = 10000, bonusCoef = 0.4, bonusBasePrice = 4000 }, expected = 20800, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "heal.perTick",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "replenish x Utils.secondsPerDay x Techs.replenishSpeedModifier; the product 12 and 13.2 (modifier 1.1) match 2,048/2,048 recorded heals",
	expression = "replenish * secondsPerDay * replenishSpeedModifier",
	unit = "health per day tick",
	inputs = { replenish = "ArmyCommon.troopNameToInfo[troop].replenish", secondsPerDay = "Utils.secondsPerDay", replenishSpeedModifier = "Techs.replenishSpeedModifier" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		return inputs.replenish * inputs.secondsPerDay * inputs.replenishSpeedModifier
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { replenish = 4, secondsPerDay = 3, replenishSpeedModifier = 1 }, expected = 12, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { replenish = 4, secondsPerDay = 3, replenishSpeedModifier = 1.1 }, expected = 13.2, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.equipmentFactories",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "production = factories x outputCoef x factoryProductivity (212/212 and 385/385 updates, economy.md Â§3.3)",
	expression = "0 if stock >= remainingNeed else ceil(demandPerTick / (outputCoef * factoryProductivity))",
	unit = "factories",
	inputs = {
		stock = "item stock",
		remainingNeed = "equipment still needed to heal every damaged unit of the item to 100",
		demandPerTick = "equipment drawn per tick by the damaged units",
		outputCoef = "item outputCoef",
		factoryProductivity = "Techs.factoryProductivity",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		if inputs.stock >= inputs.remainingNeed then
			return 0
		end

		return math.ceil(inputs.demandPerTick / (inputs.outputCoef * inputs.factoryProductivity))
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { stock = 0, remainingNeed = 40, demandPerTick = 8, outputCoef = 0.5, factoryProductivity = 1 }, expected = 16, tolerance = 0 },
		{ inputs = { stock = 50, remainingNeed = 40, demandPerTick = 8, outputCoef = 0.5, factoryProductivity = 1 }, expected = 0, tolerance = 0 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.readinessFactories",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "user rule 2026-09-25: at war the split is balanced; a front unit that is hit heals from the next tick on",
	expression = "0 if stock >= frontDrawPerTick else ceil((frontDrawPerTick - stock) / (outputCoef * factoryProductivity))",
	unit = "factories",
	inputs = {
		stock = "item stock",
		frontDrawPerTick = "sum of consumption of our units in contact that use the item",
		outputCoef = "item outputCoef",
		factoryProductivity = "Techs.factoryProductivity",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		if inputs.stock >= inputs.frontDrawPerTick then
			return 0
		end

		return math.ceil((inputs.frontDrawPerTick - inputs.stock) / (inputs.outputCoef * inputs.factoryProductivity))
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { stock = 2, frontDrawPerTick = 8, outputCoef = 0.5, factoryProductivity = 1 }, expected = 12, tolerance = 0 },
		{ inputs = { stock = 9, frontDrawPerTick = 8, outputCoef = 0.5, factoryProductivity = 1 }, expected = 0, tolerance = 0 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.factoryPaybackTicks",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "a Consumer Goods factory adds Economy.consumerGoodsTaxIncome per tick (economy.md Â§3.2)",
	expression = "price / consumerGoodsTaxIncome",
	unit = "day ticks",
	inputs = { price = "econ.factoryPrice", consumerGoodsTaxIncome = "Economy.consumerGoodsTaxIncome" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		return inputs.price / inputs.consumerGoodsTaxIncome
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { price = 1000, consumerGoodsTaxIncome = 40 }, expected = 25, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "econ.roundTicksLeft",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "round end = Map.startTime + workspace.maxGameLength (client countdown, war-and-loop.md Â§3.7)",
	expression = "(startTime + maxGameLength - now) / tickPeriod",
	unit = "day ticks",
	inputs = { startTime = "workspace.Map.startTime", maxGameLength = "workspace.maxGameLength", now = "GetServerTimeNow", tickPeriod = "tick.periodSeconds" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		return (inputs.startTime + inputs.maxGameLength - inputs.now) / inputs.tickPeriod
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { startTime = 0, maxGameLength = 9000, now = 3000, tickPeriod = 3 }, expected = 2000, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

---Evaluate one economy formula (for tests and tools).
---@param id string
---@param inputs table
---@return any
function EconomyFormulas.evaluate(id, inputs)
	return Formula.evaluate(id, inputs)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Economy.EconomyFormulas", EconomyFormulas)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Economy/EconomyPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Economy/EconomyPlanner

	Runs once per day tick, after the front plan (DESIGN Â§7). Lexicographic order of spending:
	1. production split (free), gated by economy.enabled;
	2. training and manpower, gated by economy.enabled;
	3. research (Features/Economy/ResearchPlanner), gated by research.enabled;
	4. national focus, gated by focus.enabled;
	5. city defences on our city tiles the front analysis marks unsafe, gated by economy.enabled;
	6. factory purchase (one per tick in the cheapest city when it pays back before the round ends),
	   gated by factories.enabled (independent of economy.enabled). Research reservation is honoured:
	   money reserved for a pending tech purchase is never spent on a factory.
]]

local EconomyPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

---@module Game.CountryState
local CountryState = require("Game/CountryState")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Economy.EconomyFormulas
local EconomyFormulas = require("Features/Economy/EconomyFormulas")

---@module Features.Economy.ResearchPlanner
local ResearchPlanner = require("Features/Economy/ResearchPlanner")

---@module Features.Economy.FocusPlanner
local FocusPlanner = require("Features/Economy/FocusPlanner")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local CONSUMER_GOODS_ITEM = "Consumer Goods"
local FULL_HEALTH = 100
local TIER = "E"

local ESSENTIAL_ITEM_KEYWORDS = {
	"plane", "aircraft", "fighter", "bomber", "cas",
	"missile", "rocket", "nuke", "uranium",
}
local ESSENTIAL_MIN_FACTORIES = 2
local ESSENTIAL_LOW_STOCK = 3

local function isEssentialItem(itemName)
	local lower = string.lower(itemName)
	for _, kw in ipairs(ESSENTIAL_ITEM_KEYWORDS) do
		if string.find(lower, kw, 1, true) then return true end
	end
	return false
end

EconomyPlanner.lastSummary = nil

local function equipmentDemand(world, healPerTick)
	local TroopCommon = GameModules.get("TroopCommon")
	local demand = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		local itemName = unit.replenishSuppliesName

		if itemName ~= nil and unit.health < FULL_HEALTH and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, unit.tile) then
			local consumption = TroopCommon.GetTroopConsumption(unit.model, world.meInstance)
			local ticksToFull = math.ceil((FULL_HEALTH - unit.health) / healPerTick)
			local entry = demand[itemName]

			if entry == nil then
				entry = { demandPerTick = 0, remainingNeed = 0, units = 0 }
				demand[itemName] = entry
			end

			entry.demandPerTick += consumption
			entry.remainingNeed += consumption * ticksToFull
			entry.units += 1
		end
	end

	return demand
end

local function frontDraw(world, analysis)
	local draw = {}

	if analysis == nil then
		return draw
	end

	local TroopCommon = GameModules.get("TroopCommon")

	for _, unit in pairs(analysis.contact) do
		local itemName = unit.replenishSuppliesName

		if itemName ~= nil then
			draw[itemName] = (draw[itemName] or 0) + TroopCommon.GetTroopConsumption(unit.model, world.meInstance)
		end
	end

	return draw
end

local function planProduction(world, country, analysis)
	local items = CountryState.productionItems(country)
	local atWar = next(world.enemies) ~= nil
	local totalFactories = Resolver.requireAttribute(country, "factories", "number")
	local productivity = CountryState.folderNumber(country, "Techs", "factoryProductivity")
	local replenishSpeed = CountryState.folderNumber(country, "Techs", "replenishSpeedModifier")
	local secondsPerDay = GameModules.get("Utils").secondsPerDay
	local troopInfo = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").troopNameToInfo

	local healPerTick = nil
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		local info = troopInfo[unit.troopName]

		if info ~= nil and info.replenish ~= nil then
			local perTick = Formula.evaluate("heal.perTick", { replenish = info.replenish, secondsPerDay = secondsPerDay, replenishSpeedModifier = replenishSpeed })

			if healPerTick == nil or perTick < healPerTick then
				healPerTick = perTick
			end
		end
	end

	if totalFactories <= 0 then
		return { skipped = true, totalFactories = totalFactories }
	end

	local demand = {}
	if healPerTick ~= nil then
		demand = equipmentDemand(world, healPerTick)
	end
	local draw = frontDraw(world, analysis)
	local desired = {}
	local assigned = 0

	local reservedForEssential = 0
	for _, item in ipairs(items) do
		if isEssentialItem(item.name) and (item.stock or 0) < ESSENTIAL_LOW_STOCK then
			reservedForEssential += ESSENTIAL_MIN_FACTORIES
		end
	end
	local combatBudget = math.max(0, totalFactories - reservedForEssential)

	if atWar then
		for _, item in ipairs(items) do
			local need = demand[item.name]
			local itemDraw = draw[item.name]
			local factories = 0

			if need ~= nil then
				factories = Formula.evaluate("econ.equipmentFactories", {
					stock = item.stock,
					remainingNeed = need.remainingNeed,
					demandPerTick = need.demandPerTick,
					outputCoef = item.outputCoef,
					factoryProductivity = productivity,
				})
			end

			if itemDraw ~= nil then
				factories = math.max(factories, Formula.evaluate("econ.readinessFactories", {
					stock = item.stock,
					frontDrawPerTick = itemDraw,
					outputCoef = item.outputCoef,
					factoryProductivity = productivity,
				}))
			end

			if factories > 0 then
				desired[item.name] = math.min(factories, combatBudget - assigned)
				if desired[item.name] < 0 then desired[item.name] = 0 end
				assigned += desired[item.name]
			end
		end
	elseif next(demand) ~= nil then
		Logger.info("Features.Economy.EconomyPlanner", "peaceHealingUnfunded", {
			demand = demand,
			reason = "user rule: at peace every factory makes Consumer Goods; damaged units heal only from existing stock",
		})
	end

	local essentialAlloc = {}
	for _, item in ipairs(items) do
		if assigned >= totalFactories then break end
		if isEssentialItem(item.name) and (desired[item.name] or 0) == 0 then
			local stock = item.stock or 0
			if stock < ESSENTIAL_LOW_STOCK then
				local want = math.min(ESSENTIAL_MIN_FACTORIES, totalFactories - assigned)
				if want > 0 then
					desired[item.name] = want
					assigned += want
					essentialAlloc[item.name] = want
					Logger.info("Features.Economy.EconomyPlanner", "essentialProduction", {
						item = item.name, factories = want, stock = stock,
						reason = "essential item under stock threshold",
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- [AIR-r5] Smart plane production: alokasikan factory tambahan untuk pesawat
	-- berdasarkan kebutuhan front aktual, bukan hanya keyword match minimum.
	if atWar and assigned < totalFactories then
		local planeKeywords = { "cas", "fighter", "bomber" }
		local planeItems = {}
		for _, item in ipairs(items) do
			local lower = string.lower(item.name)
			for _, kw in ipairs(planeKeywords) do
				if string.find(lower, kw, 1, true) then
					table.insert(planeItems, item)
					break
				end
			end
		end
		if #planeItems > 0 then
			local spareFactories = totalFactories - assigned
			local planeBudget = math.min(spareFactories, math.max(2, math.floor(totalFactories * 0.15)))
			local perItem = math.max(1, math.floor(planeBudget / #planeItems))
			for _, pItem in ipairs(planeItems) do
				local currentDesired = desired[pItem.name] or 0
				local stock = pItem.stock or 0
				if stock < 5 and currentDesired < perItem then
					local extra = math.min(perItem - currentDesired, totalFactories - assigned)
					if extra > 0 then
						desired[pItem.name] = currentDesired + extra
						assigned += extra
						Logger.info("Features.Economy.EconomyPlanner", "smartPlaneAlloc", {
							item = pItem.name, added = extra, stock = stock,
						})
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	desired[CONSUMER_GOODS_ITEM] = totalFactories - assigned

	local changes = {}

	for _, item in ipairs(items) do
		local wanted = desired[item.name] or 0

		if wanted ~= item.factories then
			local weight = wanted / totalFactories

			if weight ~= item.factoriesValue then
				table.insert(changes, { item = item.name, from = item.factories, to = wanted, weight = weight })

				ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
					remote = "SetProductionPercentage",
					args = { item.instance, weight },
					key = "production:" .. item.name .. ":" .. tostring(weight),
					reason = string.format("%s: %d -> %d factories", item.name, item.factories, wanted),
					tier = TIER,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	return { atWar = atWar, totalFactories = totalFactories, demand = demand, frontDraw = draw, desired = desired, changes = changes, healPerTick = healPerTick, essential = essentialAlloc }
end

local function planTraining(world, country, budget)
	local classes = CountryState.armyClasses(country)
	local counterDemand = {}

	-- [FIX-B4] hubungan counter konstan 3x3; Formula.evaluate mengalokasi tabel per panggilan.
	local COUNTER_STRONG = {
		AntiTank = { Tanks = true },
		Infantry = { AntiTank = true },
		Tanks = { Infantry = true },
	}

	for className in pairs(classes) do
		counterDemand[className] = 0
		local strongAgainst = COUNTER_STRONG[className]

		for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
			if strongAgainst ~= nil and strongAgainst[enemy.class] then
				counterDemand[className] += 1
			end
		end
	end

	local ranked = {}
	for className, classState in pairs(classes) do
		table.insert(ranked, {
			id = className,
			score = counterDemand[className] - classState.count,
			facts = { demand = counterDemand[className], count = classState.count, target = classState.target, hasManpower = classState.hasManpower },
			state = classState,
		})
	end

	table.sort(ranked, function(first, second)
		if first.score ~= second.score then
			return first.score > second.score
		end

		return first.id < second.id
	end)

	-- [FIX-P1-3] recordChoice melempar error pada list kosong; ResearchPlanner/FocusPlanner sudah dijaga.
	if #ranked > 0 then
		Formula.recordChoice("E.trainingPriority", ranked, "counter demand minus our count (combat.counter > 1 against enemy units at war)")
	end

	local raised = {}
	local manpowerWanted = false
	local treasury = CountryState.treasury(country)
	local divisionSize = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").divisionSize

	for _, entry in ipairs(ranked) do
		local classState = entry.state
		local pipelineIdle = classState.target <= classState.count

		if entry.score > 0 and pipelineIdle then
			if not classState.hasManpower and treasury.manpower < divisionSize then
				manpowerWanted = true
			else
				local cost = Formula.evaluate("econ.trainingCost", {
					target = classState.target,
					count = classState.count,
					costIncrease = classState.costIncrease,
					baseCost = classState.baseCost,
				})

				if budget.money >= cost then
					budget.money -= cost
					table.insert(raised, { class = entry.id, cost = cost })

					ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
						remote = "TryIncreaseTarget",
						args = { classState.folder },
						key = "target:" .. entry.id .. ":" .. tostring(classState.target),
						reason = string.format("%s counters %d enemy units, we have %d", entry.id, counterDemand[entry.id], classState.count),
						tier = TIER,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local manpowerBought = nil
	if manpowerWanted then
		local army = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Army")
		local price = Formula.evaluate("econ.manpowerPrice", {
			divisions = treasury.divisions,
			manpower = treasury.manpower,
			divisionSize = divisionSize,
			bonusCoef = Resolver.requireAttribute(army, "bonusCoef", "number"),
			bonusBasePrice = Resolver.requireAttribute(army, "bonusBasePrice", "number"),
		})

		if budget.money >= price then
			budget.money -= price
			manpowerBought = price

			ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
				remote = "TryBuyManpower",
				args = {},
				key = "manpower:" .. tostring(treasury.divisions) .. ":" .. tostring(math.floor(treasury.manpower)),
				reason = "training of a needed class is blocked by manpower",
				tier = TIER,
			})
		end
	end

	return { counterDemand = counterDemand, raised = raised, manpowerWanted = manpowerWanted, manpowerPrice = manpowerBought }
end

local function planDefense(world, country, analysis, budget)
	if analysis == nil then
		return nil
	end

	local techs = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Techs")
	-- [FIX-P1-2] CombatModel memperlakukan atribut ini opsional (default 1); jangan melempar di sini.
	local strengthModifier = Resolver.optionalAttribute(techs, "cityDefensesStrengthModifier", "number", "TroopCommon.lua:515 reads it with default 1") or 1
	local cities = {}

	for _, city in ipairs(CountryState.cities(country)) do
		cities[city.tileName] = city
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		local city = cities[entry.tile]

		if city ~= nil and city.defenses + city.defenseBuildQueue < city.maxDefenses then
			local hypothetical = CombatModel.placedOn(entry.unit, entry.tile)
			hypothetical.defense = entry.unit.defense + strengthModifier
			local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, entry.tile, hypothetical)

			if not threat.breaks then
				local price = Formula.evaluate("econ.defensePrice", {
					cityDefensesPrice = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "cityDefensesPrice", "number"),
					defenses = city.defenses,
					queue = city.defenseBuildQueue,
					cityDefensesPriceMultiplier = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "cityDefensesPriceMultiplier", "number"),
				})

				if budget.money >= price then
					budget.money -= price

					ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
						remote = "PurchaseDefense",
						args = { city.folder },
						key = "defense:" .. city.name .. ":" .. tostring(city.defenses + city.defenseBuildQueue),
						reason = string.format("one more defence holds %s against the adjacent stack (ratio %.2f -> %.2f)", entry.tile, entry.threat.ratio, threat.ratio),
						tier = TIER,
					})

					return { city = city.name, price = price, ratioBefore = entry.threat.ratio, ratioAfter = threat.ratio }
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

-- [FIX-FORT] Spek fortifikasi dari ctw_ui_source.txt L20685-21020.
local FORT_ROMAN = { "I", "II", "III" }
local FORT_MAX_LEVEL = 3
local FORT_DEFAULT_PRICE = 5000
local FORT_DEFAULT_PRICE_MULTIPLIER = 0.75

---Level fort yang diizinkan oleh tech "Fortifications I/II/III" (harus berurutan, progress == 1).
---@param country Instance
---@return number 0..3
local function fortTechLevel(country)
	local techs = country:FindFirstChild("Techs")
	if techs == nil then return 0 end

	local level = 0
	for index = 1, FORT_MAX_LEVEL do
		local tech = techs:FindFirstChild("Fortifications " .. FORT_ROMAN[index])
		if tech == nil or tech:GetAttribute("progress") ~= 1 then break end
		level = index
	end
	return level
end

---Jumlah fort yang sedang diantre di sebuah tile (ctw_ui_source.txt L20775).
local function fortQueueCount(tile)
	local queued = tile:GetAttribute("fortBuildQueue")
	if type(queued) == "number" then return math.max(0, math.floor(queued)) end
	if tile:GetAttribute("fortBuildStart") then return 1 end
	return 0
end

---Harga fort berikutnya pada level tertentu (ctw_ui_source.txt L20690).
local function fortPrice(techs, level)
	local base = techs:GetAttribute("tileFortPrice") or FORT_DEFAULT_PRICE
	local multiplier = techs:GetAttribute("tileFortPriceMultiplier") or FORT_DEFAULT_PRICE_MULTIPLIER
	return base * (1 + (level - 1) * multiplier)
end

---Beli SATU fort di tile kita yang paling terancam, bila tech dan uang mencukupi.
---Dipanggil dari EconomyPlanner.run saat Settings "forts.enabled" aktif.
---@return table? { tile, level, price, ratio } atau { blocked = alasan }
local function planFort(world, country, analysis, budget)
	if analysis == nil then return nil end

	local techs = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Techs")
	local techLevel = fortTechLevel(country)
	if techLevel == 0 then
		return { blocked = "tech Fortifications belum diteliti" }
	end

	local mapModel = Resolver.requireChild(workspace, "Map")

	-- Kandidat: tile kita yang sedang terancam, lalu tile yang bolong (gap).
	local candidates = {}
	local seen = {}
	local function consider(tileName, ratio)
		if tileName == nil or seen[tileName] then return end
		if world.tileOwner[tileName] ~= world.me then return end
		seen[tileName] = true
		table.insert(candidates, { tile = tileName, ratio = ratio or 0 })
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		consider(entry.tile, entry.threat and entry.threat.ratio or 0)
	end
	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.gaps) do
		consider(entry.tile, 1)
	end

	table.sort(candidates, function(first, second)
		if first.ratio ~= second.ratio then return first.ratio > second.ratio end
		return first.tile < second.tile
	end)

	for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
		local tile = mapModel:FindFirstChild(candidate.tile)
		if tile ~= nil then
			local level = (tile:GetAttribute("fortLevel") or 0) + fortQueueCount(tile) + 1
			if level <= FORT_MAX_LEVEL and techLevel >= level then
				local price = fortPrice(techs, level)
				if budget.money >= price then
					budget.money -= price
					ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
						remote = "PurchaseFort",
						args = { tile },
						key = "fort:" .. candidate.tile .. ":" .. tostring(level),
						reason = string.format("bunker level %d di tile terancam %s (ratio %.2f)",
							level, candidate.tile, candidate.ratio),
						tier = TIER,
					})
					return { tile = candidate.tile, level = level, price = price, ratio = candidate.ratio }
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

local function planFactory(country, budget)
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()

	if period == nil then
		return nil
	end

	local techs = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Techs")
	local economy = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Economy")
	local mapModel = Resolver.requireChild(workspace, "Map")
	local cities = CountryState.cities(country)
	local treasury = CountryState.treasury(country)

	local allQueued = 0
	for _, city in ipairs(cities) do
		allQueued += city.factoryBuildQueue
	end

	local best = nil
	for _, city in ipairs(cities) do
		if city.factories + city.factoryBuildQueue < city.maxFactories then
			local price = Formula.evaluate("econ.factoryPrice", {
				factoryPrice = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "factoryPrice", "number"),
				cityFactories = city.factories,
				cityQueue = city.factoryBuildQueue,
				factoryPriceMultiplier = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "factoryPriceMultiplier", "number"),
				countryFactories = treasury.factories,
				allQueued = allQueued,
				factoryTotalPriceMultiplier = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "factoryTotalPriceMultiplier", "number"),
			})

			if best == nil or price < best.price then
				best = { city = city, price = price }
			end
		end
	end

	if best == nil then
		return nil
	end

	local payback = Formula.evaluate("econ.factoryPaybackTicks", {
		price = best.price,
		consumerGoodsTaxIncome = Resolver.requireAttribute(economy, "consumerGoodsTaxIncome", "number"),
	})

	local ticksLeft = Formula.evaluate("econ.roundTicksLeft", {
		startTime = Resolver.requireAttribute(mapModel, "startTime", "number"),
		maxGameLength = Resolver.requireAttribute(workspace, "maxGameLength", "number"),
		now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
		tickPeriod = period,
	})

	local manpowerPrice = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "factoryManpowerPrice", "number")

	if payback >= ticksLeft or budget.money < best.price or treasury.manpower < manpowerPrice then
		return { city = best.city.name, price = best.price, paybackTicks = payback, ticksLeft = ticksLeft, bought = false }
	end

	budget.money -= best.price

	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "PurchaseFactory",
		args = { best.city.folder },
		key = "factory:" .. best.city.name .. ":" .. tostring(best.city.factories + best.city.factoryBuildQueue),
		reason = string.format("cheapest factory (%.0f) pays back in %.0f of %.0f ticks left", best.price, payback, ticksLeft),
		tier = TIER,
	})

	return { city = best.city.name, price = best.price, paybackTicks = payback, ticksLeft = ticksLeft, bought = true }
end

function EconomyPlanner.run(world, analysis)
	local country = world.meInstance
	local treasury = CountryState.treasury(country)
	local budget = { money = treasury.money }

	local summary = { money = treasury.money }

	if Settings.get("economy.enabled") then
		summary.production = planProduction(world, country, analysis)
		summary.training = planTraining(world, country, budget)
	end

	if Settings.get("research.enabled") then
		summary.research = ResearchPlanner.run(world, country, budget)
	end

	if Settings.get("focus.enabled") then
		summary.focus = FocusPlanner.run(world, country)
	end

	if Settings.get("economy.enabled") then
		summary.defense = planDefense(world, country, analysis, budget)
	end

	if Settings.get("factories.enabled") then
		local reservedMoney = (summary.research and summary.research.reservedMoney) or 0

		if budget.money > reservedMoney then
			local spendable = { money = budget.money - reservedMoney }
			summary.factory = planFactory(country, spendable)
			budget.money = spendable.money + reservedMoney
		end
	end

	-- Fort dibeli paling akhir supaya tidak menyerobot anggaran riset/factory.
	if Settings.get("forts.enabled") then
		summary.fort = planFort(world, country, analysis, budget)
	end

	summary.moneyLeftInPlan = budget.money
	EconomyPlanner.lastSummary = summary

	Logger.info("Features.Economy.EconomyPlanner", "planned", summary)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Economy.EconomyPlanner", EconomyPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Economy/FocusPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Economy/FocusPlanner

	Runs one national focus at a time (a second one needs a gamepass, economy.md Â§4).
]]

local FocusPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Utility.Serializer
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

---@module Game.CountryState
local CountryState = require("Game/CountryState")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local COMPLETE = 1
local TIER = "F"
local JUSTIFICATION_COST_ATTRIBUTES = { "SameJustPP", "NeutralJustPP", "OppositeJustPP" }
local EFFECT_CLASSES = {
	{ name = "combat", fragments = { "attack", "defense", "defence", "division", "infantry", "tank", "anti-tank", "experience" } },
	{ name = "manpower", fragments = { "manpower", "healing", "replenish" } },
	{ name = "economy", fragments = { "income", "tax", "factor", "production", "research", "consumer" } },
}
local RANK_REST = #EFFECT_CLASSES + 1

local function classify(effectLinesText, focus)
	if effectLinesText == nil then
		if focus ~= nil and focus.instance ~= nil then
			local raw = focus.instance:GetAttribute("effectText")
			if type(raw) == "string" and raw ~= "" then
				effectLinesText = raw
			end
		end
	end

	if effectLinesText == nil then
		if focus ~= nil and focus.ppCost <= 30 then
			return 3, "cheap focus (no effectLines, ppCost <= 30)"
		end
		return RANK_REST, "no effectLines"
	end

	local lowered = string.lower(effectLinesText)

	for rank, effectClass in ipairs(EFFECT_CLASSES) do
		for _, fragment in ipairs(effectClass.fragments) do
			if string.find(lowered, fragment, 1, true) then
				return rank, effectClass.name .. " ('" .. fragment .. "')"
			end
		end
	end

	return RANK_REST, "no class fragment"
end

function FocusPlanner.run(world, country)
	local state = CountryState.focuses(country)

	if #state.current > 0 then
		return { running = state.current }
	end

	local diplomacy = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Diplomacy")
	local reserve = 0
	for _, attributeName in ipairs(JUSTIFICATION_COST_ATTRIBUTES) do
		reserve = math.max(reserve, Resolver.requireAttribute(diplomacy, attributeName, "number"))
	end

	local politicalPower = Resolver.requireAttribute(country, "politicalPower", "number")
	-- [FIX-A8] reserve = MAX biaya justifikasi bisa >= cap PP -> AI tidak pernah memulai focus lagi.
	reserve = math.min(reserve, math.max(0, math.floor(politicalPower * 0.5)))
	local ranked = {}

	for focusName, focus in pairs(state.focuses) do
		if focus.progress < COMPLETE and not focus.purchased then
			local unlocked = true

			for _, requiredName in ipairs(focus.required) do
				local requiredFocus = state.focuses[requiredName]

				if requiredFocus == nil or requiredFocus.progress < COMPLETE then
					unlocked = false
				end
			end

			if unlocked and politicalPower - focus.ppCost >= reserve then
				local rank, why = classify(focus.effectLines, focus)

				table.insert(ranked, {
					id = focusName,
					score = rank,
					facts = { class = why, ppCost = focus.ppCost, duration = focus.duration },
					focus = focus,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(ranked, function(first, second)
		if first.score ~= second.score then
			return first.score < second.score
		end

		if first.focus.duration ~= second.focus.duration then
			return first.focus.duration < second.focus.duration
		end

		return first.id < second.id
	end)

	if #ranked == 0 then
		return { chosen = nil, politicalPower = politicalPower, reserve = reserve }
	end

	Formula.recordChoice("F.nextFocus", ranked, "effect class, then shortest duration; PP above the justification reserve")

	local chosen = ranked[1]

	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "TryStartFocus",
		args = { chosen.focus.instance },
		key = "focus:" .. chosen.id,
		reason = string.format("%s (%s), %d PP of %.1f, reserve %d for the user's diplomacy", chosen.id, chosen.facts.class, chosen.focus.ppCost, politicalPower, reserve),
		tier = TIER,
	})

	Logger.debug("Features.Economy.FocusPlanner", "effectLines", { focus = chosen.id, text = chosen.focus.effectLines and Serializer.toJsonSafe(chosen.focus.effectLines, {}) or nil })

	return { chosen = chosen.id, politicalPower = politicalPower, reserve = reserve }
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Economy.FocusPlanner", FocusPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Economy/ResearchPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Economy/ResearchPlanner

	Keeps the research slots busy (DESIGN Â§7.4).
]]

local ResearchPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Game.CountryState
local CountryState = require("Game/CountryState")

---@module Features.Economy.TechCatalog
local TechCatalog = require("Features/Economy/TechCatalog")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local COMPLETE = 1
local TIER = "R"

-- [RESEARCH-FIXED] Prioritas riset hardcoded — user tinggal terima jadi.
-- Urutan array = prioritas (index 1 = paling tinggi).
-- Nama tech yang tidak ada di tech tree cukup diabaikan (tidak match = boost 0).
local BUILTIN_RESEARCH_PRIORITY = {
	"Infantry Equipment II",
	"Infantry Equipment III",
	"Housing I",
	"Infantry Equipment IV",
	"Mechanization I",
	"Mechanization II",
	"Anti Tank I",
	"Anti Tank II",
	"Anti Tank III",
	"Tank Parts I",
	"Tank Parts II",
	"Tank Parts III",
	"Tank Parts IV",
	"Housing II",
	"Housing III",
	"Mechanization III",
	"CAS I",
	"Fighter I",
	"CAS II",
	"Fighter II",
	"Infantry Armor II",
	"Infantry Armor III",
	"Infantry Armor IV",
	"Infantry Attack Equipment I",
	"Infantry Attack Equipment II",
	"Support Equipment I",
	"Support Equipment II",
	"Support Equipment III",
	"Tank Turret I",
	"Tank Turret II",
	"Tank Turret III",
	"Tank Turret IV",
	"Bomber I",
	"Bomber II",
	"Industry I",
	"Industry II",
	"Uranium Mining",
	"Uranium Enrichment",
	"Nuke",
}

-- Basis boost. Tech di posisi 1 = -1000, posisi 2 = -999, dst.
-- Nilai negatif ini jauh lebih besar dari score tier (1..5), jadi tech di
-- daftar SELALU dipilih lebih dulu dibanding tech di luar daftar.
local PRIORITY_BOOST_BASE = 1000

local function priorityBoost(techName)
	for index, name in ipairs(BUILTIN_RESEARCH_PRIORITY) do
		if name == techName then
			return -(PRIORITY_BOOST_BASE - index)
		end
	end
	return 0
end

local TECH_BLACKLIST = {
}

local TECH_BLACKLIST_KEYWORDS = {
	"Intelligence",
}

local function isBlacklisted(techName)
	if TECH_BLACKLIST[techName] then return true end
	for _, keyword in ipairs(TECH_BLACKLIST_KEYWORDS) do
		if string.find(techName, keyword, 1, true) then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function fieldedClasses(world, country)
	local classes = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		classes[unit.class] = true
	end

	for className in pairs(CountryState.armyClasses(country)) do
		classes[className] = true
	end

	return classes
end

function ResearchPlanner.run(world, country, budget)
	local slots = CountryState.researchSlots(country)
	local freeSlot = nil
	local inSlot = {}

	local slotNames = {}
	for slotName in pairs(slots) do
		table.insert(slotNames, slotName)
	end
	table.sort(slotNames)

	for _, slotName in ipairs(slotNames) do
		local techName = slots[slotName]

		if techName == false then
			if freeSlot == nil then
				freeSlot = slotName
			end
		else
			inSlot[techName] = true
		end
	end

	if freeSlot == nil then
		return { freeSlot = false, reservedMoney = 0 }
	end

	local techs = CountryState.techs(country)
	local fielded = fieldedClasses(world, country)
	local ranked = {}

	for techName, tech in pairs(techs) do
		local entry = TechCatalog.entry(techName)

			if entry ~= nil and tech.progress < COMPLETE and not inSlot[techName]
				and not isBlacklisted(techName) then
			local unlocked = true
			local missing = nil

			for _, requiredName in ipairs(entry.required) do
				local requiredTech = techs[requiredName]

				if requiredTech == nil or requiredTech.progress < COMPLETE then
					unlocked = false
					missing = requiredName
				end
			end

			local tier = entry.tier
			if entry.class ~= nil and not fielded[entry.class] then
				tier = TechCatalog.TIERS.other
			end

			if unlocked then
				table.insert(ranked, {
					id = techName,
					score = tier + priorityBoost(techName),
					facts = { tier = tier, duration = tech.duration, price = tech.price, purchased = tech.purchased, evidence = entry.evidence },
					tech = tech,
				})
			else
				Logger.trace("Features.Economy.ResearchPlanner", "locked", { tech = techName, missing = missing })
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(ranked, function(first, second)
		if first.score ~= second.score then
			return first.score < second.score
		end

		if first.tech.duration ~= second.tech.duration then
			return first.tech.duration < second.tech.duration
		end

		if first.tech.price ~= second.tech.price then
			return first.tech.price < second.tech.price
		end

		return first.id < second.id
	end)

	if #ranked == 0 then
		return { freeSlot = freeSlot, chosen = nil, reservedMoney = 0 }
	end

	Formula.recordChoice("R.nextTech", ranked, "TechCatalog tier, then shortest duration, then price")

	local chosen = ranked[1]

	if chosen.tech.purchased then
		ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
			remote = "StartResearch",
			args = { chosen.tech.instance },
			key = "start:" .. chosen.id,
			reason = string.format("slot %s is free; %s is paid", freeSlot, chosen.id),
			tier = TIER,
		})

		return { freeSlot = freeSlot, chosen = chosen.id, action = "start", reservedMoney = 0 }
	end

	local atWar = next(world.enemies) ~= nil
	local reserveCap
	if atWar then
		reserveCap = math.floor(budget.money * 0.25)
	else
		reserveCap = math.floor(budget.money * 0.75)
	end

	if budget.money >= chosen.tech.price then
		budget.money -= chosen.tech.price

		ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
			remote = "TryPurchaseTech",
			args = { chosen.tech.instance },
			key = "buy:" .. chosen.id,
			reason = string.format("slot %s is free; best candidate %s (tier %d)", freeSlot, chosen.id, chosen.score),
			tier = TIER,
		})

		return { freeSlot = freeSlot, chosen = chosen.id, action = "purchase", price = chosen.tech.price, reservedMoney = 0 }
	end

	local needed = chosen.tech.price - budget.money
	local reserve = math.min(needed, reserveCap)
	return { freeSlot = freeSlot, chosen = chosen.id, action = "saving", price = chosen.tech.price, reservedMoney = reserve }
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Economy.ResearchPlanner", ResearchPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Economy/TechCatalog"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Economy/TechCatalog

	What each research does, derived from the game's own research catalogue
	(ReplicatedStorage.Common.ResearchCommon.techs, T3).
]]

local TechCatalog = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

-- Constants.
local TIER_UNIT_LEVEL = 1
local TIER_HEALING = 2
local TIER_BOOST = 3
local TIER_ECONOMY = 4
local TIER_OTHER = 5
local HEALING_FRAGMENT = "healing speed"
local ECONOMY_FRAGMENTS = { "factory limit", "manpower" }
local DIGITS_PATTERN = "%d"
local CLASS_NAMES = { "AntiTank", "Infantry", "Tanks" }

TechCatalog.TIERS = { unitLevel = TIER_UNIT_LEVEL, healing = TIER_HEALING, boost = TIER_BOOST, economy = TIER_ECONOMY, other = TIER_OTHER }

TechCatalog.entries = nil

function TechCatalog.build()
	local ResearchCommon = GameModules.get("ResearchCommon")
	local ArmyCommon = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon")

	local statsTextByTroop = {}
	for troopName in pairs(ArmyCommon.troopNameToInfo) do
		statsTextByTroop[troopName] = ArmyCommon.GetTroopBaseStatsRTF(troopName)
	end

	local entries = {}
	local counts = {}

	for techName, tech in pairs(ResearchCommon.techs) do
		local description = type(tech.description) == "string" and tech.description or ""
		local entry = { tier = TIER_OTHER, required = tech.required or {}, evidence = "no rule matched" }

		for troopName, statsText in pairs(statsTextByTroop) do
			if statsText ~= "" and string.find(description, statsText, 1, true) then
				entry.tier = TIER_UNIT_LEVEL
				entry.troopName = troopName
				entry.class = string.gsub(troopName, DIGITS_PATTERN, "")
				entry.evidence = "description embeds GetTroopBaseStatsRTF(" .. troopName .. ")"
			end
		end

		if entry.tier == TIER_OTHER and ArmyCommon.upgrades[techName] ~= nil then
			for attributeName in pairs(ArmyCommon.upgrades[techName]) do
				for _, className in ipairs(CLASS_NAMES) do
					local statPart = string.match(attributeName, "^" .. className .. "(%a+)Boost$")

					if statPart == "Attack" or statPart == "Defense" then
						entry.tier = TIER_BOOST
						entry.class = className
						entry.stat = statPart
						entry.evidence = "ArmyCommon.upgrades." .. attributeName
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if entry.tier == TIER_OTHER then
			local lowered = string.lower(description)

			if string.find(lowered, HEALING_FRAGMENT, 1, true) then
				entry.tier = TIER_HEALING
				entry.evidence = "description mentions '" .. HEALING_FRAGMENT .. "'"
			else
				for _, fragment in ipairs(ECONOMY_FRAGMENTS) do
					if entry.tier == TIER_OTHER and string.find(lowered, fragment, 1, true) then
						entry.tier = TIER_ECONOMY
						entry.evidence = "description mentions '" .. fragment .. "'"
					end
				end
			end
		end

		entries[techName] = entry
		counts[entry.tier] = (counts[entry.tier] or 0) + 1
	end

	TechCatalog.entries = entries

	Logger.info("Features.Economy.TechCatalog", "built", { techs = counts })

	return entries
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Economy.TechCatalog", TechCatalog)

function TechCatalog.entry(techName)
	if TechCatalog.entries == nil then
		TechCatalog.build()
	end

	return TechCatalog.entries[techName]
end

return TechCatalog

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Diplomacy/PeacetimePrep"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Diplomacy/PeacetimePrep

	=== [PEACETIME GUARDS REV-2 â€” anti muter-muter] ===

	FIX 2026-10-01:
	- countGuards menghitung unit di SEMUA staging tiles (border + 1 hop), bukan hanya border langsung.
	- Unit yang SEDANG bergerak ke staging (targetTile di staging) juga dihitung.
	- Setiap cycle, semua unit yang sudah di staging di-HOLD supaya tidak diambil tier lain.
	- Skip reassign unit yang sudah dekat / sedang menuju staging.
]]

local PeacetimePrep = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Front.Tactics
local Tactics = require("Features/Front/Tactics")

---@module Features.Front.ThreatHeat
local ThreatHeat = require("Features/Front/ThreatHeat")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

-- Constants.
local TIER = "P"
local MIN_GUARDS_PER_BORDER = 4
local CAPITAL_DEFENSE_KEEP = 2
local CAPITAL_RADIUS_HOPS = 2
local BORDER_RADIUS_HOPS = 1
local MIN_UNIT_HEALTH = 60
local HEAT_AVOID_THRESHOLD = 40
local MAX_UNITS_PER_CYCLE = 20

-- State.
PeacetimePrep.lastSummary = nil
PeacetimePrep.borderSummary = {}

local function bordersByNeighbor(world)
	-- [FIX-B3b] identik dengan Ownership.borderNeighbours; jangan hitung ulang per siklus.
	return Ownership.borderNeighbours(world)
end

local function protectedCapitalUnitIds(world)
	local capitalTile = world.capitalTile
	if capitalTile == nil or world.tileOwner[capitalTile] ~= world.me then return {} end

	local seen = { [capitalTile] = 0 }
	local queue = { capitalTile }
	local head = 1
	local near = {}

	while head <= #queue do
		local cur = queue[head]
		head += 1
		local d = seen[cur]
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[cur]
		-- [FIX-P1-6] tile bisa basi setelah rebuild; jangan nil-index.
		if record == nil then break end
		if d <= CAPITAL_RADIUS_HOPS then
			-- [FIX-A0] capital bisa ter-stack; unitsByTile hanya melihat satu unit.
			for _, u in ipairs(world.unitsOnTile[cur] or {}) do
				if u.owner == world.me then
					table.insert(near, u)
				end
			end
			for _, nbr in ipairs(record.landNeighbours) do
				if seen[nbr] == nil then
					seen[nbr] = d + 1
					table.insert(queue, nbr)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(near, function(a, b)
		local ah = a.health or 0
		local bh = b.health or 0
		if ah ~= bh then return ah > bh end
		return a.id < b.id
	end)

	local protected = {}
	local keep = math.min(CAPITAL_DEFENSE_KEEP, #near)
	for i = 1, keep do
		protected[near[i].id] = true
	end
	return protected
end

local function buildAllStagingTiles(world, borders)
	local staging = {}
	for borderTile in pairs(borders) do
		if world.tileOwner[borderTile] == world.me then
			staging[borderTile] = true
		end
	end

	if BORDER_RADIUS_HOPS <= 0 then return staging end

	local seeds = {}
	for tile in pairs(staging) do table.insert(seeds, tile) end

	for _, borderTile in ipairs(seeds) do
		local seen = { [borderTile] = 0 }
		local queue = { borderTile }
		local head = 1
		while head <= #queue do
			local cur = queue[head]
			head += 1
			if seen[cur] < BORDER_RADIUS_HOPS then
				for _, nbr in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[cur].landNeighbours) do
					if seen[nbr] == nil and world.tileOwner[nbr] == world.me then
						seen[nbr] = seen[cur] + 1
						staging[nbr] = true
						table.insert(queue, nbr)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return staging
end

local function countGuardsInStaging(world, stagingTiles)
	local count = 0
	local seen = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if stagingTiles[unit.tile] then
			seen[unit.id] = true
			count += 1
		elseif unit.targetTile and stagingTiles[unit.targetTile] then
			seen[unit.id] = true
			count += 1
		end
	end
	return count, seen
end

local function isCandidate(world, unit, protectedIds)
	if unit.owner ~= world.me then return false end
	if protectedIds[unit.id] then return false end
	if unit.state ~= "Idle" then return false end
	if unit.flagged then return false end
	if (unit.health or 0) < MIN_UNIT_HEALTH then return false end
	if not SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, unit.tile) then return false end
	return true
end

function PeacetimePrep.run(world, plan)
	if not Settings.get("peacetime.prepare") then return nil end
	if not Settings.get("front.attack") then return nil end
	if next(world.enemies) ~= nil then return nil end
	if plan == nil then return nil end
	if world.cycleKind ~= "tick" then return PeacetimePrep.lastSummary end

	local byNeighbor = bordersByNeighbor(world)
	local neighborList = {}
	for name in pairs(byNeighbor) do
		table.insert(neighborList, name)
	end
	table.sort(neighborList)

	if #neighborList == 0 then
		PeacetimePrep.borderSummary = {}
		PeacetimePrep.lastSummary = { neighbors = {}, borders = 0, totalDeficit = 0, details = {} }
		return PeacetimePrep.lastSummary
	end

	local stagingByNeighbor = {}
	for _, name in ipairs(neighborList) do
		stagingByNeighbor[name] = buildAllStagingTiles(world, byNeighbor[name])
	end

	local protectedIds = protectedCapitalUnitIds(world)
	local summaries = {}
	local totalDeficit = 0
	local guardedUnitIds = {}

	for _, neighborName in ipairs(neighborList) do
		local stagingTiles = stagingByNeighbor[neighborName]
		local count, seen = countGuardsInStaging(world, stagingTiles)

		for unitId in pairs(protectedIds) do
			if seen[unitId] then
				count -= 1
			end
		end
		local deficit = math.max(0, MIN_GUARDS_PER_BORDER - count)

		table.insert(summaries, {
			country = neighborName,
			stagingTiles = stagingTiles,
			guards = count,
			deficit = deficit,
		})
		totalDeficit += deficit

		for unitId in pairs(seen) do
			if not protectedIds[unitId] then
				guardedUnitIds[unitId] = true
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(summaries, function(a, b)
		if a.deficit ~= b.deficit then return a.deficit > b.deficit end
		return a.country < b.country
	end)

	PeacetimePrep.borderSummary = summaries
	local details = {}
	for _, s in ipairs(summaries) do
		if s.deficit > 0 then
			table.insert(details, string.format("%s(%d/%d)", s.country, s.guards, MIN_GUARDS_PER_BORDER))
		end
	end
	PeacetimePrep.lastSummary = {
		neighbors = neighborList,
		borders = #summaries,
		totalDeficit = totalDeficit,
		details = details,
	}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if guardedUnitIds[unit.id] and plan:isAvailable(unit) then
			plan:hold(unit, "borderGuardHold", TIER,
				"already at border guard staging position")
		end
	end

	if totalDeficit == 0 then
		Logger.info("Features.Diplomacy.PeacetimePrep", "allBordersGuarded", {
			borders = #summaries,
			target = MIN_GUARDS_PER_BORDER,
		})
		return PeacetimePrep.lastSummary
	end

	local pool = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
		if isCandidate(world, unit, protectedIds) and not guardedUnitIds[unit.id] then
			table.insert(pool, unit)
		end
	end
	table.sort(pool, function(a, b)
		local aa = a.attack or 0
		local ba = b.attack or 0
		if aa ~= ba then return aa > ba end
		return a.id < b.id
	end)

	local moved = 0
	local usedStaging = {}

	for _, summary in ipairs(summaries) do
		if summary.deficit > 0 and moved < MAX_UNITS_PER_CYCLE then
			local safeStaging = {}
			for tile in pairs(summary.stagingTiles) do
				if not usedStaging[tile]
					and plan:isFreeDestination(tile)
					and ThreatHeat.at(tile) < HEAT_AVOID_THRESHOLD
				then
					safeStaging[tile] = true
				end
			end

			local filled = 0
			for _, unit in ipairs(pool) do
				if filled >= summary.deficit then break end
				if moved >= MAX_UNITS_PER_CYCLE then break end

				if plan:isAvailable(unit) then
					-- [FIX-B1] satu BFS per unit lewat predikat accept, bukan satu BFS per staging tile.
					local bestPath = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit,
						function(t) return safeStaging[t] == true end, false)
					local bestTile = nil
					if bestPath ~= nil then bestTile = bestPath[#bestPath] end

					if bestPath ~= nil then
						plan:assign(unit, bestPath, "borderGuard", TIER,
							string.format("guard border with %s (%d/%d)",
								summary.country, summary.guards + filled, MIN_GUARDS_PER_BORDER),
							{ country = summary.country, target = MIN_GUARDS_PER_BORDER })
						usedStaging[bestTile] = true
						safeStaging[bestTile] = nil
						filled += 1
						moved += 1
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if moved > 0 then
		Logger.info("Features.Diplomacy.PeacetimePrep", "borderGuardsStaged", {
			moved = moved,
			borders = #summaries,
			target = MIN_GUARDS_PER_BORDER,
			remainingDeficit = totalDeficit - moved,
		})
	end

	return PeacetimePrep.lastSummary
end

function PeacetimePrep.summary()
	local s = PeacetimePrep.borderSummary
	-- [FIX-C4] kembalikan bentuk yang sama dengan lastSummary; pemanggil baru bisa table.concat(details).
	if s == nil or #s == 0 then
		return { active = false, borders = 0, totalDeficit = 0, details = {} }
	end
	local totalDeficit = 0
	local details = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(s) do
		totalDeficit += entry.deficit
		if entry.deficit > 0 then
			table.insert(details, string.format("%s(%d/%d)",
				entry.country, entry.guards, MIN_GUARDS_PER_BORDER))
		end
	end
	return {
		active = true,
		borders = #s,
		totalDeficit = totalDeficit,
		details = details,
	}
end

function PeacetimePrep.reset(reason)
	PeacetimePrep.lastSummary = nil
	PeacetimePrep.borderSummary = {}
	Logger.info("Features.Diplomacy.PeacetimePrep", "reset", { reason = reason })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Diplomacy.PeacetimePrep", PeacetimePrep)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Military/BorderPrep"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Military/BorderPrep

	=== [MILITARY BORDER PREP] ===

	Fitur:
	1. Scan tetangga darat + power mereka (formula sama dengan WarAdvisor).
	2. Button-triggered: siapkan border vs satu negara target.
	   - Power kita >= 1.5x mereka -> kirim sampai border power ~1.5x mereka.
	   - Power kita < 1.5x mereka -> kirim semua kecuali capital guard.
	3. Gather idle: unit nganggur dikumpulin di ring capital (radius 3).
	4. Hold: unit di border tidak dicuri tier lain.
]]

local BorderPrep = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

---@module Features.Front.Tactics
local Tactics = require("Features/Front/Tactics")

-- Constants.
local TIER_PREP = "BP"
local TIER_GATHER = "G"
local STRONG_RATIO = 1.5
local CAPITAL_GUARD_KEEP = 2
local CAPITAL_GUARD_RADIUS = 2
local CAPITAL_GATHER_RADIUS = 3
local MIN_UNIT_HEALTH = 60
local MAX_PREP_UNITS = 30

-- State.
BorderPrep.active = nil
BorderPrep.pendingRequest = nil
BorderPrep.gatherEnabled = true
BorderPrep.lastPrepareResult = nil

local function powerOf(world, owner)
	local total = 0
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.unitsOf[owner] or {}) do
		if unit.state ~= "Dead" then
			total += (unit.attack or 1) * (unit.health or 1)
		end
	end
	return total
end

local function borderTilesTo(world, targetName)
	-- [FIX-B3b] ambil dari border map yang sudah dihitung sekali per siklus (read-only).
	local map = Ownership.borderNeighbours(world)
	return map[targetName] or {}
end

function BorderPrep.scan(world)
	if world == nil or world.meInstance == nil then return {} end

	-- [FIX-B3b] satu border map yang di-memo per siklus, bukan pemindaian tileOwner per negara.
	local bordersByNeighbour = Ownership.borderNeighbours(world)

	local ourPower = powerOf(world, world.me)
	local result = {}
	for name, borders in pairs(bordersByNeighbour) do
		local theirPower = powerOf(world, name)
		local borderCount = 0
		for _ in pairs(borders) do borderCount += 1 end

		table.insert(result, {
			name = name,
			power = theirPower,
			ourPower = ourPower,
			ratio = ourPower / math.max(theirPower, 1),
			borderTiles = borders,
			borderCount = borderCount,
		})
	end
	table.sort(result, function(a, b)
		if a.ratio ~= b.ratio then return a.ratio < b.ratio end
		return a.name < b.name
	end)
	return result
end

local function capitalGuardIds(world)
	local capitalTile = world.capitalTile
	if capitalTile == nil or world.tileOwner[capitalTile] ~= world.me then return {}, 0 end

	local seen = { [capitalTile] = 0 }
	local queue = { capitalTile }
	local head = 1
	local near = {}

	while head <= #queue do
		local cur = queue[head]
		head += 1
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[cur]
		-- [FIX-P1-6] tile bisa basi setelah rebuild; jangan nil-index.
		if record == nil then break end
		if seen[cur] <= CAPITAL_GUARD_RADIUS then
			-- [FIX-A0] capital bisa ter-stack; unitsByTile hanya melihat satu unit.
			for _, u in ipairs(world.unitsOnTile[cur] or {}) do
				if u.owner == world.me then
					table.insert(near, u)
				end
			end
			for _, nbr in ipairs(record.landNeighbours) do
				if seen[nbr] == nil then
					seen[nbr] = seen[cur] + 1
					table.insert(queue, nbr)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(near, function(a, b)
		local ap = (a.attack or 0) * (a.health or 0)
		local bp = (b.attack or 0) * (b.health or 0)
		if ap ~= bp then return ap > bp end
		return a.id < b.id
	end)

	local ids = {}
	local totalPower = 0
	local keep = math.min(CAPITAL_GUARD_KEEP, #near)
	for i = 1, keep do
		ids[near[i].id] = true
		totalPower += (near[i].attack or 0) * (near[i].health or 0)
	end
	return ids, totalPower
end

local function candidateUnits(world, plan, protected, alreadyAtBorder)
	local pool = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
		if unit.owner == world.me
			and not protected[unit.id]
			and not alreadyAtBorder[unit.id]
			and not unit.flagged
			and unit.state == "Idle"
			and (unit.health or 0) >= MIN_UNIT_HEALTH
			and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, unit.tile)
		then
			table.insert(pool, unit)
		end
	end
	table.sort(pool, function(a, b)
		local ap = (a.attack or 0) * (a.health or 0)
		local bp = (b.attack or 0) * (b.health or 0)
