		if ap ~= bp then return ap > bp end
		return a.id < b.id
	end)
	return pool
end

local function powerAtBorder(world, borders)
	local total = 0
	local ids = {}
	for tile in pairs(borders) do
		-- [FIX-A0] border sering berisi stack; unitsByTile hanya satu unit sehingga power kurang dihitung.
		for _, u in ipairs(world.unitsOnTile[tile] or {}) do
			if u.owner == world.me then
				total += (u.attack or 0) * (u.health or 0)
				ids[u.id] = true
			end
		end
	end
	return total, ids
end

function BorderPrep.prepare(targetName, world, plan)
	if world == nil or plan == nil then
		return { ok = false, reason = "no world/plan" }
	end
	if next(world.enemies) ~= nil then
		return { ok = false, reason = "at war â€” border prep disabled" }
	end

	local borders = borderTilesTo(world, targetName)
	local borderCount = 0
	for _ in pairs(borders) do borderCount += 1 end
	if borderCount == 0 then
		return { ok = false, reason = targetName .. " bukan tetangga darat" }
	end

	local ourTotal = powerOf(world, world.me)
	local theirTotal = powerOf(world, targetName)
	local protected, capitalGuardPower = capitalGuardIds(world)
	local sendablePower = math.max(0, ourTotal - capitalGuardPower)

	local targetPower
	local strong = ourTotal >= STRONG_RATIO * theirTotal
	if strong then
		targetPower = math.min(STRONG_RATIO * theirTotal, sendablePower)
	else
		targetPower = sendablePower
	end

	local atBorderPower, atBorderIds = powerAtBorder(world, borders)
	local needToSend = math.max(0, targetPower - atBorderPower)

	local result = {
		ok = true,
		target = targetName,
		ourPower = ourTotal,
		theirPower = theirTotal,
		capitalGuardPower = capitalGuardPower,
		sendablePower = sendablePower,
		targetPower = targetPower,
		atBorderPower = atBorderPower,
		needToSend = needToSend,
		ratio = ourTotal / math.max(theirTotal, 1),
		strong = strong,
		borderCount = borderCount,
		sent = {},
		sentPower = 0,
	}

	BorderPrep.lastPrepareResult = result

	local activeIds = {}
	for id in pairs(atBorderIds) do activeIds[id] = true end

	if needToSend <= 0 then
		result.reason = "border sudah cukup kuat"
		BorderPrep.active = {
			target = targetName,
			targetPower = targetPower,
			unitIds = activeIds,
			startedTick = world.tickNumber or 0,
			strong = strong,
		}
		Logger.info("Features.Military.BorderPrep", "alreadyPrepared", result)
		return result
	end

	local pool = candidateUnits(world, plan, protected, atBorderIds)
	if #pool == 0 then
		result.reason = "tidak ada unit yang bisa dikirim"
		-- [FIX-C3] jangan tinggalkan target lama tetap ter-hold saat request baru gagal.
		BorderPrep.active = nil
		return result
	end

	local stagingTiles = {}
	for tile in pairs(borders) do
		stagingTiles[tile] = true
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[tile]
		if record ~= nil then
			for _, nbr in ipairs(record.landNeighbours) do
				if world.tileOwner[nbr] == world.me then
					stagingTiles[nbr] = true
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local sentIds = {}
	local sentPower = 0
	for _, unit in ipairs(pool) do
		if sentPower >= needToSend then break end
		if #result.sent >= MAX_PREP_UNITS then break end

		if plan:isAvailable(unit) then
			local unitPower = (unit.attack or 0) * (unit.health or 0)

			-- [FIX-B1] satu BFS per unit lewat predikat accept, bukan satu BFS per staging tile.
			local bestPath = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit,
				function(t) return stagingTiles[t] == true end, false)
			local bestTile = nil
			if bestPath ~= nil then bestTile = bestPath[#bestPath] end

			if bestPath ~= nil then
				plan:assign(unit, bestPath, "borderPrep", TIER_PREP,
					string.format("border prep vs %s (target power %.0f)", targetName, targetPower),
					{ target = targetName, unitPower = unitPower })
				table.insert(result.sent, { id = unit.id, power = unitPower, tile = bestTile })
				sentIds[unit.id] = true
				sentPower += unitPower
			end
		end
	end

	result.sentPower = sentPower

	for id in pairs(sentIds) do activeIds[id] = true end
	BorderPrep.active = {
		target = targetName,
		targetPower = targetPower,
		unitIds = activeIds,
		startedTick = world.tickNumber or 0,
		strong = strong,
	}

	Logger.info("Features.Military.BorderPrep", "prepared", {
		target = targetName,
		ourPower = ourTotal, theirPower = theirTotal,
		targetPower = targetPower, atBorderPower = atBorderPower,
		sent = #result.sent, sentPower = sentPower, strong = strong,
	})
	return result
end

function BorderPrep.runPending(world, plan)
	if BorderPrep.pendingRequest == nil then return end
	local req = BorderPrep.pendingRequest
	BorderPrep.pendingRequest = nil
	if world.cycleKind ~= "tick" then
		BorderPrep.pendingRequest = req
		return
	end
	BorderPrep.prepare(req.target, world, plan)
end

function BorderPrep.holdActive(world, plan)
	if BorderPrep.active == nil then return end
	-- [FIX-A12] active tidak punya TTL dan prepare menolak saat perang, jadi hold bisa
	-- menyandera unit terkuat seumur perang. Batasi umurnya dan lepaskan saat perang mulai.
	if next(world.enemies) ~= nil
		or (world.tickNumber or 0) - (BorderPrep.active.startedTick or 0) > 40 then
		BorderPrep.active = nil
		return
	end
	for unitId in pairs(BorderPrep.active.unitIds) do
		local unit = world.unitsById[unitId]
		if unit ~= nil and plan:isAvailable(unit) then
			plan:hold(unit, "borderPrepHold", TIER_PREP,
				"reserved for border prep vs " .. tostring(BorderPrep.active.target))
		end
	end
end

---Mode lama: tarik SEMUA idle unit ke ring capital (radius 3).
---Dipanggil oleh wrapper `BorderPrep.gatherIdle` hanya saat toggle ON.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
local function gatherToCapital(world, plan)
	if next(world.enemies) ~= nil then return end
	if world.capitalTile == nil or world.tileOwner[world.capitalTile] ~= world.me then return end

	local protected = capitalGuardIds(world)

	local ringDistance = { [world.capitalTile] = 0 }
	local queue = { world.capitalTile }
	local head = 1
	while head <= #queue do
		local cur = queue[head]
		head += 1
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[cur]
		-- [FIX-P1-6] tile bisa basi setelah rebuild; jangan nil-index.
		if record == nil then break end
		if ringDistance[cur] < CAPITAL_GATHER_RADIUS then
			for _, nbr in ipairs(record.landNeighbours) do
				if ringDistance[nbr] == nil and world.tileOwner[nbr] == world.me then
					ringDistance[nbr] = ringDistance[cur] + 1
					table.insert(queue, nbr)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local ringTiles = {}
	for tile, dist in pairs(ringDistance) do
		if dist > 0 then
			table.insert(ringTiles, { tile = tile, distance = dist })
		end
	end
	table.sort(ringTiles, function(a, b)
		if a.distance ~= b.distance then return a.distance < b.distance end
		return a.tile < b.tile
	end)

	local prepIds = {}
	if BorderPrep.active ~= nil then
		for id in pairs(BorderPrep.active.unitIds) do prepIds[id] = true end
	end

	local moved = 0
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.owner == world.me
			and not protected[unit.id]
			and not prepIds[unit.id]
			and not unit.flagged
			and unit.state == "Idle"
			and (unit.health or 0) >= MIN_UNIT_HEALTH
			and ringDistance[unit.tile] == nil
			and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, unit.tile)
			and plan:isAvailable(unit)
		then
			-- [FIX-B1] satu BFS per unit. CATATAN: pemilihan tile ring kini "terdekat ke unit",
			-- bukan "terdekat ke capital" seperti sebelumnya â€” unit tetap berakhir di dalam ring.
			local bestPath = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit,
				function(t) return ringDistance[t] ~= nil and ringDistance[t] > 0 and plan:isFreeDestination(t) end, false)
			if bestPath ~= nil then
				plan:assign(unit, bestPath, "gatherIdle", TIER_GATHER,
					"gather idle near capital", {})
				moved += 1
			end
		end
	end
	if moved > 0 then
		Logger.info("Features.Military.BorderPrep", "gatherIdle", { moved = moved })
	end
end

---[SPREAD-FIX] Mode BARU saat toggle `gatherEnabled` OFF:
---Sebar unit Idle ke border tile, TANPA mengganggu:
---  * 1 unit penjaga capital  (sudah di-hold oleh DefensePlanner.capital, tier T0)
---  * 3 unit penjaga ring     (sudah di-hold oleh DefensePlanner.ensureCapitalRing, tier T0)
---Karena unit di capital/ring sudah di-assign oleh tier T0, mereka otomatis
---tidak akan diambil oleh fungsi ini (plan:isAvailable mengembalikan false).
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@return table { moved, freeBorderTiles?, reason? }
local function spreadIdleToBorder(world, plan)
	-- 1. Kumpulkan SEMUA border tile kita (vs semua negara tetangga darat).
	local borderMap = Ownership.borderNeighbours(world)
	local borderTiles = {}
	for _, tiles in pairs(borderMap) do
		for tile in pairs(tiles) do
			if world.tileOwner[tile] == world.me then
				borderTiles[tile] = true
			end
		end
	end
	if next(borderTiles) == nil then
		return { moved = 0, reason = "no border tiles" }
	end

	-- 2. Kumpulkan border tile yang masih KOSONG dan bisa ditempati.
	local freeBorder = {}
	local freeCount = 0
	for tile in pairs(borderTiles) do
		if world.unitsByTile[tile] == nil and plan:isFreeDestination(tile) then
			freeBorder[tile] = true
			freeCount += 1
		end
	end
	if freeCount == 0 then
		return { moved = 0, reason = "every border tile already occupied" }
	end

	-- 3. Kandidat: unit Idle kita yang:
	--    - bukan di border tile (sudah jaga di sana)
	--    - bukan di capital/ring (sudah di-hold tier T0)
	--    - lulus gate health/supply/flagged seperti gatherToCapital
	local candidates = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.owner == world.me
			and not unit.flagged
			and unit.state == "Idle"
			and (unit.health or 0) >= MIN_UNIT_HEALTH
			and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, unit.tile)
			and not borderTiles[unit.tile]
			and plan:isAvailable(unit)
		then
			table.insert(candidates, unit)
		end
	end
	if #candidates == 0 then
		return { moved = 0, reason = "no eligible idle units to spread" }
	end

	-- 4. Prioritas: unit paling JAUH dari capital dulu
	--    (biar capital cepat lega, unit dekat capital tetap di dekat capital).
	local capitalPos = MapGraph.tiles[world.capitalTile].position
	table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
		local aPos = MapGraph.tiles[a.tile].position
		local bPos = MapGraph.tiles[b.tile].position
		local aDist = (aPos - capitalPos).Magnitude
		local bDist = (bPos - capitalPos).Magnitude
		if aDist ~= bDist then return aDist > bDist end
		return a.id < b.id
	end)

	-- 5. Assign satu unit per border tile, sampai kandidat habis.
	local moved = 0
	local used = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(candidates) do
		if moved >= freeCount then break end
		if plan:isAvailable(unit) then
			-- [FIX-B1] satu BFS per unit lewat predikat accept,
			-- bukan satu BFS per border tile (mahal).
			local bestPath = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit,
				function(t) return freeBorder[t] == true and used[t] ~= true end,
				false)
			if bestPath ~= nil then
				local dest = bestPath[#bestPath]
				plan:assign(unit, bestPath, "spreadIdle", TIER_GATHER,
					"spread idle unit to border (capital guard already assigned by T0)",
					{ destination = dest })
				used[dest] = true
				moved += 1
			end
		end
	end

	if moved > 0 then
		Logger.info("Features.Military.BorderPrep", "spreadIdleToBorder", {
			moved = moved, freeBorderTiles = freeCount,
			borderNeighbours = (function()
				local names = {}
				for name in pairs(borderMap) do table.insert(names, name) end
				table.sort(names)
				return names
			end)(),
		})
	end

	return { moved = moved, freeBorderTiles = freeCount }
end

---[SPREAD-FIX] Wrapper publik: dipilih otomatis berdasarkan toggle GUI.
---  * gatherEnabled == true  -> mode lama: tarik semua idle ke ring capital radius 3
---  * gatherEnabled == false -> mode baru: sebar idle ke border, sisakan 3 di ring
---@param world table
---@param plan table
function BorderPrep.gatherIdle(world, plan)
	if next(world.enemies) ~= nil then return end
	if world.capitalTile == nil or world.tileOwner[world.capitalTile] ~= world.me then return end

	if BorderPrep.gatherEnabled then
		gatherToCapital(world, plan)
	else
		spreadIdleToBorder(world, plan)
	end
end

function BorderPrep.cancel(reason)
	BorderPrep.active = nil
	BorderPrep.pendingRequest = nil
	BorderPrep.lastPrepareResult = nil
	Logger.info("Features.Military.BorderPrep", "cancelled", { reason = reason })
end

function BorderPrep.request(targetName)
	BorderPrep.pendingRequest = { target = targetName }
end

function BorderPrep.summary()
	local active = BorderPrep.active
	if active == nil then return nil end
	local n = 0
	for _ in pairs(active.unitIds) do n += 1 end
	return {
		target = active.target,
		targetPower = active.targetPower,
		unitCount = n,
		startedTick = active.startedTick,
		strong = active.strong,
	}
end

function BorderPrep.reset(reason)
	-- [FIX-P1-5] active/pendingRequest tidak boleh bertahan lintas rebuild atau pergantian negara.
	BorderPrep.active = nil
	BorderPrep.pendingRequest = nil
	BorderPrep.lastPrepareResult = nil
	Logger.info("Features.Military.BorderPrep", "reset", { reason = reason })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Military.BorderPrep", BorderPrep)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/ConvoyManager"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/ConvoyManager

	=== [CONVOY UPGRADE] ===
	Setiap unit yang bergerak ke target penting (capital strike, pincer cut, chase)
	HARUS punya minimal MIN_ESCORT unit lain di land-neighbour dari staging tile-nya.
	Kalau tidak cukup, unit leader TIDAK BOLEH launch â€” dia nunggu di posisi sekarang.
]]

local ConvoyManager = {}

---@module Utility/Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility/Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Features.Movement.Pathing
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

-- Constants.
ConvoyManager.MIN_ESCORT = 2
ConvoyManager.MAX_ESCORT = 4
ConvoyManager.ESCORT_MIN_HEALTH = 55
ConvoyManager.ESCORT_RADIUS_HOPS = 3

function ConvoyManager.countNearby(world, plan, leadUnit, stagingTile)
	local nearby = {}
	local seen = {}

	local record = MapGraph.tiles[stagingTile]
	if record ~= nil then
		for _, nbr in ipairs(record.landNeighbours) do
			local u = world.unitsByTile[nbr]
			-- [FIX-D7] escort harus lolos ambang health & tidak mati/retreat/flagged.
			if u ~= nil and u.owner == world.me and u.id ~= leadUnit.id and not seen[u.id]
				and not u.flagged and (u.health or 0) >= ConvoyManager.ESCORT_MIN_HEALTH
				and u.state ~= "Dead" and u.state ~= "Retreating" then
				seen[u.id] = true
				table.insert(nearby, u)
			end
		end
	end

	for _, u in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if u.id ~= leadUnit.id and not seen[u.id]
			and (u.targetTile == stagingTile
				or (u.targetTile ~= nil and record ~= nil and table.find(record.landNeighbours, u.targetTile) ~= nil))
			and u.state ~= "Dead" and u.state ~= "Retreating"
			and not u.flagged
		then
			seen[u.id] = true
			table.insert(nearby, u)
		end
	end

	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		if not seen[order.unitId] and order.destination ~= stagingTile
			and record ~= nil and table.find(record.landNeighbours, order.destination) ~= nil
		then
			seen[order.unitId] = true
			local u = world.unitsById[order.unitId]
			if u ~= nil then table.insert(nearby, u) end
		end
	end

	return #nearby, nearby
end

function ConvoyManager.validate(world, plan, leadUnit, path)
	local staging = path[#path]
	local count, escorts = ConvoyManager.countNearby(world, plan, leadUnit, staging)

	local leadEta = 0
	for index = 2, #path do
		leadEta += Pathing.hopSeconds(world.paths, leadUnit, path[index - 1], path[index])
	end

	local required = ConvoyManager.MIN_ESCORT
	if leadEta > 15 then
		required = math.min(ConvoyManager.MAX_ESCORT, ConvoyManager.MIN_ESCORT + 1)
	end

	local ok = count >= required

	return ok, {
		nearby = count,
		required = required,
		leadEtaSeconds = leadEta,
		staging = staging,
		escortIds = (function()
			local ids = {}
			for _, u in ipairs(escorts) do table.insert(ids, u.id) end
			return ids
		end)(),
	}
end

function ConvoyManager.requiresEscort(purpose)
	return purpose == "capitalApproach"
		or purpose == "capitalStrike"
		or purpose == "capitalWalkIn"
		or purpose == "chase"
		or purpose == "pincerCut"
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.ConvoyManager", ConvoyManager)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/AttackPlanner"] = [=[
local AttackPlanner = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")
local Arrows = require("Game/Arrows")
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")
local Tactics = require("Features/Front/Tactics")
local ThreatHeat = require("Features/Front/ThreatHeat")
local ConvoyManager = require("Features/Front/ConvoyManager")
local Plan = require("Features/Front/Plan")

local TIER_RELIEF = "A1"
local TIER_CAPITAL = "A0"
-- [FIX-CAP-3] Tier baru: amankan koridor (antara capital sweep dan strike capital).
local TIER_CORRIDOR = "A0b"
local TIER_KILL = "A2"
local TIER_PINCER = "A5"
-- [HUNT] Tier A6: kejar musuh killable off-poros untuk cegah flank.
local TIER_HUNT = "A6"
local TIER_CUT = "A3"
local TIER_CAPTURE = "A4"
local TIER_REVIEW = "AR"
local PINCER_MIN_VALUE = 120
local PINCER_MIN_CUT = 2
local PINCER_MAX_CUT = 2
local BROAD_FRONT_NEIGHBOURS = 2
local NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES = { Retreating = true, Dead = true }
local FIRST_ROUTE_TILE = 2
local CAPITAL_SWEEP_RADIUS = 3
local CAPITAL_SWEEP_MIN_STACK = 2
local CAPITAL_SWEEP_HORIZON_SECONDS = 15

local AXIS_OFFTILE_HOP = 6
local AXIS_COORIDOR_T_MIN = -0.2
local AXIS_COORIDOR_T_MAX = 1.3
local OFF_AXIS_STRENGTH_RATIO = 3.0
local OFF_AXIS_CUT_UNITS_MIN = 3

-- [FIX-CAP-1] Bobot prioritas musuh. Tujuan perang di game ini adalah CAPITAL, jadi jarak
-- ke capital musuh harus DOMINAN dan kekuatan hanya penentu kedua.
local CAPITAL_REACH_WEIGHT = 1.0   -- per 1000 studs jarak capital kita -> capital musuh
local ENEMY_UNIT_WEIGHT = 0.05     -- 20 unit musuh setara 1000 studs
local ENEMY_TILE_WEIGHT = 0.01     -- 100 tile musuh setara 1000 studs
-- [FIX-CAP-2] Pincer di luar poros hanya diterima bila nilainya jauh lebih besar.
local OFF_AXIS_PINCER_VALUE_MULT = 3.0
-- [FIX-CAP-3] Batas tile koridor yang diperiksa per siklus (biaya reach() per tile).
local CORRIDOR_MAX_TILES = 12

local function axisDistanceTo(world, tileName)
	if world.capitalTile == nil then return math.huge, nil end
	local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]
	if record == nil then return math.huge, nil end

	local ourPos = MapGraph.tiles[world.capitalTile].position
	local tilePos = record.position
	local best = math.huge
	local bestCap = nil

	for enemyName in pairs(world.enemies) do
		local ec = world.enemyCapitals[enemyName]
		if ec ~= nil and world.tileOwner[ec] == enemyName then
			local ecPos = MapGraph.tiles[ec].position
			local line = ecPos - ourPos
			local toTile = tilePos - ourPos
			local denom = line:Dot(line)
			if denom > 0.0001 then
				local t = toTile:Dot(line) / denom
				if t >= AXIS_COORIDOR_T_MIN and t <= AXIS_COORIDOR_T_MAX then
					local clampedT = math.clamp(t, 0, 1)
					local projected = ourPos + line * clampedT
					local d = (tilePos - projected).Magnitude
					if d < best then
						best = d
						bestCap = ec
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return best, bestCap
end

local function isOnAxis(world, tileName)
	local d, _ = axisDistanceTo(world, tileName)
	if d == math.huge then return true, d end
	local avgEdge = 15
	return d <= AXIS_OFFTILE_HOP * avgEdge, d
end

AttackPlanner.reportedLosing = {}

function AttackPlanner.enemyPriority(world)
	-- [FIX-C1] dipakai 3x per siklus (capitals/kills/pincers): histogram sekali, lalu memo di world.
	if world._enemyPriority ~= nil then return world._enemyPriority end
	local priorities = {}

	for enemyName in pairs(world.enemies) do
		local units = world.unitsOf[enemyName] or {}
		local unitCount = #units

		-- [FIX-B3b] pakai penghitung inkremental Ownership, bukan pass penuh atas tileOwner.
		local tileCount = Ownership.countryTileCounts[enemyName] or 0

		local capitalDistance = math.huge
		local enemyCapital = world.enemyCapitals[enemyName]
		if enemyCapital ~= nil and world.capitalTile ~= nil then
			local enemyPos = MapGraph.tiles[enemyCapital].position
			local ourPos = MapGraph.tiles[world.capitalTile].position
			capitalDistance = (enemyPos - ourPos).Magnitude
		end

		-- [FIX-CAP-1] Rumus lama: unitCount * 1.0 + tileCount * 0.15 + capitalDistance * 0.0005
		-- Bobot jarak praktis NOL (4000 studs hanya +2), sedangkan skor diurutkan naik ->
		-- musuh TERLEMAH selalu dipilih lebih dulu, walau capitalnya di seberang peta.
		-- Akibatnya unit berbaris jauh menembus tile yang tidak strategis.
		-- Sekarang: capital terdekat/terjangkau dulu, kekuatan sebagai penentu kedua.
		local reach = capitalDistance / 1000
		local score = reach * CAPITAL_REACH_WEIGHT
			+ unitCount * ENEMY_UNIT_WEIGHT
			+ tileCount * ENEMY_TILE_WEIGHT

		priorities[enemyName] = {
			score = score,
			unitCount = unitCount,
			tileCount = tileCount,
			capitalDistance = capitalDistance,
		}
	end

	world._enemyPriority = priorities
	return priorities
end

local MIN_ATTACK_HEALTH = 60
local CRITICAL_HP_FLOOR = 35

local function strikers(world, plan, tileName, targetOwner, mode)
	local units = {}
	local minHealth = (mode == "critical") and CRITICAL_HP_FLOOR or MIN_ATTACK_HEALTH
	for _, unit in ipairs(Tactics.availableNeighbours(world, plan, tileName)) do
		if not unit.flagged
			and unit.state ~= "Attacking"
			and (unit.health or 0) >= minHealth
			and Tactics.mayAttackFromHot(world, unit.tile, targetOwner) then
			table.insert(units, unit)
		end
	end
	return units
end

local function launch(plan, stack, targetTile, purpose, tier, reason)
	-- [FIX-C2] satu-satunya plan:assign tanpa guard di pipeline front; cegah entri ganda.
	local launched = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(stack.attackers) do
		if not launched[entry.unit.id] and plan:isAvailable(entry.unit) then
			launched[entry.unit.id] = true
			plan:assign(entry.unit, { entry.unit.tile, targetTile }, purpose, tier, reason, {
				ratio = stack.result.ratio,
				breakSeconds = stack.result.breakSeconds,
				stackSize = #stack.all,
			})
		end
	end
	for _, entry in ipairs(stack.all) do
		if entry.existing and plan:isAvailable(entry.unit) then
			plan:hold(entry.unit, purpose, tier, "already attacking in a winning stack")
		end
	end
end

local function surround(world, plan, defender, stack, purpose, tier)
	local exits = FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, defender)
	local breakSeconds = stack.result.breakSeconds
	local used = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(stack.all) do
		used[entry.unit.id] = true
	end
	if #exits == 0 then return true, { exits = 0 } end
	if breakSeconds == nil then return false, { reason = "no break time" } end
	local ourMovement = world.paths.movement[world.me] or Ownership.movementCountries(world.me)
	world.paths.movement[world.me] = ourMovement
	local chosen = {}
	for _, exitName in ipairs(exits) do
		local owner = world.tileOwner[exitName]
		if owner == nil or not ourMovement[owner] or not plan:isFreeDestination(exitName) then
			return false, { reason = "exit not enterable", exit = exitName, owner = owner }
		end
		local best = nil
		for _, unit in ipairs(Tactics.availableNeighbours(world, plan, exitName)) do
			if not used[unit.id] and not unit.flagged then
				local hop = Pathing.hopSeconds(world.paths, unit, unit.tile, exitName)
				local placed = CombatModel.placedOn(unit, exitName)
				local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, exitName, placed)
				if hop < breakSeconds and not threat.breaks and (best == nil or hop < best.hop) then
					best = { unit = unit, hop = hop, threatRatio = threat.ratio }
				end
			end
		end
		if best == nil then
			return false, { reason = "no unit reaches the exit before the break", exit = exitName, breakSeconds = breakSeconds }
		end
		used[best.unit.id] = true
		table.insert(chosen, { exit = exitName, choice = best })
	end
	for _, item in ipairs(chosen) do
		plan:assign(item.choice.unit, { item.choice.unit.tile, item.exit }, purpose, tier, "occupy the defender's exit so it dies when beaten", {
			defenderTile = defender.tile,
			hopSeconds = item.choice.hop,
			breakSeconds = breakSeconds,
			threatRatio = item.choice.threatRatio,
		})
	end
	return true, { exits = #exits }
end

function AttackPlanner.relieve(world, plan, entry)
	local defender = entry.unit
	local ordered = table.clone(entry.threat.attackers)
	local damageByUnit = {}
	for index, attackerEntry in ipairs(entry.threat.attackers) do
		damageByUnit[attackerEntry.unit.id] = entry.threat.damages and entry.threat.damages[index] or 0
	end
	table.sort(ordered, function(first, second)
		local firstAttacking = first.unit.state == "Attacking" and first.unit.targetTile == entry.tile
		local secondAttacking = second.unit.state == "Attacking" and second.unit.targetTile == entry.tile
		if firstAttacking ~= secondAttacking then return firstAttacking end
		if damageByUnit[first.unit.id] ~= damageByUnit[second.unit.id] then
			return damageByUnit[first.unit.id] > damageByUnit[second.unit.id]
		end
		return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
	end)
	local relieved = {}
	local remainingThreat = nil
	local facts = { attackers = #ordered, relieved = 0 }
	for _, attackerEntry in ipairs(ordered) do
		local enemy = attackerEntry.unit
		local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, enemy.tile, strikers(world, plan, enemy.tile, enemy.owner))
		if stack ~= nil then
			launch(plan, stack, enemy.tile, "relief", TIER_RELIEF, "beat a unit threatening " .. entry.tile)
			relieved[enemy.id] = true
			facts.relieved += 1
			local remaining = {}
			for _, other in ipairs(entry.threat.attackers) do
				if not relieved[other.unit.id] then table.insert(remaining, other) end
			end
			if #remaining == 0 then
				remainingThreat = { ratio = 0, breaks = false }
				break
			end
			remainingThreat = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, remaining, defender, entry.tile, "defender")
			if not remainingThreat.breaks then break end
		end
	end
	facts.remainingRatio = remainingThreat and remainingThreat.ratio or entry.threat.ratio
	return { remainingThreat = remainingThreat, facts = facts }
end

function AttackPlanner.capitals(world, plan)
	local enemyNames = {}
	for enemyName in pairs(world.enemies) do table.insert(enemyNames, enemyName) end
	local priorities = AttackPlanner.enemyPriority(world)

	table.sort(enemyNames, function(first, second)
		-- [FIX-CAP-4] Dulu musuh ber-AI selalu didahulukan atas pemain manusia. Sekarang
		-- tidak: yang menentukan adalah seberapa TERJANGKAU capitalnya, siapa pun pemiliknya.
		local firstPrio = priorities[first] and priorities[first].score or math.huge
		local secondPrio = priorities[second] and priorities[second].score or math.huge

		if firstPrio ~= secondPrio then
			return firstPrio < secondPrio
		end

		return first < second
	end)
	for _, enemyName in ipairs(enemyNames) do
		local capitalTile = world.enemyCapitals[enemyName]
		if capitalTile ~= nil and world.tileOwner[capitalTile] == enemyName then
			local occupant = world.unitsByTile[capitalTile]
			if occupant == nil then
				local incumbent = nil
				for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
					if unit.targetTile == capitalTile then incumbent = unit end
				end
				if incumbent ~= nil then
					if plan:isAvailable(incumbent) then
						plan:hold(incumbent, "capitalWalkIn", TIER_CAPITAL, "already entering the empty enemy capital")
					end
				else
					local candidates = {}
					for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
						if not unit.flagged then table.insert(candidates, unit) end
					end
					local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, capitalTile, nil)
					plan:decide(TIER_CAPITAL, "walkIn:" .. enemyName, ranked, "earliest arrival at the empty enemy capital")
					if best ~= nil then
						plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, "capitalWalkIn", TIER_CAPITAL, enemyName .. "'s capital is empty: entering it annexes the country", { seconds = best.seconds, enemy = enemyName })
					end
				end
			elseif world.enemies[occupant.owner] and not NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES[occupant.state] then
				local stack, stackFacts = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, capitalTile, strikers(world, plan, capitalTile, occupant.owner, "critical"), "critical")
				if stack ~= nil then
					local ourFullHP = 0
					for _, entry in ipairs(stack.all) do
						ourFullHP += (entry.unit.health or 100)
					end
					local enemyHP = occupant.health or 100
					local adj = FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, capitalTile)
					-- [FIX-D1] jumlahkan SEMUA musuh yang berdekatan, bukan hanya yang pertama.
					for _, adjUnit in ipairs(adj) do enemyHP += (adjUnit.health or 100) end
					if ourFullHP < enemyHP then
						-- [BUGFIX-C10] field name was "ourHP" with undefined variable;
						-- the value we want to log is ourFullHP.
						Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "capitalParityTooLow", {
							tile = capitalTile, ourHP = ourFullHP, enemyHP = enemyHP,
						})
					else
						local dies = FrontAnalyzer.diesWhenBeaten(world, plan, occupant)
						local surroundFacts = nil
						if not dies then
							local _, facts = surround(world, plan, occupant, stack, "capitalSurround", TIER_CAPITAL)
							surroundFacts = facts
						end
						launch(plan, stack, capitalTile, "capitalStrike", TIER_CAPITAL, "beat the defender of " .. enemyName .. "'s capital")
						local logStrike = Logger.info
						if #stack.attackers == 0 then logStrike = Logger.debug end
						logStrike("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "capitalStrike", {
							enemy = enemyName, tile = capitalTile, defender = occupant.troopName,
							ratio = stack.result.ratio, breakSeconds = stack.result.breakSeconds,
							defenderDiesWhenBeaten = dies, surround = surroundFacts,
						})
					end
				else
					AttackPlanner.approachCapital(world, plan, enemyName, capitalTile, occupant, stackFacts)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

---[FIX-CAP-3] Amankan koridor menuju capital.
---"Scan kanan-kiri dulu": habisi musuh yang, bila dibiarkan, akan MEMUTUS supply kita di
---poros sehingga unit di depan kehilangan supply dan produksi tersendat. Ini mencegah
---flank yang memotong jalan.
---Dijalankan SETELAH A0 (strike capital tetap mendapat unit lebih dulu) tetapi SEBELUM
---A2/A5/A3 supaya unit sisa dipakai membersihkan flank, bukan capture yang jauh.
function AttackPlanner.corridorSecurity(world, plan)
	local candidates = {}

	-- 1. Musuh yang bersebelahan dengan tile KITA di poros. Iterasi dibatasi pada
	--    world.enemyUnits (bukan seluruh tileOwner) supaya tidak menambah scan penuh.
	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if not NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES[enemy.state] and not enemy.flagged then
			local enemyRecord = MapGraph.tiles[enemy.tile]
			if enemyRecord ~= nil then
				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(enemyRecord.landNeighbours) do
					if world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me then
						local onAxis, axisDist = isOnAxis(world, neighbourName)
						if onAxis then
							table.insert(candidates, {
								enemy = enemy, guard = neighbourName, axisDist = axisDist,
							})
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if #candidates == 0 then return end

	table.sort(candidates, function(first, second)
		if first.axisDist ~= second.axisDist then return first.axisDist < second.axisDist end
		if first.guard ~= second.guard then return first.guard < second.guard end
		return first.enemy.id < second.enemy.id
	end)

	-- 2. Apakah kehilangan tile itu benar-benar memutus supply kita?
	--    unitsCutByLosing sudah di-memoize per (negara, tile); jumlah pemeriksaan dibatasi.
	local threats = {}
	local checked = 0
	for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
		if checked >= CORRIDOR_MAX_TILES then break end
		checked += 1

		local cut = SupplyModel.unitsCutByLosing(world, world.me, candidate.guard)
		if #cut > 0 then
			local existing = threats[candidate.enemy.id]
			if existing == nil or #cut > existing.cutUnits then
				threats[candidate.enemy.id] = {
					enemy = candidate.enemy, guard = candidate.guard, cutUnits = #cut,
				}
			end
		end
	end

	if next(threats) == nil then return end

	local ordered = {}
	for _, threat in pairs(threats) do table.insert(ordered, threat) end
	table.sort(ordered, function(first, second)
		if first.cutUnits ~= second.cutUnits then return first.cutUnits > second.cutUnits end
		if first.enemy.health ~= second.enemy.health then return first.enemy.health < second.enemy.health end
		return first.enemy.id < second.enemy.id
	end)

	-- 3. Serang, paling mengancam dulu.
	local struck = 0
	for _, threat in ipairs(ordered) do
		local enemy = threat.enemy
		local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, enemy.tile,
			strikers(world, plan, enemy.tile, enemy.owner))
		if stack ~= nil and #stack.attackers > 0 then
			launch(plan, stack, enemy.tile, "corridorClear", TIER_CORRIDOR,
				string.format("kalau %s jatuh, %d unit kita kehilangan supply",
					threat.guard, threat.cutUnits))
			struck += 1
			Logger.info("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "corridorClear", {
				tile = enemy.tile, guard = threat.guard, cutUnits = threat.cutUnits,
				enemy = enemy.owner, ratio = stack.result.ratio,
			})
		end
	end

	if struck > 0 then
		plan.corridorStruck = struck
	end
end

function AttackPlanner.capitalSweep(world, plan)
	local pathTiles = {}
	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		if order.purpose == "capitalApproach" or order.purpose == "capitalStrike" then
			for _, t in ipairs(order.path) do pathTiles[t] = true end
		end
	end

	if next(pathTiles) == nil then
		for enemyName in pairs(world.enemies) do
			local ec = world.enemyCapitals[enemyName]
			if ec ~= nil then pathTiles[ec] = true end
		end
	end

	if next(pathTiles) == nil then return 0 end

	local sweepTargets = {}
	for tile in pairs(pathTiles) do
		-- [FIX-H2] seen harus per-root; kalau dipakai bersama, hanya root pertama yang tersapu.
		local seen = {}
		local queue = { { tile, 0 } }
		local head = 1
		while head <= #queue do
			local cur = queue[head][1]
			local d = queue[head][2]
			head += 1
			if d <= CAPITAL_SWEEP_RADIUS and not seen[cur] then
				seen[cur] = true
				local occupant = world.unitsByTile[cur]
				if occupant ~= nil and world.enemies[occupant.owner]
					and not NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES[occupant.state]
				then
					local nearby = FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, cur)
					if #nearby + 1 >= CAPITAL_SWEEP_MIN_STACK then
						table.insert(sweepTargets, {
							tile = cur,
							unit = occupant,
							distanceToPath = d,
							threat = #nearby + 1,
						})
					end
				end
				for _, nbr in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[cur].landNeighbours) do
					table.insert(queue, { nbr, d + 1 })
				end
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(sweepTargets, function(a, b)
		if a.distanceToPath ~= b.distanceToPath then return a.distanceToPath < b.distanceToPath end
		if a.threat ~= b.threat then return a.threat > b.threat end
		return a.unit.id < b.unit.id
	end)

	local cleared = 0
	for _, target in ipairs(sweepTargets) do
		if not target.unit.flagged then
			local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, target.tile,
				strikers(world, plan, target.tile, target.unit.owner))
			if stack ~= nil and #stack.attackers > 0 then
				launch(plan, stack, target.tile, "pathClear", "A0a",
					string.format("clear threat %d hop dari jalur capital (%d musuh)", target.distanceToPath, target.threat))
				cleared += 1
			end
		end
	end

	if cleared > 0 then
		Logger.info("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "capitalSweep", {
			cleared = cleared, candidates = #sweepTargets,
		})
	end
	return cleared
end

function AttackPlanner.approachCapital(world, plan, enemyName, capitalTile, defender, adjacentFacts)
	local entries = Tactics.existingAttackers(world, capitalTile)
	local inStack = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do inStack[entry.unit.id] = true end
	for _, unit in ipairs(strikers(world, plan, capitalTile, defender.owner)) do
		-- [FIX-H4] unit yang sudah ada di entries tidak boleh dimasukkan dua kali.
		if not inStack[unit.id] then
			table.insert(entries, { unit = unit, fromTile = unit.tile, adjacent = true })
			inStack[unit.id] = true
		end
	end
	for unitId, commitment in pairs(plan.commitments) do
		local unit = world.unitsById[unitId]
		if unit ~= nil and not inStack[unitId] and commitment.purpose == "capitalApproach" and commitment.facts ~= nil and commitment.facts.capitalTile == capitalTile then
			table.insert(entries, { unit = CombatModel.placedOn(unit, commitment.destination), fromTile = commitment.destination, committed = true })
			inStack[unitId] = true
		end
	end
	if world.cycleKind ~= "tick" then
		local approachRunning = false
		for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
			if entry.committed then approachRunning = true end
		end
		if approachRunning then
			for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
				if entry.adjacent and plan:isAvailable(entry.unit) then
					plan:hold(entry.unit, "capitalStack", TIER_CAPITAL, "waits for the staged units to strike together")
				end
			end
		end
		return
	end
	local result = nil
	if #entries > 0 then
		result = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, entries, defender, capitalTile, "attacker")
	end
	local ourMovement = world.paths.movement[world.me] or Ownership.movementCountries(world.me)
	world.paths.movement[world.me] = ourMovement
	local staging = {}
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[capitalTile].landNeighbours) do
		local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]
		if owner ~= nil and ourMovement[owner] and plan:isFreeDestination(neighbourName) and world.unitsByTile[neighbourName] == nil then
			table.insert(staging, neighbourName)
		end
	end
	local planned = {}
	if (result == nil or not result.breaks or result.tie) and #staging > 0 then
		local field = Pathing.distanceField(staging, function(tileName)
			local owner = world.tileOwner[tileName]
			return owner ~= nil and ourMovement[owner] and world.unitsByTile[tileName] == nil and plan.locks[tileName] == nil and plan.claims[tileName] == nil
		end)
		local routes = {}
		for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
			if not unit.flagged and not inStack[unit.id] then
				local firstStep = nil
				local firstDistance = math.huge
				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
					local neighbourDistance = field.distance[neighbourName]
					if neighbourDistance ~= nil then
						local total = neighbourDistance + MapGraph.edgeLength(unit.tile, neighbourName)
						if total < firstDistance then firstDistance = total; firstStep = neighbourName end
					end
				end
				if firstStep ~= nil then
					local path = { unit.tile, firstStep }
					local step = firstStep
					while field.towardSource[step] ~= nil do
						step = field.towardSource[step]
						table.insert(path, step)
					end
					table.insert(routes, { unit = unit, path = path, studs = firstDistance })
				end
			end
		end
		table.sort(routes, function(first, second)
			local firstHeat = ThreatHeat.at(first.path[#first.path])
			local secondHeat = ThreatHeat.at(second.path[#second.path])
			local firstScore = first.studs - firstHeat * 0.1
			local secondScore = second.studs - secondHeat * 0.1
			if firstScore ~= secondScore then
				return firstScore < secondScore
			end
			return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
		end)
		local function routeIsSafe(route)
			for index = FIRST_ROUTE_TILE, #route.path do
				local tileName = route.path[index]
				if world.tileOwner[tileName] == world.me and not SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, tileName) then
					return false, "unsupplied tile " .. tileName
				end
				local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, tileName, CombatModel.placedOn(route.unit, tileName))
				if threat.breaks then return false, "static threat at " .. tileName end
			end
			local interdiction = FrontAnalyzer.pathInterdiction(world, plan, route.unit, route.path, CAPITAL_SWEEP_HORIZON_SECONDS)
			for _, cut in ipairs(interdiction) do
				if cut.kind == "incoming" and cut.atSeconds <= 1.5 then
					return false, "incoming interdiction at " .. cut.tile .. " by unit " .. tostring(cut.byUnit)
				end
			end
			return true, nil
		end
		local taken = {}
		for _, route in ipairs(routes) do
			local stagingTile = route.path[#route.path]
			local routeSafe, routeWhy = routeIsSafe(route)
			if not taken[stagingTile] and #route.path <= Pathing.MAX_PATH_TILES and routeSafe then
				local convoyOk, convoyFacts = ConvoyManager.validate(world, plan, route.unit, route.path)
				if not convoyOk then
					Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "capitalConvoyTooSmall", {
						unit = route.unit.id, path = route.path, convoy = convoyFacts,
					})
				else
					taken[stagingTile] = true
					table.insert(planned, { unit = route.unit, path = route.path, staging = stagingTile, studs = route.studs })
					table.insert(entries, { unit = CombatModel.placedOn(route.unit, stagingTile), fromTile = stagingTile })
					result = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, entries, defender, capitalTile, "attacker")
					if result.breaks and not result.tie then break end
				end
			elseif not routeSafe then
				Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "capitalRouteUnsafe", {
					unit = route.unit.id, path = route.path, why = routeWhy,
				})
			end
		end
	end
	if result ~= nil and result.breaks and not result.tie then
		for _, item in ipairs(planned) do
			plan:assign(item.unit, item.path, "capitalApproach", TIER_CAPITAL, "stage next to " .. enemyName .. "'s capital", {
				capitalTile = capitalTile, staging = item.staging, studs = item.studs, stagedRatio = result.ratio,
			})
		end
		for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
			if entry.adjacent and plan:isAvailable(entry.unit) then
				plan:hold(entry.unit, "capitalStack", TIER_CAPITAL, "waits for the staged units to strike together")
			end
		end
	else
		Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "capitalOutOfReach", {
			enemy = enemyName, tile = capitalTile, defender = defender.troopName,
			adjacentFacts = adjacentFacts, stagingTiles = #staging, stackSize = #entries,
			bestStagedRatio = result and result.ratio or 0,
		})
	end
end

function AttackPlanner.kills(world, plan)
	local targets = {}
	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if not NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES[enemy.state] then
			local dies, why = FrontAnalyzer.diesWhenBeaten(world, plan, enemy)
			local cut = enemy.noSupply and not SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, enemy.tile)
			if dies or cut then table.insert(targets, { enemy = enemy, dies = dies, why = why, cut = cut }) end
		end
	end
	local priorities = AttackPlanner.enemyPriority(world)

	for _, target in ipairs(targets) do
		local onAxis, axisDist = isOnAxis(world, target.enemy.tile)
		target.onAxis = onAxis
		target.axisDist = axisDist
	end

	table.sort(targets, function(first, second)
		if first.onAxis ~= second.onAxis then return first.onAxis end
		if first.onAxis and second.onAxis and first.axisDist ~= second.axisDist then
			return first.axisDist < second.axisDist
		end

		local firstPrio = priorities[first.enemy.owner] and priorities[first.enemy.owner].score or math.huge
		local secondPrio = priorities[second.enemy.owner] and priorities[second.enemy.owner].score or math.huge

		if firstPrio ~= secondPrio then
			return firstPrio < secondPrio
		end

		if first.dies ~= second.dies then
			return first.dies
		end

		if first.enemy.health ~= second.enemy.health then
			return first.enemy.health < second.enemy.health
		end

		return first.enemy.id < second.enemy.id
	end)

	local targeted = {}
	for _, target in ipairs(targets) do
		local enemy = target.enemy
		local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, enemy.tile, strikers(world, plan, enemy.tile, enemy.owner))
		if stack ~= nil and #stack.attackers > 0 then
			local purpose = "kill"
			if not target.dies then purpose = "cutOffAttack" end
			launch(plan, stack, enemy.tile, purpose, TIER_KILL, target.why)
			targeted[enemy.id] = true
		end
	end
	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if not targeted[enemy.id] and not NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES[enemy.state] and not enemy.flagged then
			local strikeUnits = strikers(world, plan, enemy.tile, enemy.owner)
			if #strikeUnits > 0 then
				local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, enemy.tile, strikeUnits)
				if stack ~= nil and #stack.attackers > 0 then
					local exits = FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, enemy)
					if #exits > 0 then
						local covered, facts = surround(world, plan, enemy, stack, "surround", TIER_KILL)
						if covered then
							launch(plan, stack, enemy.tile, "surroundKill", TIER_KILL, "exits occupied: the beaten defender dies")
							targeted[enemy.id] = true
							Logger.info("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "surroundKill", {
								tile = enemy.tile, troop = enemy.troopName, ratio = stack.result.ratio,
								breakSeconds = stack.result.breakSeconds, exits = facts.exits,
							})
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local ourMovement = world.paths.movement[world.me] or Ownership.movementCountries(world.me)
	world.paths.movement[world.me] = ourMovement
	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if enemy.state == "Retreating" and enemy.targetTile ~= nil then
			local landing = enemy.targetTile
			local owner = world.tileOwner[landing]
			if owner ~= nil and ourMovement[owner] and world.unitsByTile[landing] == nil and plan:isFreeDestination(landing) then
				local endTime = Arrows.endTime("RetreatArrow", enemy.tile, landing)
				if endTime ~= nil then
					local remaining = endTime - world.serverTime
					local candidates = {}
					for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
						if not unit.flagged then table.insert(candidates, unit) end
					end
					local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, landing, remaining)
					if best ~= nil and best.seconds < remaining then
						plan:decide(TIER_KILL, "chase:" .. landing, ranked, "arrives before the retreating unit lands")
						plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, "chase", TIER_KILL, "entering a retreat destination first kills the retreating unit", {
							seconds = best.seconds, retreatRemainingSeconds = remaining, troop = enemy.troopName,
						})
						Logger.info("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "retreatTrapped", {
							enemyTroop = enemy.troopName, landing = landing,
							ourEta = best.seconds, retreatRemaining = remaining,
						})
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

function AttackPlanner.tryPincer(world, plan, enemyName, pocket)
	for _, cutTile in ipairs(pocket.cutSet) do
		local occ = world.unitsByTile[cutTile]
		if occ ~= nil and occ.owner == world.me then
			-- Sudah di-cut, skip tile ini.
		elseif occ ~= nil and world.enemies[occ.owner] then
			return false   -- musuh masih di sana
		elseif not plan:isFreeDestination(cutTile) then
			return false
		end
	end
	local assignments = {}
	local usedUnits = {}
	for _, cutTile in ipairs(pocket.cutSet) do
		local best = nil
		for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
			if not usedUnits[unit.id] and not unit.flagged and unit.state ~= "Attacking" then
				local adjacent = false
				for _, nbr in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[cutTile].landNeighbours) do
					if unit.tile == nbr then adjacent = true; break end
				end
				if adjacent then
					best = { unit = unit, path = { unit.tile, cutTile }, seconds = 0 }
					break
				end
			end
		end
		if best == nil then
			local candidates = {}
			for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
				if not usedUnits[unit.id] and not unit.flagged and unit.state ~= "Attacking" then
					table.insert(candidates, unit)
				end
			end
			local arrival = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, cutTile, nil)
			if arrival == nil then return false end
			best = arrival
		end
		usedUnits[best.unit.id] = true
		table.insert(assignments, { unit = best.unit, path = best.path, cutTile = cutTile, seconds = best.seconds })
	end
	for _, a in ipairs(assignments) do
		plan:assign(a.unit, a.path, "pincerCut", TIER_PINCER,
			string.format("pincer on %s isolates %d enemy units", a.cutTile, #pocket.units),
			{ cutSet = pocket.cutSet, pocketValue = pocket.value, unitsCut = #pocket.units, arrivalSeconds = a.seconds })
	end
	Logger.info("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "pincerLaunched", {
		enemy = enemyName, cutSet = pocket.cutSet, unitsCut = #pocket.units, value = pocket.value, arrivals = assignments,
	})
	return true
end

function AttackPlanner.pincers(world, plan)
	if world.cycleKind ~= "tick" then
		return
	end

	local priorities = AttackPlanner.enemyPriority(world)
	local enemyNames = {}
	for enemyName in pairs(world.enemies) do
		table.insert(enemyNames, enemyName)
	end
	table.sort(enemyNames, function(a, b)
		local aPrio = priorities[a] and priorities[a].score or math.huge
		local bPrio = priorities[b] and priorities[b].score or math.huge
		if aPrio ~= bPrio then
			return aPrio < bPrio
		end
		return a < b
	end)

	for _, enemyName in ipairs(enemyNames) do
		local pockets = FrontAnalyzer.supplyPockets(world, enemyName, PINCER_MAX_CUT)
		for _, pocket in ipairs(pockets) do
			-- [FIX-CAP-2] Pincer dulu SAMA SEKALI tidak sadar poros, jadi bisa mengirim unit
			-- keluar jalur menuju capital. Sekarang pocket harus menyentuh poros, kecuali
			-- nilainya jauh lebih besar (mengurung banyak unit musuh).
			local pocketOnAxis = false
			for _, cutTile in ipairs(pocket.cutSet) do
				if isOnAxis(world, cutTile) then pocketOnAxis = true; break end
			end

			if (pocketOnAxis or pocket.value >= PINCER_MIN_VALUE * OFF_AXIS_PINCER_VALUE_MULT)
				and pocket.value >= PINCER_MIN_VALUE
				and (#pocket.cutSet >= PINCER_MIN_CUT or #pocket.units >= 3) then
				if not AttackPlanner.tryPincer(world, plan, enemyName, pocket) then
					Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "pincerSkipped", {
						enemy = enemyName, cutSet = pocket.cutSet, value = pocket.value,
						reason = "cut tiles not all reachable this cycle",
					})
					plan.pendingPincer = true
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if plan.pendingPincer ~= true then
		plan.pendingPincer = false
	end
end

-- [HUNT] ====================================================================
-- Tier A6: kejar musuh KILLABLE yang belum ditangani tier atas.
-- Tujuan: musuh off-poros yang bebas berkeliaran (flanker potensial) dihabisi
-- sebelum mereka memotong supply kita atau bergabung ke stack besar.
--
-- Filter berlapis (semua harus lolos):
--   1. Musuh belum ditangani tier A0-A5 (tidak ada di plan.orders)
--   2. Bukan Retreating/Dead/flagged
--   3. < 3 musuh adjacent (hindari trap stack)
--   4. Ada unit kita dalam radius maxHops
--   5. Tactics.winningStack menghasilkan margin >= minRatio
--   6. Health setelah menang >= minHealthAfter
--   7. Tactics.safeAfterFight: tidak akan mati oleh counter
--
-- Cap MAX_HUNTS_PER_CYCLE untuk menjaga komputasi. Sisanya besok lagi.
-- ============================================================================
local MAX_HUNTS_PER_CYCLE = 3
local HUNT_AVG_EDGE_STUDS = 15

function AttackPlanner.hunt(world, plan, opts)
	opts = opts or {}
	local minRatio = opts.minRatio or 1.5
	local minHealthAfter = opts.minHealthAfter or 40
	local maxHops = opts.maxHops or 6
	local maxStuds = maxHops * HUNT_AVG_EDGE_STUDS

	-- 1. Target yang sudah ditangani tier atas.
	local alreadyTargeted = {}
	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		if order.isAttack then
			alreadyTargeted[order.destination] = true
		end
	end

	-- 2. Kandidat unit kita: belum diassign, cukup sehat, tidak cut supply.
	local ourCandidates = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
		if not unit.flagged
			and unit.state ~= "Attacking"
			and (unit.health or 0) >= MIN_ATTACK_HEALTH
			and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, unit.tile)
		then
			local record = MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile]
			if record ~= nil then
				table.insert(ourCandidates, { unit = unit, position = record.position })
			end
		end
	end
	if #ourCandidates == 0 then
		return { hunted = 0, reason = "no candidate units" }
	end

	-- 3. Kumpulkan musuh yang layak dihunt.
	local targets = {}
	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if not alreadyTargeted[enemy.tile]
			and enemy.state ~= "Retreating"
			and enemy.state ~= "Dead"
			and not enemy.flagged
		then
			local adjacent = FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, enemy.tile)
			if #adjacent < 3 then
				table.insert(targets, enemy)
			end
		end
	end
	if #targets == 0 then
		return { hunted = 0, reason = "no eligible targets" }
	end

	-- 4. Sort: musuh dengan health terendah dulu (kill termurah dulu).
	table.sort(targets, function(a, b)
		if (a.health or 100) ~= (b.health or 100) then
			return (a.health or 100) < (b.health or 100)
		end
		return a.id < b.id
	end)

	-- 5. Untuk setiap target, cari unit kita terdekat dalam radius dan cek stack.
	local hunted = 0
	for _, enemy in ipairs(targets) do
		if hunted >= MAX_HUNTS_PER_CYCLE then break end

		local enemyPos = MapGraph.tiles[enemy.tile].position

		local closeCandidates = {}
		for _, entry in ipairs(ourCandidates) do
			if plan:isAvailable(entry.unit) then
				local dist = (entry.position - enemyPos).Magnitude
				if dist <= maxStuds then
					table.insert(closeCandidates, { unit = entry.unit, distance = dist })
				end
			end
		end

		if #closeCandidates > 0 then
			table.sort(closeCandidates, function(a, b)
				if a.distance ~= b.distance then return a.distance < b.distance end
				return a.unit.id < b.unit.id
			end)

			-- Cap kandidat untuk winningStack (limit komputasi).
			local candidateUnits = {}
			local cap = math.min(#closeCandidates, MAX_STACK_PREFILTER)
			for i = 1, cap do
				table.insert(candidateUnits, closeCandidates[i].unit)
			end

			local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, enemy.tile, candidateUnits)
			if stack ~= nil
				and #stack.attackers > 0
				and stack.result.ratio >= minRatio
			then
				-- 6. Cek health setelah menang.
				local minHealth = 100
				local hpList = stack.result.healthsAtBreak or stack.result.finalHealths or {}
				for _, h in ipairs(hpList) do
					if type(h) == "number" and h < minHealth then
						minHealth = h
					end
				end

				if minHealth >= minHealthAfter then
					-- 7. Cek apakah aman setelah fight (tidak akan mati oleh counter).
					local allSafe = true
					for idx, entry in ipairs(stack.all) do
						local hpAtBreak = stack.result.healthsAtBreak and stack.result.healthsAtBreak[idx]
						local hp = hpAtBreak or entry.unit.health or 100
						local safe = Tactics.safeAfterFight(world, entry, hp, enemy.tile)
						if not safe then
							allSafe = false
							break
						end
					end

					if allSafe then
						launch(plan, stack, enemy.tile, "hunt", TIER_HUNT,
							string.format("hunt %s at %s (ratio %.2f, hp-after %.0f)",
								enemy.troopName or "enemy", enemy.tile, stack.result.ratio, minHealth))
						hunted += 1
						Logger.info("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "huntLaunch", {
							target = enemy.tile, troop = enemy.troopName,
							ratio = stack.result.ratio, attackers = #stack.attackers,
							minHealthAfter = minHealth,
						})
					else
						Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "huntUnsafeAfter", {
							target = enemy.tile, ratio = stack.result.ratio,
						})
					end
				else
					Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "huntLowHealth", {
						target = enemy.tile, ratio = stack.result.ratio, minHealth = minHealth,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if hunted > 0 then
		Logger.info("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "hunt", {
			hunted = hunted, targets = #targets, candidates = #ourCandidates,
			minRatio = minRatio, maxHops = maxHops,
		})
	end

	return { hunted = hunted, targets = #targets, candidates = #ourCandidates }
end

local function ownNeighbourCount(world, plan, tileName)
	local count = 0
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local claimant = plan.claims[neighbourName]
		local claimedByOurMove = claimant ~= nil and ((plan.byUnit[claimant] ~= nil and not plan.byUnit[claimant].isAttack) or plan.commitments[claimant] ~= nil)
		if world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me or claimedByOurMove then count += 1 end
	end
	return count
end

function AttackPlanner.captures(world, plan)
	local walkIns = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
		if not unit.flagged and unit.state ~= "Attacking" then
			for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
				if Tactics.isEnemyTile(world, neighbourName) and world.unitsByTile[neighbourName] == nil and plan:isFreeDestination(neighbourName)
					and not Plan.isRecentlyCleared(neighbourName) then
					local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]
					local cut = SupplyModel.unitsCutByLosing(world, owner, neighbourName)
					local isCity = MapGraph.tiles[neighbourName].cityName ~= nil
					local onAxis, axisDist = isOnAxis(world, neighbourName)
					local becomesSource = false
					if isCity then
						local cityFolder = GameModules.get("MapData").tileNameToCity[neighbourName]
						if cityFolder ~= nil then
							local ownerCountry = Ownership.country(owner)
							local cities = ownerCountry and ownerCountry:FindFirstChild("Cities")
							local folder = cities and cities:FindFirstChild(cityFolder.name)
							becomesSource = folder ~= nil and folder:GetAttribute("isPort") == true
						end
					end
					local strategic = onAxis or isCity or becomesSource or #cut >= OFF_AXIS_CUT_UNITS_MIN
					if strategic then
						table.insert(walkIns, {
							unit = unit, tile = neighbourName, cutUnits = #cut, isCity = isCity,
							onAxis = onAxis, axisDist = axisDist,
						})
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(walkIns, function(first, second)
		if first.onAxis ~= second.onAxis then return first.onAxis end
		if first.cutUnits ~= second.cutUnits then return first.cutUnits > second.cutUnits end
		if first.isCity ~= second.isCity then return first.isCity end
		if first.axisDist ~= second.axisDist then return first.axisDist < second.axisDist end
		if first.tile ~= second.tile then return first.tile < second.tile end
		return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
	end)
	for _, candidate in ipairs(walkIns) do
		local unit = candidate.unit
		if plan:isAvailable(unit) and plan:isFreeDestination(candidate.tile) then
			local placed = CombatModel.placedOn(unit, candidate.tile)
			local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, candidate.tile, placed)
			local originExposed = #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, unit.tile) > 0
			local broad = ownNeighbourCount(world, plan, candidate.tile) >= BROAD_FRONT_NEIGHBOURS
			local cityFolder = nil
			local becomesSource = false
			if candidate.isCity then
				cityFolder = GameModules.get("MapData").tileNameToCity[candidate.tile]
			end
			if cityFolder ~= nil then
				local ownerCountry = Ownership.country(world.tileOwner[candidate.tile])
				local cities = ownerCountry and ownerCountry:FindFirstChild("Cities")
				local folder = cities and cities:FindFirstChild(cityFolder.name)
				becomesSource = folder ~= nil and folder:GetAttribute("isPort") == true
			end
			if not threat.breaks and not originExposed and (broad or becomesSource) then
				local tier = TIER_CAPTURE
				local purpose = "walkIn"
				if candidate.cutUnits > 0 then tier = TIER_CUT; purpose = "cutWalkIn" end
				plan:assign(unit, { unit.tile, candidate.tile }, purpose, tier, "capture an empty enemy tile", {
					cutUnits = candidate.cutUnits, isCity = candidate.isCity, threatRatio = threat.ratio,
				})
			end
		end
	end
	local candidates = {}
	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if not NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES[enemy.state] then table.insert(candidates, enemy) end
	end
	table.sort(candidates, function(first, second)
		if first.health ~= second.health then return first.health < second.health end
		return first.id < second.id
	end)
	for _, enemy in ipairs(candidates) do
		local onAxis, axisDist = isOnAxis(world, enemy.tile)
		local defendingUs = enemy.state == "Attacking"
			and enemy.targetTile ~= nil
			and world.tileOwner[enemy.targetTile] == world.me
		local cut = SupplyModel.unitsCutByLosing(world, enemy.owner, enemy.tile)
		local adjacentToUs = false
		for _, nbr in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[enemy.tile].landNeighbours) do
			if world.tileOwner[nbr] == world.me then adjacentToUs = true; break end
		end
		local offAxisAllowed = defendingUs or #cut >= OFF_AXIS_CUT_UNITS_MIN or adjacentToUs

		if onAxis or offAxisAllowed then
			local strikeUnits = strikers(world, plan, enemy.tile, enemy.owner)
			if #strikeUnits > 0 then
				local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, enemy.tile, strikeUnits)
				if stack ~= nil and #stack.attackers > 0 then
					local ratio = stack.result.ratio or 0
					if not onAxis and ratio < OFF_AXIS_STRENGTH_RATIO and not defendingUs then
						Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "offAxisSkipped", {
							tile = enemy.tile, ratio = ratio,
							reason = "off-axis target below strength threshold",
						})
					else
						local allSafe = true
						local safetyFacts = {}
						for index, entry in ipairs(stack.all) do
							local healthAfter = stack.result.healthsAtBreak and stack.result.healthsAtBreak[index] or entry.unit.health
							local safe, facts = Tactics.safeAfterFight(world, entry, healthAfter, enemy.tile)
							table.insert(safetyFacts, facts)
							if not safe then allSafe = false end
						end
						if allSafe then
							local reason = onAxis
								and "winning attack on-axis"
								or (defendingUs and "counter-attack (defender attacked us)"
									or "off-axis but cuts supply")
							launch(plan, stack, enemy.tile, "attack", TIER_CAPTURE, reason)
						else
							Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "attackUnsafeAfter", { tile = enemy.tile, ratio = stack.result.ratio, safety = safetyFacts })
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

local function pinsAThreat(world, enemy)
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[enemy.tile].landNeighbours) do
		local ours = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]
		if ours ~= nil and ours.owner == world.me then
			local current = CombatModel.potentialAttackers(world, neighbourName, world.enemies)
			local withEnemy = table.clone(current)
			local alreadyIn = false
			for _, entry in ipairs(current) do
				if entry.unit.id == enemy.id then alreadyIn = true end
			end
			if not alreadyIn then
				table.insert(withEnemy, { unit = enemy, fromTile = enemy.tile })
				local before = #current > 0 and CombatModel.engage(world.combat, current, ours, neighbourName, "defender") or { breaks = false }
				local after = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, withEnemy, ours, neighbourName, "defender")
				if after.breaks and not before.breaks then return true, neighbourName end
			end
		end
	end
	return false, nil
end

function AttackPlanner.reviewOngoing(world, plan)
	local reviewed = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.state == "Attacking" and unit.targetTile ~= nil and plan:isUnassigned(unit) and not reviewed[unit.targetTile] then
			local targetTile = unit.targetTile
			reviewed[targetTile] = true
			local defender = world.unitsByTile[targetTile]
			local entries = Tactics.existingAttackers(world, targetTile)
			if defender ~= nil and #entries > 0 then
				local result = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, entries, defender, targetTile, "attacker")
			local pinning, pinnedTile = pinsAThreat(world, defender)
			local losing = not (result.breaks and not result.tie) and not pinning

			local avgHealthAfter = 0
			local hpList = result.healthsAtBreak or result.finalHealths or {}
			if #hpList > 0 then
				for _, h in ipairs(hpList) do avgHealthAfter += (type(h) == "number" and h or 0) end
				avgHealthAfter = avgHealthAfter / #hpList
				if avgHealthAfter < 30 then losing = true end
			end
				for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
					if entry.unit.owner == world.me and plan:isUnassigned(entry.unit) then
						if losing then
							plan:halt(entry.unit, TIER_REVIEW,
								"attack predicted losing; disengage before auto-retreat",
								{ ratio = result.ratio, breakSeconds = result.breakSeconds })
						elseif pinning then
							plan:hold(entry.unit, "attackPin", TIER_REVIEW, "the attack keeps the enemy from breaking " .. tostring(pinnedTile))
						else
							plan:hold(entry.unit, "attackContinue", TIER_REVIEW, "the running attack still wins")
						end
					end
				end
				if losing and AttackPlanner.reportedLosing[targetTile] == nil then
					AttackPlanner.reportedLosing[targetTile] = true
					Logger.info("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "attackLosing", { target = targetTile, ratio = result.ratio, attackers = #entries })
				elseif not losing then
					AttackPlanner.reportedLosing[targetTile] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", AttackPlanner)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/DefensePlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/DefensePlanner

	Defence tiers T0..T4. === [FASE-2] === T1 unsafe iterated round-robin per theater.
]]
local DefensePlanner = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")
local Tactics = require("Features/Front/Tactics")
local WorldView = require("Features/Front/WorldView")
local ThreatHeat = require("Features/Front/ThreatHeat")

local TIER_CAPITAL = "T0"
local TIER_UNSAFE = "T1"
local TIER_LANES = "T2"
local TIER_ROTATION = "T3"
local TIER_RESERVES = "T4"
local STATES_THAT_CANNOT_MOVE = { Defending = true, Retreating = true, Dead = true }

DefensePlanner.trappedReported = {}
DefensePlanner.unguardedReported = false

local function enemyNearZone(world, tileName)
	if #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, tileName) > 0 then return true end
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		if world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, neighbourName) > 0 then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function isRestTile(world, plan, tileName)
	return world.tileOwner[tileName] == world.me
		and plan:isFreeDestination(tileName)
		and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, tileName) == 0
		and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, tileName)
end

-- =====================================================================
-- [REST-HOLD] Unit sekarat: diam & heal sampai health >= 80.
-- Menggantikan rotateWounded + blok rest lama.
-- Aturan:
--   * Saat perang saja.
--   * Unit flagged (dieOnNextRetreat) ATAU health < 80 -> masuk mode diam.
--   * Kalau ada musuh flagged / health < 80 di sekitar dengan
--     ratio >= 1.3 dan health kita setelah menang >= 20 -> boleh serang.
--   * Kalau sedang Attacking -> jangan diganggu (AR tier yang urus).
--   * Kalau sedang Moving -> halt di tempat.
--   * Kalau cut off supply -> bebas cari supply (termasuk rute berbahaya).
-- =====================================================================
local REST_HEALTH_THRESHOLD = 80
local REST_ATTACK_RATIO_MIN = 1.3
local REST_ATTACK_MIN_HEALTH_AFTER = 20

---Cari target musuh di sekitar unit sekarat yang layak diserang.
---Kriteria musuh: flagged (pasti mati kalau kalah) ATAU health < 80.
---Kriteria kita: menang solo dengan ratio >= REST_ATTACK_RATIO_MIN dan
---health setelah menang >= REST_ATTACK_MIN_HEALTH_AFTER.
---@param world table
---@param unit table snapshot
---@return string? targetTile, number ratio
local function findRestAttackTarget(world, unit)
	local bestTile = nil
	local bestRatio = 0

	for _, nbr in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
		local enemy = world.unitsByTile[nbr]
		if enemy ~= nil and world.enemies[enemy.owner] then
			local isFlagged = enemy.flagged or enemy.liveFlag
			local isWeak = (enemy.health or 100) < 80

			if isFlagged or isWeak then
				local engage = CombatModel.engage(world.combat,
					{ { unit = unit, fromTile = unit.tile } },
					enemy, nbr, "attacker")

				if engage.breaks and not engage.tie
					and engage.ratio >= REST_ATTACK_RATIO_MIN then
					local healthAfter = engage.healthsAtBreak
						and engage.healthsAtBreak[1] or 100
					if healthAfter >= REST_ATTACK_MIN_HEALTH_AFTER
						and engage.ratio > bestRatio then
						bestRatio = engage.ratio
						bestTile = nbr
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return bestTile, bestRatio
end

-- [SPREAD-FIX] User request: 3 unit penjaga di ring capital (langsung tetangga capital).
-- Capital sendiri tetap 1 unit (di-hold oleh DefensePlanner.capital).
-- Sisanya bebas untuk spread ke border (lihat BorderPrep.gatherIdle mode OFF).
local CAPITAL_RING_SIZE = 3
local CAPITAL_RING_MIN_TOTAL_UNITS = 4

---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param capitalTile string
---@param zoneThreatened boolean  true = capital/neighbour sedang diserang
---@return number placed
local function ensureCapitalRing(world, plan, capitalTile, zoneThreatened)
	local record = MapGraph.tiles[capitalTile]
	if record == nil then return 0 end

	local totalUnits = 0
	for _, u in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if not u.flagged then totalUnits += 1 end
	end
	if totalUnits < CAPITAL_RING_MIN_TOTAL_UNITS then return 0 end

	local candidates = {}
	for _, nbr in ipairs(record.landNeighbours) do
		if world.tileOwner[nbr] == world.me then
			local exposure = #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, nbr)
			table.insert(candidates, { tile = nbr, exposure = exposure })
		end
	end
	if #candidates == 0 then return 0 end

	table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
		if a.exposure ~= b.exposure then return a.exposure > b.exposure end
		return a.tile < b.tile
	end)

	local placed = 0
	for _, entry in ipairs(candidates) do
		if placed >= CAPITAL_RING_SIZE then break end
		local tile = entry.tile
		local occ = world.unitsByTile[tile]

		if occ ~= nil and occ.owner == world.me and not occ.flagged then
			-- [FIX-RING-ATTACK] Hold HANYA saat capital tidak terancam.
			-- Saat musuh menyerang, biarkan unit ini bebas supaya tier T1
			-- (relief) dan A0 (capitalStrike) bisa memakainya untuk melawan.
			if not zoneThreatened and plan:isAvailable(occ) then
				plan:hold(occ, "capitalRingHold", TIER_CAPITAL,
					"already guarding a capital ring tile (peaceful)")
			end
			placed += 1
		elseif occ == nil and plan:isFreeDestination(tile) then
			local pool = {}
			for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
				if not unit.flagged and unit.tile ~= tile then
					table.insert(pool, unit)
				end
			end
			if #pool > 0 then
				local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, pool, tile, nil)
				plan:decide(TIER_CAPITAL, "capitalRing:" .. tile, ranked,
					"nearest unit fills the capital ring")
				if best ~= nil then
					local placedUnit = CombatModel.placedOn(best.unit, tile)
					local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, tile, placedUnit)
					if not threat.breaks then
						plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, "capitalRingGuard", TIER_CAPITAL,
							string.format("capital ring tile (exposure %d)", entry.exposure),
							{ capitalTile = capitalTile, exposure = entry.exposure, seconds = best.seconds })
						placed += 1
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return placed
end

function DefensePlanner.capital(world, plan)
	local capitalTile = world.capitalTile
	if capitalTile == nil then
		Logger.error("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "capitalUnknown", { reason = world.capitalReason })
		return
	end
	local occupant = world.unitsByTile[capitalTile]
	local zoneThreatened = enemyNearZone(world, capitalTile)
	if occupant ~= nil then DefensePlanner.unguardedReported = false end
	if occupant ~= nil and occupant.owner == world.me then
		if occupant.flagged and not zoneThreatened and plan:isAvailable(occupant) then
			local replacements = {}
			for _, unit in ipairs(Tactics.availableNeighbours(world, plan, capitalTile)) do
				if not unit.flagged and unit.id ~= occupant.id and unit.owner == world.me then
					table.insert(replacements, unit)
				end
			end
			local restPath = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, occupant, function(tileName) return isRestTile(world, plan, tileName) end, true)
			if #replacements > 0 and restPath ~= nil then
				local replacement = replacements[1]
				plan:assign(occupant, restPath, "capitalRotateOut", TIER_CAPITAL, "flagged capital defender rests while no enemy is near", { health = occupant.health })
				plan:assign(replacement, { replacement.tile, capitalTile }, "capitalRotateIn", TIER_CAPITAL, "unflagged neighbour takes the capital", { health = replacement.health, from = replacement.tile })
			else
				plan:hold(occupant, "capitalHold", TIER_CAPITAL, "flagged, but no replacement or rest tile")
				Logger.warn("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "flaggedCapitalDefender", { tile = capitalTile, replacements = #replacements, restPath = restPath ~= nil })
			end
		else
			plan:hold(occupant, "capitalHold", TIER_CAPITAL, "the capital is never left empty")
		end
	elseif occupant == nil then
		local incumbent = nil
		for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
			if unit.targetTile == capitalTile and plan.byUnit[unit.id] == nil then incumbent = unit end
		end
		if incumbent ~= nil then
			plan:hold(incumbent, "capitalGuard", TIER_CAPITAL, "already entering the empty capital")
		else
			local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, plan:availableUnits(), capitalTile, nil)
			local purpose = "capitalGuard"
			if best == nil then
				local userUnits = {}
				for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
					if plan.blockedUnits[unit.id] and plan.byUnit[unit.id] == nil and not STATES_THAT_CANNOT_MOVE[unit.state] then
						table.insert(userUnits, unit)
					end
				end
				best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, userUnits, capitalTile, nil)
				purpose = "capitalGuardOverridesUser"
			end
			plan:decide(TIER_CAPITAL, purpose, ranked, "earliest arrival at our empty capital")
			if best ~= nil then
				DefensePlanner.unguardedReported = false
				plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, purpose, TIER_CAPITAL, "our capital is empty", { seconds = best.seconds })
				if purpose == "capitalGuardOverridesUser" then
					Logger.warn("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "capitalGuardOverridesUser", { unit = best.unit.id, tile = best.unit.tile, seconds = best.seconds })
				end
			elseif not DefensePlanner.unguardedReported then
				DefensePlanner.unguardedReported = true
				Logger.error("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "capitalUnguarded", { tile = capitalTile, reason = "no unit of ours can reach it" })
			end
		end
	else
		Logger.error("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "capitalHeldByOther", { tile = capitalTile, owner = occupant.owner, troop = occupant.troopName })
	end
	local laneTile = nil
	local capitalAsUnit = { owner = world.me, tile = capitalTile }
	for _, exitName in ipairs(FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, capitalAsUnit)) do
		if laneTile == nil and plan.locks[exitName] == nil and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, exitName) == 0 then laneTile = exitName end
	end
	if laneTile == nil then
		for _, exitName in ipairs(FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, capitalAsUnit)) do
			if laneTile == nil and plan.locks[exitName] == nil then laneTile = exitName end
		end
	end
	if laneTile ~= nil then
		plan:lock(laneTile, "lane:" .. capitalTile)
	elseif zoneThreatened then
		local cleared = false
		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[capitalTile].landNeighbours) do
			local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]
			if not cleared and unit ~= nil and plan:isAvailable(unit) and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, neighbourName) == 0 then
				local path = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit, function(tileName) return tileName ~= capitalTile and isRestTile(world, plan, tileName) end, true)
				if path ~= nil then
					plan:assign(unit, path, "capitalLaneClear", TIER_CAPITAL, "the capital had no free neighbour", { from = neighbourName })
					plan:lock(neighbourName, "lane:" .. capitalTile)
					cleared = true
				end
			end
		end
		if not cleared then
			Logger.warn("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "capitalWithoutLane", { tile = capitalTile, reason = "every neighbour is enemy-held or held by a unit that cannot move" })
		end
	end
	if zoneThreatened then
		local hasReserve = false
		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[capitalTile].landNeighbours) do
			local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]
			if unit ~= nil and unit.owner == world.me and not unit.flagged and not STATES_THAT_CANNOT_MOVE[unit.state] then
				hasReserve = true
				if plan:isAvailable(unit) then
					plan:hold(unit, "capitalReserve", TIER_CAPITAL, "re-enters the capital if its defender leaves")
				end
				break
			end
		end
		if not hasReserve then
			local bestPlacement = nil
			local bestRanked = {}
			for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[capitalTile].landNeighbours) do
				if world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me and plan:isFreeDestination(neighbourName) then
					local candidates = {}
					for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
						if not unit.flagged then table.insert(candidates, unit) end
					end
					local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, neighbourName, bestPlacement and bestPlacement.seconds or nil)
					if best ~= nil and (bestPlacement == nil or best.seconds < bestPlacement.seconds) then
						bestPlacement = best
						bestRanked = ranked
					end
				end
			end
			plan:decide(TIER_CAPITAL, "capitalReserve", bestRanked, "earliest unflagged arrival next to the capital")
			if bestPlacement ~= nil then
				plan:assign(bestPlacement.unit, bestPlacement.path, "capitalReserve", TIER_CAPITAL, "enemies near the capital and no reserve next to it", { seconds = bestPlacement.seconds })
			end
		end
	end

	ensureCapitalRing(world, plan, capitalTile, zoneThreatened)
end

function DefensePlanner.unsafe(world, plan, analysis, attackPlanner)
	if #analysis.unsafe == 0 then return end

	local theaters = FrontAnalyzer.theaters(world, analysis)
	local tileToEntry = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		tileToEntry[entry.tile] = entry
	end

	local queues = {}
	for index, theater in ipairs(theaters) do
		local queue = {}
		for _, tileName in ipairs(theater.tiles) do
			local entry = tileToEntry[tileName]
			if entry ~= nil then table.insert(queue, entry) end
		end
		table.sort(queue, function(first, second)
			if first.consequence ~= second.consequence then return first.consequence < second.consequence end
			if first.threat.ratio ~= second.threat.ratio then return first.threat.ratio > second.threat.ratio end
			return first.tile < second.tile
		end)
		queues[index] = queue
	end

	local round = 1
	while true do
		local progressed = false
		for _, queue in ipairs(queues) do
			local entry = queue[round]
			if entry ~= nil then
				progressed = true
				local defender = entry.unit
				local tileName = entry.tile
				local fixed = false
				local relieved = attackPlanner.relieve(world, plan, entry)
				if relieved.remainingThreat ~= nil and not relieved.remainingThreat.breaks then fixed = true end
				if not fixed and plan:isAvailable(defender) then
					local enemyEntry = FrontAnalyzer.enemyEntrySeconds(world, tileName)
					local lanes = FrontAnalyzer.safeLanes(world, plan, defender)
					local ranked = {}
					if #lanes > 0 then
						for _, candidate in ipairs(Tactics.availableNeighbours(world, plan, tileName)) do
							if candidate.id ~= defender.id and candidate.owner == world.me and not candidate.flagged and world.tileOwner[candidate.tile] == world.me then
								local placed = CombatModel.placedOn(candidate, tileName)
								local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, tileName, placed)
								local hop = Pathing.hopSeconds(world.paths, candidate, candidate.tile, tileName)
