	return { distance = distance, towardSource = toward }
end

function Pathing.retreatSeconds(unit, fromName, toName)
	return Formula.evaluate("move.retreatSeconds", {
		distance = MapGraph.edgeLength(fromName, toName), speed = unit.speed,
	})
end

return Pathing
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher

	The single door for every non-movement remote (economy, research, focus, army groups, navy,
	air). Same rules as the order executor: the act-or-stop gate decides between sending and
	dry-run; an action already in flight is not sent twice; every call and every reply is logged.

	=== BUGFIX-C4 (2026-10-01) ===
	ActionDispatcher.history is now bounded via Stats.pushBounded.
	ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock is pruned once per day tick.
]]

local ActionDispatcher = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Stats
local Stats = require("Utility/Stats")

---@module Game.Remotes
local Remotes = require("Game/Remotes")

---@module Features.Core.Guard
local Guard = require("Features/Core/Guard")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

-- Constants.
local RESULTS_START_INDEX = 2
local PROMPTING_REPLIES = { PurchaseFactory = true, PurchaseDefense = true }
local REPLY_REMOTES = { "PurchaseFactory", "PurchaseDefense", "AddTroopFromTileToArmy" }
local MAX_HISTORY = 256
local EVENT_CLOCK_MAX_AGE = 30

-- State.
ActionDispatcher.maid = nil
ActionDispatcher.inFlight = {}
ActionDispatcher.lastDryRun = {}
ActionDispatcher.lastByRemote = {}
ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock = {}
ActionDispatcher.stats = { dispatched = 0, dryRun = 0, dryRunRepeats = 0, duplicates = 0, results = 0, failures = 0, refusals = 0 }
ActionDispatcher.history = {}

local function describeArgs(args)
	local described = {}

	for index, value in ipairs(args) do
		if typeof(value) == "Instance" then
			described[index] = value:GetFullName()
		else
			described[index] = value
		end
	end

	return described
end

-- [BUGFIX-C4] Prune eventSentClock entries older than EVENT_CLOCK_MAX_AGE.
function ActionDispatcher.pruneEventClock()
	local now = os.clock()
	for key, sentAt in pairs(ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock) do
		if now - sentAt > EVENT_CLOCK_MAX_AGE then
			ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock[key] = nil
		end
	end
end

function ActionDispatcher.dispatch(action)
	local verdict = Guard.check()
	local described = describeArgs(action.args)

	if not verdict.canSend then
		ActionDispatcher.stats.dryRun += 1

		if ActionDispatcher.lastDryRun[action.key] ~= nil then
			ActionDispatcher.stats.dryRunRepeats += 1
		else
			ActionDispatcher.lastDryRun[action.key] = true
			Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "dryRunAction", {
				remote = action.remote,
				args = described,
				reason = action.reason,
				tier = action.tier,
				gate = verdict.reason,
			})
		end

		return "dryRun"
	end

	if ActionDispatcher.inFlight[action.key] then
		ActionDispatcher.stats.duplicates += 1
		return "duplicate"
	end

	local entry = Remotes.entry(action.remote)

	if entry.class == "RemoteEvent" then
		local sentClock = ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock[action.key]
		local period = Tick.periodSeconds()

		if sentClock ~= nil and period ~= nil and os.clock() - sentClock < period then
			ActionDispatcher.stats.duplicates += 1
			return "duplicate"
		end

		ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock[action.key] = os.clock()
	end

	ActionDispatcher.stats.dispatched += 1
	ActionDispatcher.lastByRemote[action.remote] = { key = action.key, args = described, reason = action.reason, sentServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() }

	Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "actionSent", {
		remote = action.remote,
		args = described,
		reason = action.reason,
		tier = action.tier,
	})

	if entry.class == "RemoteEvent" then
		Remotes.send(action.remote, table.unpack(action.args))
		return "sent"
	end

	ActionDispatcher.inFlight[action.key] = true

	local thread = task.spawn(function()
		local packed = table.pack(pcall(Remotes.send, action.remote, table.unpack(action.args)))
		ActionDispatcher.inFlight[action.key] = nil

		if not packed[1] then
			ActionDispatcher.stats.failures += 1
			Logger.error("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "invokeFailed", {
				remote = action.remote,
				args = described,
				message = tostring(packed[RESULTS_START_INDEX]),
			})
			return
		end

		local results = { table.unpack(packed, RESULTS_START_INDEX, packed.n) }
		ActionDispatcher.stats.results += 1

		-- [BUGFIX-C4] bounded history
		Stats.pushBounded(ActionDispatcher.history, { remote = action.remote, key = action.key, ok = results[1], results = results, serverTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() }, MAX_HISTORY)

		Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "actionResult", {
			remote = action.remote,
			args = described,
			results = results,
			reason = action.reason,
		})

		if action.onResult ~= nil then
			action.onResult(results[1], results)
		end
	end)
	-- [FIX-B1] Thread tidak boleh di-give ke maid: Maid tidak punya operasi remove, sehingga
	-- ActionDispatcher.maid.tasks tumbuh satu entri per aksi RemoteFunction sepanjang sesi.

	return "sent"
end

function ActionDispatcher.attach(maid)
	ActionDispatcher.maid = maid

	for _, remoteName in ipairs(REPLY_REMOTES) do
		maid:give(Remotes.replySignal(remoteName):Connect(function(...)
			local reply = table.pack(...)
			local ok = reply[1]
			local last = ActionDispatcher.lastByRemote[remoteName]

			ActionDispatcher.stats.results += 1

			local payload = {
				remote = remoteName,
				reply = describeArgs({ table.unpack(reply, 1, reply.n) }),
				lastAction = last,
			}

			local period = Tick.periodSeconds()
			local ours = last ~= nil and period ~= nil and workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - last.sentServerTime <= period

			if not ours then
				Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "replyToUserAction", payload)
			elseif PROMPTING_REPLIES[remoteName] and ok == false then
				ActionDispatcher.stats.refusals += 1
				Logger.error("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "purchaseRefused", payload)
			else
				Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "reply", payload)
			end
		end))
	end

	-- [BUGFIX-C4] prune clock table on every day tick
	maid:give(Tick.dayTick:connect(function()
		ActionDispatcher.pruneEventClock()
	end))

	Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "attached", { replyRemotes = REPLY_REMOTES })
end

function ActionDispatcher.reset(reason)
	table.clear(ActionDispatcher.inFlight)
	table.clear(ActionDispatcher.lastDryRun)
	table.clear(ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock)
	Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "reset", { reason = reason })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", ActionDispatcher)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Orders/ArmyControl"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Orders/ArmyControl

	=== [FIX-GEN-8] Full-lock per group — stop pop-up "Max division size for army is 10" ===
	Rekomendasi user: begitu group sudah menyentuh kapasitas penuh (10/10), fitur DIAM
	TOTAL untuk group itu sampai jumlah unit di group turun di bawah kapasitas
	(misal ada 1 unit gugur -> 9/10), baru fitur boleh bekerja lagi.

	Mekanisme:
	  1. LOCK — saat snapshot menunjukkan inside >= MaxDivisions, group masuk lock.
	     Selama lock: TIDAK ada request apapun (add/remove/enforce/target/replenish).
	  2. UNLOCK — saat snapshot menunjukkan inside < MaxDivisions, minimal sudah
	     ter-lock 10 detik (mencegah flap dari snapshot jitter).
	  3. Safety lapis-3 tetap: reply hook + backoff + modal safety net.

	Batas tambahan: maksimal 1 add + 1 remove per group per tick (anti burst).
]]

local ArmyControl = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.CountryState
local CountryState = require("Game/CountryState")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

---@module Utility.Serializer
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Movement.Pathing
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

---@module Features.Front.FrontAnalyzer
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

---@module Game.Remotes
local Remotes = require("Game/Remotes")

local TIER = "C"

-- Mode jenderal.
local GENERAL_MODE_COMMAND = "Defend"
local GENERAL_UNLOCK_RETRY_SECONDS = 60
local GENERAL_STATUS_INTERVAL = 30
local GENERAL_ADD_ARMY_INTERVAL = 20

-- Anti-spam.
local GENERAL_BACKOFF_SECONDS = 30
local GENERAL_MAX_ADDS_PER_GROUP_PER_TICK = 1
local GENERAL_MAX_REMOVES_PER_GROUP_PER_TICK = 1
local GENERAL_PENDING_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 8
local GENERAL_ERROR_HARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD = 3

-- [FIX-GEN-8] Full-lock.
local GENERAL_FULL_LOCK_MIN_SECONDS = 10
local GENERAL_FULL_LOCK_LOG_INTERVAL = 20

-- State umum.
ArmyControl.unlockAttempts = {}
ArmyControl.blockedGenerals = {}
ArmyControl.lastGeneralReply = nil
ArmyControl.lastGeneralStatus = nil
ArmyControl.lastGeneralStatusClock = 0
ArmyControl.lastAddArmyClock = 0

-- Anti-spam state.
ArmyControl.pendingByArmy = {}
ArmyControl.pendingQueue = {}
ArmyControl.blockedArmies = {}
ArmyControl.errorCountByArmy = {}
ArmyControl.addReplyHook = nil
ArmyControl.lastAddReply = nil
ArmyControl.modalSafetyNetCleanup = nil
ArmyControl.booted = false

-- [FIX-GEN-8] Full-lock state.
ArmyControl.fullLockedArmies = {}
ArmyControl.lastFullLockLog = {}

local TROOP_CLASSES = { "Infantry", "Tanks", "AntiTank" }

-- =====================================================================
-- Status & diagnostics
-- =====================================================================

local function reportGeneralStatus(message)
	local now = os.clock()
	if ArmyControl.lastGeneralStatus == message
		and (now - ArmyControl.lastGeneralStatusClock) < GENERAL_STATUS_INTERVAL then
		return
	end
	ArmyControl.lastGeneralStatus = message
	ArmyControl.lastGeneralStatusClock = now
	print("[CTW_WarAI] general: " .. message)
end

local function logFullLockForce(armyName, message)
	ArmyControl.lastFullLockLog[armyName] = os.clock()
	print("[CTW_WarAI] general: " .. message)
end

local function logFullLockThrottled(armyName, message)
	local now = os.clock()
	local last = ArmyControl.lastFullLockLog[armyName] or 0
	if (now - last) >= GENERAL_FULL_LOCK_LOG_INTERVAL then
		ArmyControl.lastFullLockLog[armyName] = now
		print("[CTW_WarAI] general: " .. message)
	end
end

-- =====================================================================
-- Modal safety net (lapis-3)
-- =====================================================================

local function installModalSafetyNet()
	local players = game:GetService("Players")
	local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
	if localPlayer == nil then return nil end

	local playerGui = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
	if playerGui == nil then return nil end

	local gameGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("GameGui")
	if gameGui == nil then return nil end

	local modalFrame = gameGui:FindFirstChild("ModalFrame")
	if modalFrame == nil then return nil end

	local TITLE_PATTERNS = {
		"max division", "division size", "army is full", "max army", "division limit",
	}
	local OK_NAMES = { "Ok", "OK", "No", "Cancel" }
	local DISMISS_COOLDOWN = 0.5
	local lastDismissClock = 0

	local function matches(text)
		if type(text) ~= "string" then return false end
		local lower = string.lower(text)
		for _, pattern in ipairs(TITLE_PATTERNS) do
			if string.find(lower, pattern, 1, true) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function tryDismiss()
		if modalFrame.Visible ~= true then return end
		local now = os.clock()
		if (now - lastDismissClock) < DISMISS_COOLDOWN then return end

		local title = modalFrame:FindFirstChild("Title")
		local text = title and title:IsA("TextLabel") and title.Text or nil
		if not matches(text) then return end

		for _, name in ipairs(OK_NAMES) do
			local button = modalFrame:FindFirstChild(name)
			if button and button:IsA("GuiButton") and button.Visible then
				lastDismissClock = now
				pcall(function() button:Activate() end)
				pcall(function() modalFrame.Visible = false end)
				print(string.format("[CTW_WarAI] modal auto-dismissed: %s (via %s)", tostring(text), name))
				return
			end
		end
	end

	local conn1 = modalFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(tryDismiss)
	local conn2 = nil
	local title = modalFrame:FindFirstChild("Title")
	if title and title:IsA("TextLabel") then
		conn2 = title:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(tryDismiss)
	end

	return function()
		if conn1 then conn1:Disconnect() end
		if conn2 then conn2:Disconnect() end
	end
end

-- =====================================================================
-- Anti-spam helpers
-- =====================================================================

local function isArmyBlocked(armyName)
	local untilClock = ArmyControl.blockedArmies[armyName]
	if untilClock == nil then return false end
	if os.clock() >= untilClock then
		ArmyControl.blockedArmies[armyName] = nil
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function hasPending(armyName)
	local clock = ArmyControl.pendingByArmy[armyName]
	if clock == nil then return false end
	if (os.clock() - clock) > GENERAL_PENDING_TIMEOUT_SECONDS then
		ArmyControl.pendingByArmy[armyName] = nil
		for index = #ArmyControl.pendingQueue, 1, -1 do
			if ArmyControl.pendingQueue[index].army == armyName then
				table.remove(ArmyControl.pendingQueue, index)
			end
		end
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function markPending(armyName)
	local clock = os.clock()
	ArmyControl.pendingByArmy[armyName] = clock
	table.insert(ArmyControl.pendingQueue, { army = armyName, clock = clock })
end

local function onAddTroopReply(_inst, value)
	if value == "On" or value == "Off" then
		local head = table.remove(ArmyControl.pendingQueue, 1)
		if head ~= nil and ArmyControl.pendingByArmy[head.army] == head.clock then
			ArmyControl.pendingByArmy[head.army] = nil
		end
		ArmyControl.lastAddReply = { value = value, ok = true, at = os.clock() }
		return
	end

	-- Server error. Naikkan error count per group.
	local blockedCount = 0
	local hardBlocked = 0
	for _, entry in ipairs(ArmyControl.pendingQueue) do
		local count = (ArmyControl.errorCountByArmy[entry.army] or 0) + 1
		ArmyControl.errorCountByArmy[entry.army] = count
		ArmyControl.pendingByArmy[entry.army] = nil
		if count >= GENERAL_ERROR_HARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD then
			-- [FIX-GEN-8] Hard-block: tidak sentuh group ini sampai reset.
			ArmyControl.blockedArmies[entry.army] = math.huge
			hardBlocked += 1
		else
			ArmyControl.blockedArmies[entry.army] = os.clock() + GENERAL_BACKOFF_SECONDS
			blockedCount += 1
		end
	end
	table.clear(ArmyControl.pendingQueue)
	ArmyControl.lastAddReply = {
		value = tostring(value), ok = false,
		blocked = blockedCount, hardBlocked = hardBlocked, at = os.clock(),
	}
	reportGeneralStatus(string.format(
		"AddTroop ditolak server (%s); %d backoff, %d hard-block",
		tostring(value), blockedCount, hardBlocked))
end

local function tryDispatchAddTroop(army, tileInstance, unitId, reason)
	if isArmyBlocked(army.name) then return false end
	if hasPending(army.name) then return false end
	markPending(army.name)
	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "AddTroopFromTileToArmy",
		args = { tileInstance, army.name },
		key = "gen:in:" .. army.name .. ":" .. tostring(unitId),
		reason = reason,
		tier = TIER,
	})
	return true
end

local function tryDispatchRemoveTroop(army, tileInstance, unitId, reason)
	if isArmyBlocked(army.name) then return false end
	if hasPending(army.name) then return false end
	markPending(army.name)
	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "AddTroopFromTileToArmy",
		args = { tileInstance, army.name },
		key = "gen:out:" .. army.name .. ":" .. tostring(unitId),
		reason = reason,
		tier = TIER,
	})
	return true
end

-- =====================================================================
-- Full-lock helpers [FIX-GEN-8]
-- =====================================================================

local function countUnitsInArmy(world, armyName)
	local n = 0
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.army == armyName then n = n + 1 end
	end
	return n
end

local function isArmyOperational(army)
	if army.command ~= GENERAL_MODE_COMMAND then return false end
	local t = army.targetCountries
	if type(t) == "string" and t ~= "[]" and t ~= "" then return false end
	if army.replenishTroops == true then return false end
	return true
end

local function isArmyFullLocked(armyName)
	return ArmyControl.fullLockedArmies[armyName] ~= nil
end

---Update lock/unlock state berdasarkan snapshot siklus ini.
local function updateArmyFullLock(world, armies, capacityByArmy)
	for _, army in ipairs(armies) do
		local capacity = capacityByArmy[army.name] or 10
		local inside = countUnitsInArmy(world, army.name)
		local lock = ArmyControl.fullLockedArmies[army.name]

		-- Hanya lock kalau group sudah operational (enforce mode selesai).
		-- Kalau belum operational, biarkan enforcement menyelesaikan tugasnya dulu.
		if isArmyOperational(army) then
			if inside >= capacity then
				if lock == nil then
					ArmyControl.fullLockedArmies[army.name] = { since = os.clock(), capacity = capacity }
					logFullLockForce(army.name, string.format(
						"%s FULL (%d/%d) -> LOCKED (diam sampai ada unit gugur)",
						army.name, inside, capacity))
				else
					logFullLockThrottled(army.name, string.format(
						"%s masih LOCKED (%d/%d)", army.name, inside, capacity))
				end
			elseif lock ~= nil then
				local elapsed = os.clock() - lock.since
				if elapsed >= GENERAL_FULL_LOCK_MIN_SECONDS then
					ArmyControl.fullLockedArmies[army.name] = nil
					logFullLockForce(army.name, string.format(
						"%s UNLOCKED (%d/%d, held %.1fs) -> AI aktif lagi",
						army.name, inside, capacity, elapsed))
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

-- =====================================================================
-- Jenderal helpers
-- =====================================================================

local function ownedGenerals(country)
	local owned = {}
	local armyFolder = country:FindFirstChild("Army")
	local boughtText = armyFolder and armyFolder:GetAttribute("boughtGenerals")
	if type(boughtText) ~= "string" then return owned end
	local ok, decoded = pcall(Serializer.decode, boughtText, "Army.boughtGenerals")
	if ok and type(decoded) == "table" then
		for _, name in ipairs(decoded) do
			if type(name) == "string" then owned[name] = true end
		end
	end
	return owned
end

local function generalBuffedClasses(armyCommon, generalName)
	local buffed = {}
	local okFind, entry = pcall(armyCommon.FindGeneralByName, generalName)
	if not okFind or entry == nil then return buffed end

	local texts = {}
	local okBuff, buffs = pcall(armyCommon.GetGeneralBuffsRTF, entry)
	if okBuff and type(buffs) == "table" then
		for _, line in ipairs(buffs) do
			if type(line) == "string" then table.insert(texts, line) end
		end
	end
	if type(entry.description) == "string" then table.insert(texts, entry.description) end
	if type(entry.buffs) == "table" then
		for key, value in pairs(entry.buffs) do
			if type(key) == "string" then table.insert(texts, key) end
			if type(value) == "string" then table.insert(texts, value) end
		end
	end

	for _, text in ipairs(texts) do
		for _, className in ipairs(TROOP_CLASSES) do
			if string.find(text, className, 1, true) then buffed[className] = true end
		end
	end
	return buffed
end

---Kurasi isi SATU group. Return awal kalau full-locked.
local function curateGeneralGroup(world, army, armyCommon, generalName, claimed, fillRest)
	if isArmyFullLocked(army.name) then
		return { capacity = 0, targetCapacity = 0, inside = 0, added = 0, removed = 0, locked = true }
	end
	if isArmyBlocked(army.name) then
		return { capacity = 0, targetCapacity = 0, inside = 0, added = 0, removed = 0, blocked = true }
	end

	local buffed = generalBuffedClasses(armyCommon, generalName)

	local capacity = army.folder:GetAttribute("MaxDivisions")
	if type(capacity) ~= "number" or capacity <= 0 then capacity = 10 end
	-- [FIX-GEN-8] Target = kapasitas penuh. Lock akan mencegah over-fill dari sisi drift.
	local targetCapacity = capacity

	local inside, outside = {}, {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.army == army.name then
			table.insert(inside, unit)
		elseif unit.army == nil then
			table.insert(outside, unit)
		end
	end

	local function better(first, second)
		local firstBuffed = buffed[first.class] and 1 or 0
		local secondBuffed = buffed[second.class] and 1 or 0
		if firstBuffed ~= secondBuffed then return firstBuffed > secondBuffed end
		local firstPower = (first.attack or 1) * (first.health or 1)
		local secondPower = (second.attack or 1) * (second.health or 1)
		if firstPower ~= secondPower then return firstPower > secondPower end
		return first.id < second.id
	end
	table.sort(inside, better)
	table.sort(outside, better)

	local function claimable(unit)
		return claimed[unit.id] == nil or claimed[unit.id] == army.name
	end

	local function toggleAllowed(unit)
		local bucket = world.unitsOnTile and world.unitsOnTile[unit.tile]
		return bucket == nil or #bucket <= 1
	end

	-- Hitung siapa yang DIWANT.
	local want = {}
	local slots = 0

	-- Hysteresis: unit inside yang match-buff selalu di-want lebih dulu.
	for _, unit in ipairs(inside) do
		if slots >= targetCapacity then break end
		if claimable(unit) and buffed[unit.class] then
			want[unit.id] = true
			claimed[unit.id] = army.name
			slots += 1
		end
	end

	-- Isi dari outside yang match-buff.
	for _, unit in ipairs(outside) do
		if slots >= targetCapacity then break end
		if claimable(unit) and buffed[unit.class] then
			want[unit.id] = true
			claimed[unit.id] = army.name
			slots += 1
		end
	end

	-- Sisa slot (hanya di pass fillRest).
	if fillRest then
		for _, unit in ipairs(inside) do
			if slots >= targetCapacity then break end
			if want[unit.id] == nil and claimable(unit) then
				want[unit.id] = true
				claimed[unit.id] = army.name
				slots += 1
			end
		end
		for _, unit in ipairs(outside) do
			if slots >= targetCapacity then break end
			if want[unit.id] == nil and claimable(unit) then
				want[unit.id] = true
				claimed[unit.id] = army.name
				slots += 1
			end
		end
	end

	-- Dispatch add/remove (hanya di pass fillRest).
	local added, removed = 0, 0
	if fillRest then
		for _, unit in ipairs(inside) do
			if removed >= GENERAL_MAX_REMOVES_PER_GROUP_PER_TICK then break end
			if not want[unit.id] and toggleAllowed(unit) then
				local record = MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile]
				if record ~= nil then
					if tryDispatchRemoveTroop(army, record.instance, unit.id,
						"bukan kelas yang di-buff jenderal, slot dialihkan") then
						removed += 1
					end
				end
			end
		end
		for _, unit in ipairs(outside) do
			if added >= GENERAL_MAX_ADDS_PER_GROUP_PER_TICK then break end
			if want[unit.id] and toggleAllowed(unit) then
				local record = MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile]
				if record ~= nil then
					if tryDispatchAddTroop(army, record.instance, unit.id,
						"kelas yang di-buff jenderal, masuk group") then
						added += 1
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if fillRest and (added > 0 or removed > 0) then
		reportGeneralStatus(string.format(
			"kurasi %s: masuk=%d keluar=%d (target %d/%d, buffed %s)",
			army.name, added, removed, slots, targetCapacity,
			table.concat((function()
				local list = {}
				for name in pairs(buffed) do table.insert(list, name) end
				table.sort(list)
				return list
			end)(), "/")))
	end

	return {
		capacity = capacity,
		targetCapacity = targetCapacity,
		inside = #inside,
		added = added,
		removed = removed,
	}
end

---Pastikan satu group pasif: Defend + tanpa target + replenish OFF.
local function enforceGroupMode(army)
	local facts = {}
	if army.command ~= GENERAL_MODE_COMMAND then
		ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
			remote = "ChangeArmyOrder",
			args = { army.folder, GENERAL_MODE_COMMAND },
			key = "gen:order:" .. army.name,
			reason = "mode jenderal: server pasif, WarAI yang menggerakkan unit",
			tier = TIER,
		})
		facts.order = GENERAL_MODE_COMMAND
	end
	local targets = army.targetCountries
	if type(targets) == "string" and targets ~= "[]" and targets ~= "" then
		ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
			remote = "ChangeTargetCountries",
			args = { army.folder, {} },
			key = "gen:targets:" .. army.name,
			reason = "mode jenderal: hapus target agar server tidak menyerang sendiri",
			tier = TIER,
		})
		facts.clearedTargets = true
	end
	if army.replenishTroops == true then
		ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
			remote = "ToggleArmyReplenish",
			args = { army.folder },
			key = "gen:replenishOff:" .. army.name,
			reason = "kurasi manual: spawn baru tidak boleh masuk acak",
			tier = TIER,
		})
		facts.replenishOff = true
	end
	return facts
end

local function usableGenerals(armyCommon, world, owned)
	local pool = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(armyCommon.generals) do
		if type(entry) == "table" and type(entry.name) == "string" then
			local condition = type(entry.unlockCondition) == "table" and entry.unlockCondition or {}
			if owned[entry.name] == true then
				table.insert(pool, { name = entry.name, cost = 0, owned = true })
			elseif condition.gamepass == nil then
				local okGate, unlockable = pcall(armyCommon.IsGeneralUnlockable, entry, world.meInstance)
				if okGate and unlockable == true then
					table.insert(pool, {
						name = entry.name,
						cost = type(entry.cost) == "number" and entry.cost or math.huge,
						owned = false,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(pool, function(first, second)
		if first.owned ~= second.owned then return first.owned end
		if first.cost ~= second.cost then return first.cost < second.cost end
		return first.name < second.name
	end)
	return pool
end

-- =====================================================================
-- Main loop
-- =====================================================================

local function maintainGeneralGroups(world)
	local armyCommon = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon")
	if armyCommon.generals == nil then
		return { noGeneralList = true }
	end

	-- Boot print sekali.
	if not ArmyControl.booted then
		ArmyControl.booted = true
		print("[CTW_WarAI] ArmyControl v2 (full-lock at capacity) active")
	end

	-- Install hook reply + safety net (retry tiap cycle sampai berhasil).
	if ArmyControl.addReplyHook == nil then
		local ok, conn = pcall(function()
			return Remotes.replySignal("AddTroopFromTileToArmy"):Connect(onAddTroopReply)
		end)
		if ok and conn ~= nil then
			ArmyControl.addReplyHook = conn
			print("[CTW_WarAI] general: AddTroopFromTileToArmy reply hook installed")
		end
	end
	if ArmyControl.modalSafetyNetCleanup == nil then
		local cleanup = installModalSafetyNet()
		if cleanup ~= nil then
			ArmyControl.modalSafetyNetCleanup = cleanup
			print("[CTW_WarAI] general: modal safety net installed")
		end
	end

	local armyFolder = Resolver.requireChild(world.meInstance, "Army")
	local armiesRoot = Resolver.requireChild(armyFolder, "Armies")

	local armies = {}
	for _, child in ipairs(armiesRoot:GetChildren()) do
		table.insert(armies, { name = child.Name, folder = child })
	end
	table.sort(armies, function(first, second) return first.name < second.name end)

	local maxArmies = armyFolder:GetAttribute("maxArmies")
	if type(maxArmies) ~= "number" or maxArmies <= 0 then maxArmies = 7 end

	local owned = ownedGenerals(world.meInstance)
	local pool = usableGenerals(armyCommon, world, owned)

	local facts = { armies = #armies, pool = #pool, maxArmies = maxArmies, blocked = 0, locked = 0 }

	-- === Pass 1: ensure pasif (Defend / no target / replenish off) ===
	for _, army in ipairs(armies) do
		if isArmyBlocked(army.name) then
			facts.blocked += 1
		elseif isArmyFullLocked(army.name) then
			facts.locked += 1
			-- Locked: SKIP enforce juga (diam total). Tapi kalau mode berubah
			-- karena user klik manual, kita tetap enforce pada tick berikutnya
			-- via isArmyOperational check di Pass 2/3.
		elseif not isArmyOperational(army) then
			enforceGroupMode(army)
		end
	end

	-- === Pass 2: update full-lock state ===
	local capacityByArmy = {}
	for _, army in ipairs(armies) do
		local cap = army.folder:GetAttribute("MaxDivisions")
		if type(cap) ~= "number" or cap <= 0 then cap = 10 end
		capacityByArmy[army.name] = cap
	end
	updateArmyFullLock(world, armies, capacityByArmy)

	-- === Pass 3: assign jenderal yang dimiliki ke group kosong ===
	local assignedTo = {}
	local freeGroups = {}
	for _, army in ipairs(armies) do
		local general = army.folder:GetAttribute("general")
		if type(general) == "string" and general ~= "" then
			assignedTo[general] = army.name
			army.general = general
		else
			table.insert(freeGroups, army)
		end
	end

	local freeOwned = {}
	for _, general in ipairs(pool) do
		if general.owned and assignedTo[general.name] == nil then
			table.insert(freeOwned, general)
		end
	end

	facts.assignments = {}
	for index, army in ipairs(freeGroups) do
		local general = freeOwned[index]
		if general == nil then break end
		if not ArmyControl.blockedGenerals["assign:" .. general.name]
			and not isArmyBlocked(army.name)
			and not isArmyFullLocked(army.name) then
			ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
				remote = "TrySetArmyGroupGeneral",
				args = { army.name, general.name },
				key = "gen:assign:" .. army.name .. ":" .. general.name,
				reason = "pasang jenderal yang sudah dimiliki (arg = nama army)",
				tier = TIER,
				onResult = function(ok, results)
					local message = results and results[2]
					reportGeneralStatus(string.format(
						"pasang %s ke %s -> ok=%s msg=%s",
						general.name, army.name, tostring(ok), tostring(message)))
					if ok ~= true then
						ArmyControl.blockedGenerals["assign:" .. general.name] = true
					end
				end,
			})
			assignedTo[general.name] = army.name
			army.general = general.name
			facts.assignments[army.name] = general.name
		end
	end

	-- === Pass 4: buat group baru kalau perlu ===
	local desired = math.min(#pool, maxArmies)
	if #armies < desired then
		local now = os.clock()
		if (now - ArmyControl.lastAddArmyClock) > GENERAL_ADD_ARMY_INTERVAL then
			ArmyControl.lastAddArmyClock = now
			ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
				remote = "AddArmy",
				args = {},
				key = "gen:addArmy:" .. tostring(#armies),
				reason = string.format("butuh group ke-%d untuk jenderal (pool=%d)", #armies + 1, #pool),
				tier = TIER,
			})
			facts.creating = #armies + 1
		end
	end

	-- === Pass 5: unlock jenderal baru kalau perlu ===
	local pick = nil
	for _, general in ipairs(pool) do
		if not general.owned and not ArmyControl.blockedGenerals[general.name] then
			pick = general
			break
		end
	end
	if pick ~= nil then
		local lastTry = ArmyControl.unlockAttempts[pick.name]
		if lastTry == nil or (os.clock() - lastTry) > GENERAL_UNLOCK_RETRY_SECONDS then
			ArmyControl.unlockAttempts[pick.name] = os.clock()
			ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
				remote = "TryUnlockGeneral",
				args = { pick.name },
				key = "gen:unlock:" .. pick.name,
				reason = string.format("buka jenderal %s (biaya %s PP)", pick.name, tostring(pick.cost)),
				tier = TIER,
				onResult = function(ok, results)
					local message = results and results[2]
					ArmyControl.lastGeneralReply = { name = pick.name, ok = ok, message = message }
					reportGeneralStatus(string.format(
						"unlock %s -> ok=%s msg=%s",
						pick.name, tostring(ok), tostring(message)))
					if ok ~= true and type(message) == "string" and message ~= "Not Enough PP" then
						ArmyControl.blockedGenerals[pick.name] = true
					end
				end,
			})
			facts.unlocking = pick.name
			facts.cost = pick.cost
		end
	end

	-- === Pass 6: kurasi (hanya group yang unlocked & unblocked) ===
	local groupsWithGeneral = {}
	for _, army in ipairs(armies) do
		if army.general ~= nil
			and not isArmyBlocked(army.name)
			and not isArmyFullLocked(army.name) then
			table.insert(groupsWithGeneral, army)
		end
	end

	facts.groupsWithGeneral = #groupsWithGeneral
	if #groupsWithGeneral > 0 then
		local claimed = {}
		for _, army in ipairs(groupsWithGeneral) do
			curateGeneralGroup(world, army, armyCommon, army.general, claimed, false)
		end
		facts.curation = {}
		for _, army in ipairs(groupsWithGeneral) do
			facts.curation[army.name] = curateGeneralGroup(world, army, armyCommon, army.general, claimed, true)
		end
	end

	return facts
end

local function releaseArmies(world, keepNames)
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.army ~= nil
			and (keepNames == nil or keepNames[unit.army] ~= true)
			and not isArmyBlocked(unit.army)
			and not isArmyFullLocked(unit.army)
			and unit.state ~= "Retreating" and unit.state ~= "Dead" then
			ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
				remote = "AddTroopFromTileToArmy",
				args = { MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].instance, unit.army },
				key = "release:" .. tostring(unit.id) .. ":" .. unit.army,
				reason = "per-tile control needs troops outside server army groups",
				tier = TIER,
			})
		end
	end

	for _, army in ipairs(CountryState.armies(world.meInstance)) do
		if army.replenishTroops == true
			and (keepNames == nil or keepNames[army.name] ~= true)
			and not isArmyBlocked(army.name)
			and not isArmyFullLocked(army.name) then
			ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
				remote = "ToggleArmyReplenish",
				args = { army.folder },
				key = "replenishOff:" .. army.name,
				reason = "new spawns must not join a server army group",
				tier = TIER,
			})
		end
	end
end

local function setPriorityCity(world, analysis)
	local army = Resolver.requireChild(world.meInstance, "Army")
	local current = army:GetAttribute("priorityCity")
	local sources = {}
	if analysis ~= nil then
		for tileName in pairs(analysis.contact) do
			table.insert(sources, tileName)
		end
		for _, gap in ipairs(analysis.gaps) do
			table.insert(sources, gap.tile)
		end
	end
	table.sort(sources)
	if #sources == 0 then
		return { changed = false, reason = "no front tiles" }
	end
	local field = Pathing.distanceField(sources, function(tileName)
		return world.tileOwner[tileName] == world.me
	end)
	local best = nil
	for _, city in ipairs(CountryState.cities(world.meInstance)) do
		local tileName = city.tileName
		local distance = field.distance[tileName]
		local usable = distance ~= nil
			and world.tileOwner[tileName] == world.me
			and world.unitsByTile[tileName] == nil
			and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, tileName) == 0
			and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, tileName)
		if usable and (best == nil or distance < best.distance or (distance == best.distance and city.name < best.name)) then
			best = { name = city.name, tile = tileName, distance = distance }
		end
	end
	if best == nil then
		return { changed = false, reason = "no free, safe, supplied city" }
	end
	if best.name == current then
		return { changed = false, city = best.name }
	end
	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "SetPriorityCity",
		args = { best.name },
		key = "priority:" .. best.name,
		reason = string.format("closest free safe city to the front (%.1f studs)", best.distance),
		tier = TIER,
	})
	return { changed = true, from = current, to = best.name, distance = best.distance }
end

function ArmyControl.run(world, analysis)
	local facts = {}
	if Settings.get("generals.enabled") then
		local generalFacts = maintainGeneralGroups(world)
		facts.generalArmy = generalFacts
		if Settings.get("armies.releaseInherited") then
			local keepNames = {}
			for name in pairs(generalFacts.curation or {}) do
				keepNames[name] = true
			end
			releaseArmies(world, keepNames)
			facts.released = true
		end
	elseif Settings.get("armies.releaseInherited") then
		releaseArmies(world)
		facts.released = true
	end
	facts.priorityCity = setPriorityCity(world, analysis)
	Logger.debug("Features.Orders.ArmyControl", "run", facts)
end

function ArmyControl.reset(reason)
	table.clear(ArmyControl.pendingByArmy)
	table.clear(ArmyControl.pendingQueue)
	table.clear(ArmyControl.blockedArmies)
	table.clear(ArmyControl.errorCountByArmy)
	table.clear(ArmyControl.unlockAttempts)
	table.clear(ArmyControl.blockedGenerals)
	table.clear(ArmyControl.fullLockedArmies)
	table.clear(ArmyControl.lastFullLockLog)
	ArmyControl.lastAddReply = nil
	reportGeneralStatus("state reset: " .. tostring(reason))
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Orders.ArmyControl", ArmyControl)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Orders/OrderExecutor"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Orders/OrderExecutor

	The single door for troop orders (DESIGN Â§6).

	=== BUGFIX-C4 (2026-10-01) ===
	OrderExecutor.acceptanceLatencies is now bounded via Stats.pushBounded.
]]

local OrderExecutor = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Stats
local Stats = require("Utility/Stats")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Units
local Units = require("Game/Units")

---@module Game.Remotes
local Remotes = require("Game/Remotes")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

---@module Features.Front.Plan
local Plan = require("Features/Front/Plan")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatFormulas
local CombatFormulas = require("Features/Combat/CombatFormulas")

-- Constants.
local UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK = math.huge
local EMPTY_PATH_JSON = "[]"
local PATH_AFTER_TARGET_START = 3
local TARGET_INDEX = 2
-- [BUGFIX-C4]
local MAX_ACCEPTANCE_SAMPLES = 256

local httpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

OrderExecutor.pending = {}
OrderExecutor.committed = {}
OrderExecutor.userControlled = {}
OrderExecutor.stats = { planned = 0, unchanged = 0, sent = 0, dryRun = 0, rejectedAtSend = 0, accepted = 0, notAccepted = 0, halts = 0, userOverrides = 0, haltAfterKill = 0 }
OrderExecutor.acceptanceLatencies = {}
OrderExecutor.lastDryRun = {}
OrderExecutor.lastSent = {}
OrderExecutor.leftArmy = {}
OrderExecutor.ourCountryName = nil
OrderExecutor.attackTargets = {}
OrderExecutor.attackersOnTile = {}

local function pathJson(path, fromIndex)
	local slice = {}

	for index = fromIndex, #path do
		table.insert(slice, path[index])
	end

	return httpService:JSONEncode(slice)
end

local function currentDestination(unit)
	if unit.currentPath ~= nil and unit.currentPath ~= "[]" then
		local decodeOk, decoded = pcall(httpService.JSONDecode, httpService, unit.currentPath)

		if not decodeOk then
			return error(string.format("OrderExecutor: currentPath of unit %s is not JSON: %s", tostring(unit.id), tostring(decoded)))
		end

		if type(decoded) == "table" and #decoded > 0 then
			return tostring(decoded[#decoded])
		end
	end

	return unit.targetTile
end

function OrderExecutor.commitments()
	return OrderExecutor.committed
end

function OrderExecutor.repeatedTooSoon(order)
	local last = OrderExecutor.lastSent[order.unitId]
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()

	return last ~= nil
		and period ~= nil
		and last.signature == table.concat(order.path, ",")
		and os.clock() - last.clock < period
end

function OrderExecutor.pruneCommitments(world)
	for unitId, commitment in pairs(OrderExecutor.committed) do
		local unit = world.unitsById[unitId]
		local finished = unit == nil
			or unit.state == "Retreating"
			or (unit.state == "Idle" and unit.targetTile == nil and (unit.currentPath == nil or unit.currentPath == EMPTY_PATH_JSON) and OrderExecutor.pending[unitId] == nil)

		if finished then
			OrderExecutor.committed[unitId] = nil
			Logger.trace("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "commitmentEnded", { unit = unitId, purpose = commitment.purpose, destination = commitment.destination })
		end
	end
end

function OrderExecutor.blockedUnits()
	local blocked = {}

	for unitId in pairs(OrderExecutor.userControlled) do
		blocked[unitId] = true
	end

	return blocked
end

local function validateAtSend(order)
	-- [FIX-B2] pakai indeks id, bukan memindai seluruh Units.records per order.
	local record = Units.byId[order.unitId]
	if record == nil or record.superseded then
		return false, "unit no longer exists"
	end

	if record.tile ~= order.fromTile then
		return false, string.format("unit moved to %s since planning", record.tile)
	end

	local state = record.model:GetAttribute("state")
	if state == "Defending" or state == "Retreating" or state == "Dead" then
		return false, "unit is " .. tostring(state)
	end

	for index = TARGET_INDEX, #order.path - 1 do
		if Units.liveUnitOn(order.path[index]) ~= nil then
			return false, string.format("intermediate tile %s is occupied", order.path[index])
		end
	end

	return true, nil
end

function OrderExecutor.execute(world, plan, canSend)
	OrderExecutor.ourCountryName = world.me

	local vacatedBy = {}
	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		vacatedBy[order.fromTile] = order
	end

	local needed = {}
	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		for index = TARGET_INDEX, #order.path do
			local vacating = vacatedBy[order.path[index]]
			if vacating ~= nil and vacating ~= order then
				needed[vacating] = true
			end
		end
	end

	local ordered = table.clone(plan.orders)
	table.sort(ordered, function(first, second)
		local firstNeeded = needed[first] == true
		local secondNeeded = needed[second] == true

		if firstNeeded ~= secondNeeded then
			return firstNeeded
		end

		local firstTier = Plan.TIER_RANK[first.tier] or UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK
		local secondTier = Plan.TIER_RANK[second.tier] or UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK

		if firstTier ~= secondTier then
			return firstTier < secondTier
		end

		return first.unitId < second.unitId
	end)

	local summary = { sent = 0, unchanged = 0, dryRun = 0, rejected = 0, halts = 0 }

	for _, order in ipairs(ordered) do
		OrderExecutor.stats.planned += 1

		local unit = world.unitsById[order.unitId]
		local destinationNow = unit and currentDestination(unit) or nil
		local attackingTarget = unit ~= nil and unit.state == "Attacking" and unit.targetTile == order.destination
		local commitment = OrderExecutor.committed[order.unitId]
		local alreadyOrdered = commitment ~= nil and commitment.destination == order.destination

		if destinationNow == order.destination or attackingTarget or alreadyOrdered then
			OrderExecutor.stats.unchanged += 1
			summary.unchanged += 1
		else
			local valid, why = validateAtSend(order)

			if not valid then
				OrderExecutor.stats.rejectedAtSend += 1
				summary.rejected += 1
				Logger.debug("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "rejectedAtSend", { unit = order.unitId, purpose = order.purpose, path = order.path, reason = why })
			elseif canSend and OrderExecutor.repeatedTooSoon(order) then
				summary.repeatSuppressed = (summary.repeatSuppressed or 0) + 1
				Logger.debug("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "repeatSuppressed", { unit = order.unitId, path = order.path, purpose = order.purpose })
			elseif not canSend then
				OrderExecutor.stats.dryRun += 1
				summary.dryRun += 1

				local signature = order.purpose .. "|" .. table.concat(order.path, ",")
				if OrderExecutor.lastDryRun[order.unitId] == signature then
					summary.dryRunRepeats = (summary.dryRunRepeats or 0) + 1
				else
					OrderExecutor.lastDryRun[order.unitId] = signature
					Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "dryRunOrder", {
						unit = order.unitId,
						troop = order.unitTroop,
						path = order.path,
						purpose = order.purpose,
						tier = order.tier,
						reason = order.reason,
						facts = order.facts,
					})
				end
			else
				-- [ONE-HOP] At war, send only the first hop. The Scheduler's
				-- ourUnitIdle trigger fires when the unit arrives, which
				-- schedules a combat cycle immediately; the planner then
				-- re-assigns the next hop from the unit's new position.
				-- At peace, send the full path (no per-hop re-plan needed).
				local pathToSend = order.path
				local atWar = next(world.enemies) ~= nil
				if atWar and #order.path > 2 then
					pathToSend = { order.path[1], order.path[2] }
				end

				local instances = {}
				for _, tileName in ipairs(pathToSend) do
					table.insert(instances, MapGraph.tiles[tileName].instance)
				end

				Remotes.send("MoveTroop", instances)

				OrderExecutor.userControlled[order.unitId] = nil
				OrderExecutor.leftArmy[order.unitId] = nil

				local pendingEntry = {
					order = order,
					sentClock = os.clock(),
					expectedTarget = pathToSend[TARGET_INDEX],
					expectedPathFromTarget = pathJson(pathToSend, TARGET_INDEX),
					expectedPathAfterTarget = nil,
				}

				if #pathToSend >= PATH_AFTER_TARGET_START then
					pendingEntry.expectedPathAfterTarget = pathJson(pathToSend, PATH_AFTER_TARGET_START)
				end

				OrderExecutor.pending[order.unitId] = pendingEntry
				OrderExecutor.committed[order.unitId] = {
					path = order.path,
					purpose = order.purpose,
					tier = order.tier,
					destination = order.destination,
					facts = order.facts,
					sentClock = os.clock(),
				}
				OrderExecutor.stats.sent += 1
				summary.sent += 1
				OrderExecutor.lastSent[order.unitId] = { signature = table.concat(order.path, ","), clock = os.clock() }

				Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "sent", {
					unit = order.unitId,
					troop = order.unitTroop,
					path = order.path,
					purpose = order.purpose,
					tier = order.tier,
					reason = order.reason,
					facts = order.facts,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	for _, halt in ipairs(plan.halts) do
		summary.halts += 1
		OrderExecutor.stats.halts += 1

		if canSend then
			Remotes.send("HaltTroop", MapGraph.tiles[halt.fromTile].instance)
			OrderExecutor.committed[halt.unitId] = nil
			Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "haltSent", { unit = halt.unitId, tile = halt.fromTile, reason = halt.reason, facts = halt.facts })
		else
			Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "dryRunHalt", { unit = halt.unitId, tile = halt.fromTile, reason = halt.reason, facts = halt.facts })
		end
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "executed", { cycle = world.cycleLabel, summary = summary, canSend = canSend })

	return summary
end

local function haltAttackersOnTile(tile, enemyRecord, reason)
	local attackers = OrderExecutor.attackersOnTile[tile]
	if attackers == nil then return 0 end
	OrderExecutor.attackersOnTile[tile] = nil

	Plan.markCleared(tile, 4.0)

	local halted = 0
	for unitId, ourRecord in pairs(attackers) do
		if ourRecord ~= nil and ourRecord.model ~= nil and ourRecord.model.Parent ~= nil then
			local tileInstance = MapGraph.tiles[ourRecord.tile].instance
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				Remotes.send("HaltTroop", tileInstance)
			end)
			if ok then
				OrderExecutor.stats.haltAfterKill = (OrderExecutor.stats.haltAfterKill or 0) + 1
				OrderExecutor.attackTargets[unitId] = nil
				OrderExecutor.committed[unitId] = nil
				halted = halted + 1
				print(string.format("[CTW_KillHalt] unit=%d tile=%s enemy=%s(%s) halted",
					unitId, tile, enemyRecord and enemyRecord.troopName or "?", reason))
			else
				print(string.format("[CTW_KillHalt] HaltTroop FAILED unit=%d tile=%s: %s",
					unitId, tile, tostring(err)))
			end
		end
	end
	return halted
end

local function onUnitChanged(record, attributeName, value)
	if attributeName ~= "targetTile" and attributeName ~= "currentPath" and attributeName ~= "state" and attributeName ~= "army" then
		return
	end

	local pendingEntry = OrderExecutor.pending[record.id]

	if pendingEntry ~= nil then
		local accepted = (attributeName == "targetTile" and value == pendingEntry.expectedTarget)
			or (attributeName == "currentPath" and (value == pendingEntry.expectedPathFromTarget or (pendingEntry.expectedPathAfterTarget ~= nil and value == pendingEntry.expectedPathAfterTarget)))

		if accepted then
			local latency = os.clock() - pendingEntry.sentClock
			OrderExecutor.pending[record.id] = nil
			OrderExecutor.stats.accepted += 1
			-- [BUGFIX-C4] bounded
			Stats.pushBounded(OrderExecutor.acceptanceLatencies, latency, MAX_ACCEPTANCE_SAMPLES)

			Logger.debug("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "accepted", {
				unit = record.id,
				purpose = pendingEntry.order.purpose,
				latencySeconds = latency,
				by = attributeName,
			})
		end

		if attributeName == "targetTile" then
			return
		end
	end

	if record.owner ~= OrderExecutor.ourCountryName then
		if attributeName == "state" and (value == "Retreating" or value == "Dead") then
			haltAttackersOnTile(record.tile, record, tostring(value))
		end
		return
	end

	if attributeName == "state" then
		if value == "Attacking" then
			local target = record.model:GetAttribute("targetTile")
			if target ~= nil then
				OrderExecutor.attackTargets[record.id] = target
				local set = OrderExecutor.attackersOnTile[target]
				if set == nil then
					set = {}
					OrderExecutor.attackersOnTile[target] = set
				end
				set[record.id] = record
			end
		elseif OrderExecutor.attackTargets[record.id] ~= nil then
			local target = OrderExecutor.attackTargets[record.id]
			OrderExecutor.attackTargets[record.id] = nil
			local set = OrderExecutor.attackersOnTile[target]
			if set ~= nil then
				set[record.id] = nil
				if next(set) == nil then
					OrderExecutor.attackersOnTile[target] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if attributeName == "army" then
		if value == nil then
			OrderExecutor.leftArmy[record.id] = true
		end

		return
	end

	local state = record.model:GetAttribute("state")
	local targetTile = record.model:GetAttribute("targetTile")
	local remaining = record.model:GetAttribute("currentPath")

	if state == "Idle" and targetTile == nil and (remaining == nil or remaining == EMPTY_PATH_JSON) then
		if OrderExecutor.userControlled[record.id] then
			OrderExecutor.userControlled[record.id] = nil
			Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "userOverrideEnded", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile })
		end

		OrderExecutor.leftArmy[record.id] = nil

		return
	end

	if attributeName ~= "targetTile" or value == nil then
		return
	end

	local health = record.model:GetAttribute("health")
	if state == "Retreating" or (type(health) == "number" and health <= CombatFormulas.DEFENDER_HEALTH_FLOOR) then
		return
	end

	local committed = OrderExecutor.committed[record.id]
	if committed ~= nil and table.find(committed.path, value) ~= nil then
		return
	end

	if record.model:GetAttribute("army") ~= nil then
		return
	end

	if OrderExecutor.leftArmy[record.id] then
		Logger.debug("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "armyOrderLeftover", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, targetTile = value })
		return
	end

	if not OrderExecutor.userControlled[record.id] then
		OrderExecutor.userControlled[record.id] = true
		OrderExecutor.stats.userOverrides += 1

		Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "userOverride", {
			unit = record.id,
			tile = record.tile,
			targetTile = value,
			reason = "our unit started a path the executor did not send; left to the user until it is Idle",
		})
	end
end

local function checkTimeouts()
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()

	if period == nil then
		return
	end

	local now = os.clock()

	for unitId, pendingEntry in pairs(OrderExecutor.pending) do
		if now - pendingEntry.sentClock > period then
			-- [FIX-B2] pakai indeks id, bukan memindai seluruh Units.records per timeout.
			local record = Units.byId[unitId]
			if record ~= nil and record.superseded then record = nil end

			local stateNow = record and record.model:GetAttribute("state") or nil

			if stateNow == "Defending" then
				pendingEntry.sentClock = now

				if not pendingEntry.deferredLogged then
					pendingEntry.deferredLogged = true
					Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "orderWaitsForDefence", {
						unit = unitId,
						path = pendingEntry.order.path,
						purpose = pendingEntry.order.purpose,
					})
				end
			else
				OrderExecutor.pending[unitId] = nil
				OrderExecutor.committed[unitId] = nil
				OrderExecutor.stats.notAccepted += 1

				Logger.error("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "orderNotAccepted", {
					unit = unitId,
					path = pendingEntry.order.path,
					purpose = pendingEntry.order.purpose,
					waitedSeconds = now - pendingEntry.sentClock,
					boundSeconds = period,
					unitTile = record and record.tile or nil,
					unitState = stateNow or "gone",
					unitTarget = record and record.model:GetAttribute("targetTile") or nil,
				})
			end
		end
	end
end

function OrderExecutor.attach(maid)
	maid:give(Units.unitChanged:connect(onUnitChanged))
	maid:give(Tick.dayTick:connect(checkTimeouts))
	maid:give(Units.unitRemoved:connect(function(record)
		if record.superseded then return end

		if record.owner ~= OrderExecutor.ourCountryName then
			haltAttackersOnTile(record.tile, record, "destroyed")
		end

		OrderExecutor.pending[record.id] = nil
		OrderExecutor.committed[record.id] = nil
		OrderExecutor.userControlled[record.id] = nil
		OrderExecutor.leftArmy[record.id] = nil
		OrderExecutor.lastSent[record.id] = nil
		local target = OrderExecutor.attackTargets[record.id]
		if target ~= nil then
			OrderExecutor.attackTargets[record.id] = nil
			local set = OrderExecutor.attackersOnTile[target]
			if set ~= nil then
				set[record.id] = nil
				if next(set) == nil then
					OrderExecutor.attackersOnTile[target] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end))

	Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "attached", {})
end

function OrderExecutor.reset(reason)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.pending)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.committed)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.userControlled)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.lastDryRun)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.lastSent)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.leftArmy)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.attackTargets)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.attackersOnTile)
	Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "reset", { reason = reason })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", OrderExecutor)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Review/PlanOverlay"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Review/PlanOverlay

	Draws the last cycle's planned orders over the map (ui.showPlans): one thin bar per hop of every
	order, coloured by what the order is for, plus a marker on every locked tile.

	=== BUGFIX-C2 (2026-10-01) ===
	Settings.get("ui.showPlans") is now backed by a real setting defined in SettingsCatalog.
]]

local PlanOverlay = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

-- Constants (display only).
local FOLDER_NAME = "CTW_WarAI_Plan"
local BAR_THICKNESS = 0.6
local HEIGHT_ABOVE_TILE = 1.5
local LOCK_MARKER_SIZE = 1.2
local BAR_TRANSPARENCY = 0.25
local COLOR_ATTACK = Color3.fromRGB(235, 64, 52)
local COLOR_DEFENCE = Color3.fromRGB(52, 152, 235)
local COLOR_MOVE = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
local COLOR_LOCK = Color3.fromRGB(245, 212, 66)
local ATTACK_TIERS = { A0 = true, A1 = true, A2 = true, A3 = true, A4 = true, A5 = true }
local DEFENCE_TIERS = { T0 = true, T1 = true, T2 = true, T3 = true }
local HALF = 0.5
local FIRST_HOP_INDEX = 2

PlanOverlay.folder = nil

local function folderFor(maid)
	if PlanOverlay.folder == nil or PlanOverlay.folder.Parent == nil then
		local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
		folder.Name = FOLDER_NAME
		folder.Parent = workspace
		PlanOverlay.folder = folder
		maid:give(folder)
	end

	return PlanOverlay.folder
end

local function addPart(parent, size, cframe, color)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CastShadow = false
	part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
	part.Transparency = BAR_TRANSPARENCY
	part.Color = color
	part.Size = size
	part.CFrame = cframe
	part.Parent = parent
end

function PlanOverlay.draw(plan, maid)
	local folder = folderFor(maid)
	folder:ClearAllChildren()

	if not Settings.get("ui.showPlans") then
		return
	end

	local lift = Vector3.new(0, HEIGHT_ABOVE_TILE, 0)
	local bars = 0

	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		local color = COLOR_MOVE
		if ATTACK_TIERS[order.tier] then
			color = COLOR_ATTACK
		elseif DEFENCE_TIERS[order.tier] then
			color = COLOR_DEFENCE
		end

		for index = FIRST_HOP_INDEX, #order.path do
			local fromPosition = MapGraph.tiles[order.path[index - 1]].position + lift
			local toPosition = MapGraph.tiles[order.path[index]].position + lift
			local length = (toPosition - fromPosition).Magnitude
			local centre = fromPosition:Lerp(toPosition, HALF)

			addPart(folder, Vector3.new(BAR_THICKNESS, BAR_THICKNESS, length), CFrame.lookAt(centre, toPosition), color)
			bars += 1
		end
	end

	for tileName in pairs(plan.locks) do
		local position = MapGraph.tiles[tileName].position + lift
		addPart(folder, Vector3.new(LOCK_MARKER_SIZE, LOCK_MARKER_SIZE, LOCK_MARKER_SIZE), CFrame.new(position), COLOR_LOCK)
	end

	Logger.trace("Features.Review.PlanOverlay", "drawn", { bars = bars })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Review.PlanOverlay", PlanOverlay)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Review/Scorecard"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Review/Scorecard

	Measures the AI instead of claiming it (DESIGN Â§11).

	=== BUGFIX-C4 (2026-10-01) ===
	Scorecard.resolved.breakErrors is now bounded via Stats.pushBounded.
	Scorecard.interruptions remains a fixed small dictionary keyed by outcome.
]]

local Scorecard = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Stats
local Stats = require("Utility/Stats")

---@module Game.Units
local Units = require("Game/Units")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Orders.OrderExecutor
local OrderExecutor = require("Features/Orders/OrderExecutor")

-- Constants.
local ATTACK_PURPOSES = { capitalStrike = true, kill = true, surroundKill = true, relief = true, attack = true, cutOffAttack = true, pincerCut = true, hunt = true }
local BEATEN_STATES = { Retreating = true, Dead = true }
-- [BUGFIX-C4]
local MAX_BREAK_ERROR_SAMPLES = 256

-- State.
Scorecard.predictions = {}
Scorecard.resolved = { won = 0, lost = 0, predictedWonAndWon = 0, breakErrors = {} }
Scorecard.lastHit = {}
Scorecard.attackTarget = {}
Scorecard.interruptions = {}

function Scorecard.recordLaunches(world, plan, canSend)
	if not canSend then
		return
	end

	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		if ATTACK_PURPOSES[order.purpose] and order.isAttack then
			local defender = world.unitsByTile[order.destination]

			if defender ~= nil and Scorecard.predictions[defender.id] == nil then
				Scorecard.predictions[defender.id] = {
					targetTile = order.destination,
					defenderTroop = defender.troopName,
					predictedRatio = order.facts and order.facts.ratio or nil,
					predictedBreakSeconds = order.facts and order.facts.breakSeconds or nil,
					startServerTime = world.serverTime,
					purpose = order.purpose,
				}
			end
		end
	end
end

local function onUnitChanged(record, attributeName, value)
	if attributeName == "state" then
		local prediction = Scorecard.predictions[record.id]

		if prediction ~= nil and BEATEN_STATES[value] then
			Scorecard.predictions[record.id] = nil
			Scorecard.resolved.won += 1

			local actual = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - prediction.startServerTime

			if prediction.predictedBreakSeconds ~= nil then
				-- [BUGFIX-C4] bounded
				Stats.pushBounded(Scorecard.resolved.breakErrors, actual - prediction.predictedBreakSeconds, MAX_BREAK_ERROR_SAMPLES)
				Scorecard.resolved.predictedWonAndWon += 1
				CombatModel.recordBreakOutcome(prediction.predictedBreakSeconds, actual)
			end

			Logger.info("Features.Review.Scorecard", "attackWon", {
				target = prediction.targetTile,
				troop = prediction.defenderTroop,
				outcome = value,
				actualSeconds = actual,
				predictedSeconds = prediction.predictedBreakSeconds,
				predictedRatio = prediction.predictedRatio,
				purpose = prediction.purpose,
			})
		end

		if record.owner == Units.ourCountryName then
			if value == "Attacking" then
				Scorecard.attackTarget[record] = record.model:GetAttribute("targetTile")
			else
				local target = Scorecard.attackTarget[record]
				Scorecard.attackTarget[record] = nil
				Scorecard.lastHit[record] = nil

				if target ~= nil then
					local stillStanding = false
					for defenderId, pending in pairs(Scorecard.predictions) do
						if pending.targetTile == target then
							stillStanding = true
						end
					end

					if stillStanding then
						Scorecard.interruptions[value] = (Scorecard.interruptions[value] or 0) + 1

						Logger.info("Features.Review.Scorecard", "attackInterrupted", {
							unit = record.id,
							troop = record.troopName,
							target = target,
							newState = value,
							health = record.model:GetAttribute("health"),
						})
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return
	end

	if attributeName == "targetTile" and record.owner == Units.ourCountryName and value ~= nil and record.model:GetAttribute("state") == "Attacking" then
		Scorecard.attackTarget[record] = value
		return
	end

	if attributeName == "health" and record.owner == Units.ourCountryName and record.model:GetAttribute("state") == "Attacking" then
		local target = record.model:GetAttribute("targetTile")
		local now = os.clock()
		local last = Scorecard.lastHit[record]

		if last ~= nil and last.target == target and type(value) == "number" and value < last.health then
			-- [BUGFIX-C4] use the bounded helper on CombatModel
			CombatModel.pushHitInterval(now - last.clock)
		end

		Scorecard.lastHit[record] = { target = target, clock = now, health = value }
	end
end

function Scorecard.resolveStopped(world)
	for defenderId, prediction in pairs(Scorecard.predictions) do
		local defender = world.unitsById[defenderId]
		local stillAttacked = false

		if defender ~= nil then
			for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
				local commitment = OrderExecutor.committed[unit.id]
				local attacking = unit.state == "Attacking" and unit.targetTile == defender.tile
				local ordered = commitment ~= nil and commitment.destination == defender.tile

				if attacking or ordered then
					stillAttacked = true
				end
			end
		end

		if defender == nil then
			Scorecard.predictions[defenderId] = nil
			Scorecard.resolved.won += 1
		elseif not stillAttacked then
			Scorecard.predictions[defenderId] = nil
			Scorecard.resolved.lost += 1

			Logger.warn("Features.Review.Scorecard", "attackLost", {
				target = prediction.targetTile,
				troop = prediction.defenderTroop,
				defenderHealth = defender.health,
				predictedRatio = prediction.predictedRatio,
				purpose = prediction.purpose,
			})
		end
	end
end

function Scorecard.summary()
	local medianError = Stats.median(Scorecard.resolved.breakErrors)

	local open = 0
	for _ in pairs(Scorecard.predictions) do
		open += 1
	end

	local interval, intervalSource = CombatModel.hitIntervalSeconds()

	return {
		interruptions = Scorecard.interruptions,
		attacksWon = Scorecard.resolved.won,
		attacksLost = Scorecard.resolved.lost,
		open = open,
		medianBreakErrorSeconds = medianError,
		hitIntervalSeconds = interval,
		hitIntervalSource = intervalSource,
	}
end

function Scorecard.attach(maid)
	maid:give(Units.unitChanged:connect(onUnitChanged))
	maid:give(Units.unitRemoved:connect(function(record)
		Scorecard.lastHit[record] = nil
		Scorecard.attackTarget[record] = nil
	end))

	Logger.info("Features.Review.Scorecard", "attached", {})
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Review.Scorecard", Scorecard)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Supply/SupplyModel"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Supply/SupplyModel

	Predicts the server's supply pass (user rule U1). REV-1 exposes sourcesOf and reachSet for the
	pincer analyzer.
]]

local SupplyModel = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

-- Constants.
local CUT_AFTER_TICKS = 2

Formula.define({
	id = "supply.cutAfterTicks",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "recorded supply census: noSupply true after two consecutive missed stamps (rec1 543/543, rec2 1,037/1,037; war-and-loop.md Â§3.13)",
	expression = "2",
	unit = "day ticks",
	inputs = {},
	compute = function()
		return CUT_AFTER_TICKS
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = {}, expected = 2, tolerance = 0 },
	},
})

function SupplyModel.sourcesOf(world, countryName)
	local sources = {}
	local capitalTile = Ownership.capitalTile(countryName)

	if capitalTile ~= nil and world.tileOwner[capitalTile] == countryName then
		table.insert(sources, capitalTile)
	end

	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)

	-- [FIX-A2] FindFirstChild + guard: negara tanpa folder Cities tidak boleh membatalkan siklus.
	local cities = country ~= nil and country:FindFirstChild("Cities") or nil
	if cities ~= nil then
		for _, cityFolder in ipairs(cities:GetChildren()) do
			if cityFolder:GetAttribute("isPort") == true then
				local tileName = cityFolder:GetAttribute("tileName")

				if type(tileName) == "string" and world.tileOwner[tileName] == countryName and tileName ~= capitalTile then
					table.insert(sources, tileName)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return sources
end

function SupplyModel.reachSet(world, countryName, sources, removedSet)
	if type(removedSet) == "string" then
		removedSet = { [removedSet] = true }
	end

	local reached = {}
	local queue = {}
	local head = 1

	for _, source in ipairs(sources) do
		if not (removedSet and removedSet[source]) and not reached[source] then
			reached[source] = true
			table.insert(queue, source)
		end
	end

	while head <= #queue do
		local current = queue[head]
		head += 1

		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
			if not reached[neighbourName]
				and not (removedSet and removedSet[neighbourName])
				and world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == countryName then
				reached[neighbourName] = true
				table.insert(queue, neighbourName)
			end
		end
	end

	return reached
end

local function reach(world, countryName, sources, removedTile)
	return SupplyModel.reachSet(world, countryName, sources, removedTile)
end

function SupplyModel.suppliedTiles(world, countryName)
	local cache = world.supplyCache
	local cached = cache[countryName]

	if cached ~= nil then
		return cached.reached
	end

	local sources = SupplyModel.sourcesOf(world, countryName)
	local reached = reach(world, countryName, sources, nil)

	cache[countryName] = { reached = reached, sources = sources }

	return reached
end

function SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, tileName)
	local owner = world.tileOwner[tileName]

	if owner == nil then
		return false
	end

	return SupplyModel.suppliedTiles(world, owner)[tileName] == true
end

function SupplyModel.unitsCutByLosing(world, countryName, tileName)
	world._unitsCutCache = world._unitsCutCache or {}
	local memoKey = countryName .. "|" .. tileName
	local memo = world._unitsCutCache[memoKey]
	if memo ~= nil then return memo end

	SupplyModel.suppliedTiles(world, countryName)

	local cached = world.supplyCache[countryName]
	local without = reach(world, countryName, cached.sources, tileName)
	local cut = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.unitsOf[countryName] or {}) do
		local unitTile = unit.tile
		if unitTile ~= tileName and cached.reached[unitTile] and not without[unitTile] then
			table.insert(cut, unit)
		end
	end

	world._unitsCutCache[memoKey] = cut
	return cut
end

function SupplyModel.logOurPrediction(world)
	local reached = SupplyModel.suppliedTiles(world, world.me)
	local predictedCut = {}
	local liveFlagged = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if world.tileOwner[unit.tile] == world.me and not reached[unit.tile] then
			table.insert(predictedCut, unit.tile)
		end

		if unit.noSupply then
			table.insert(liveFlagged, unit.tile)
		end
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Supply.SupplyModel", "ourSupply", {
		sources = world.supplyCache[world.me].sources,
		predictedCutTiles = predictedCut,
		liveNoSupplyTiles = liveFlagged,
	})
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Supply.SupplyModel", SupplyModel)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Arrows"] = [=[
local Arrows = {}
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

local PIVOT_TOLERANCE_STUDS = 0.02
local ARROWS_FOLDER = "Arrows"
local MIDPOINT_DIVISOR = 2

function Arrows.endTime(arrowName, fromName, toName)
	local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(ARROWS_FOLDER)
	local fromRecord = MapGraph.tiles[fromName]
	local toRecord = MapGraph.tiles[toName]

	if folder == nil or fromRecord == nil or toRecord == nil then
		return nil
	end

	local midpoint = (fromRecord.position + toRecord.position) / MIDPOINT_DIVISOR

	for _, arrow in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if arrow.Name == arrowName and arrow:IsA("PVInstance") then
			local pivot = arrow:GetPivot().Position
			local offsetX = pivot.X - midpoint.X
			local offsetZ = pivot.Z - midpoint.Z

			if math.sqrt(offsetX * offsetX + offsetZ * offsetZ) <= PIVOT_TOLERANCE_STUDS then
				local endTime = arrow:GetAttribute("endTime")

				if type(endTime) == "number" then
					return endTime
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Arrows", Arrows)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/CountryState"] = [=[
local CountryState = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

local ARMY_CLASS_NAMES_SOURCE = "Army.Classes children"

local function number(instance, attributeName)
	return Resolver.requireAttribute(instance, attributeName, "number")
end

function CountryState.treasury(country)
	return {
		money = number(country, "money"), manpower = number(country, "manpower"),
		politicalPower = number(country, "politicalPower"), divisions = number(country, "divisions"),
		factories = number(country, "factories"), tileCount = number(country, "tileCount"),
	}
end

function CountryState.productionItems(country)
	local folder = Resolver.requireChild(country, "ProductionItems")
	local items = {}
	for _, item in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		table.insert(items, {
			name = item.Name, instance = item,
			stock = number(item, "stock"), production = number(item, "production"),
			consumption = Resolver.optionalAttribute(item, "consumption", "number", "written from the first healing draw on"),
			factories = number(item, "factories"), factoriesValue = number(item, "factoriesValue"),
			outputCoef = number(item, "outputCoef"),
			maxStock = Resolver.optionalAttribute(item, "maxStock", "number", "Consumer Goods has no stock cap"),
		})
	end
	table.sort(items, function(first, second) return first.name < second.name end)
	return items
end

function CountryState.techs(country)
	local folder = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Techs")
	local techs = {}
	for _, tech in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if tech:IsA("ObjectValue") then
			techs[tech.Name] = {
				name = tech.Name, instance = tech,
				price = number(tech, "price"), duration = number(tech, "duration"),
				progress = number(tech, "progress"),
				purchased = Resolver.optionalAttribute(tech, "purchased", "boolean", "absent until the tech is bought") == true,
			}
		end
	end
	return techs
end

function CountryState.researchSlots(country)
	local folder = Resolver.requireChild(country, "ResearchSlots")
	local slots = {}
	for _, slot in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if slot:IsA("ObjectValue") then
			slots[slot.Name] = slot.Value and slot.Value.Name or false
		end
	end
	return slots
end

function CountryState.focuses(country)
	local government = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Government")
	local folder = Resolver.requireChild(government, "Focuses")
	local currentText = Resolver.requireAttribute(folder, "currentFocuses", "string")
	local focuses = {}
	for _, focus in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if focus:IsA("ObjectValue") then
			local requiredText = Resolver.requireAttribute(focus, "required", "string")
			focuses[focus.Name] = {
				name = focus.Name, instance = focus,
				duration = number(focus, "duration"), ppCost = number(focus, "ppCost"),
				progress = number(focus, "progress"),
				required = Serializer.decode(requiredText, focus:GetFullName() .. ".required"),
				purchased = Resolver.optionalAttribute(focus, "purchased", "boolean", "absent until the focus is started") == true,
				effectLines = Resolver.optionalAttribute(focus, "effectLines", "string", "only some focus trees replicate effect text"),
			}
		end
	end
	return {
		current = Serializer.decode(currentText, folder:GetFullName() .. ".currentFocuses"),
		focuses = focuses,
		focusSpeed = Resolver.requireAttribute(government, "focusSpeed", "number"),
	}
end

function CountryState.armyClasses(country)
	local army = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Army")
	local classesFolder = Resolver.requireChild(army, "Classes")
	local classes = {}
	for _, classFolder in ipairs(classesFolder:GetChildren()) do
		classes[classFolder.Name] = {
			name = classFolder.Name, folder = classFolder,
			count = number(classFolder, "count"), target = number(classFolder, "target"),
			startedTrainingTime = number(classFolder, "startedTrainingTime"),
			trainDuration = number(classFolder, "trainDuration"),
			baseCost = number(classFolder, "baseCost"), costIncrease = number(classFolder, "costIncrease"),
			hasManpower = Resolver.requireAttribute(classFolder, "hasManpower", "boolean"),
		}
	end
	Logger.trace("Game.CountryState", "armyClassesRead", { country = country.Name, source = ARMY_CLASS_NAMES_SOURCE })
	return classes
end

function CountryState.armies(country)
	local army = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Army")
	local armiesFolder = Resolver.requireChild(army, "Armies")
	local armies = {}
	for _, armyFolder in ipairs(armiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
		table.insert(armies, {
			name = armyFolder.Name, folder = armyFolder,
			command = Resolver.optionalAttribute(armyFolder, "command", "string", "set by ChangeArmyOrder"),
			replenishTroops = Resolver.optionalAttribute(armyFolder, "replenishTroops", "boolean", "new armies start with true"),
			divisions = Resolver.optionalAttribute(armyFolder, "divisions", "number", "new armies start with 0"),
			targetCountries = Resolver.optionalAttribute(armyFolder, "targetCountries", "string", "human-created armies have none"),
		})
	end
	table.sort(armies, function(first, second) return first.name < second.name end)
	return armies
end

function CountryState.cities(country)
	local citiesFolder = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Cities")
	local cities = {}
	for _, cityFolder in ipairs(citiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
		table.insert(cities, {
			name = cityFolder.Name, folder = cityFolder,
			tileName = Resolver.requireAttribute(cityFolder, "tileName", "string"),
			isCapital = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "isCapital", "boolean", "set on capitals only") == true,
			isPort = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "isPort", "boolean", "set on ports only") == true,
			isAirfield = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "isAirfield", "boolean", "set on airfield cities only") == true,
			population = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "population", "number", "static city data"),
			factories = number(cityFolder, "factories"), maxFactories = number(cityFolder, "maxFactories"),
			defenses = number(cityFolder, "defenses"), maxDefenses = number(cityFolder, "maxDefenses"),
			factoryBuildQueue = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "factoryBuildQueue", "number", "set while factories are queued") or 0,
			defenseBuildQueue = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "defenseBuildQueue", "number", "set while defenses are queued") or 0,
		})
	end
	table.sort(cities, function(first, second) return first.name < second.name end)
	return cities
end

function CountryState.folderNumber(country, folderName, attributeName)
	return number(Resolver.requireChild(country, folderName), attributeName)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.CountryState", CountryState)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/GameModules"] = [=[
local GameModules = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

local MODULE_SPECS = {
	MapData = { path = "MapData", members = { "colorToCountry", "countryToCities", "tileNameToCity", "GetCityFolderFromTile" } },
	Utils = { path = "Common/Utils", members = { "secondsPerDay", "GetDistanceBetweenTiles" } },
	ArmyCommon = { path = "Common/ArmyCommon", members = { "terrainModifiers", "GetRiverModifierBetweenTiles", "troopNameToInfo", "upgrades", "divisionSize", "GetTroopBaseStatsRTF", "generals", "FindGeneralByName", "GetGeneralBuffsRTF", "IsGeneralUnlockable", "IsOwningGeneral" } },
	TroopCommon = { path = "Common/TroopCommon", members = { "CalculateStatsAgainstTroop", "GetTroopClass", "GetTroopSpeed", "GetTroopConsumption" } },
	DiplomacyCommon = { path = "Common/DiplomacyCommon", members = { "AreCountriesFriendly", "CanAttackFromCountryTile", "GetCaptureCountryFromOriginTile", "CompareIdeologies" } },
	TileGraph = { path = "Common/TileGraph", members = { "GetAsync" } },
	ResearchCommon = { path = "Common/ResearchCommon", members = { "techs" } },
	GameLoopCommon = { path = "Common/GameLoopCommon", members = { "relationToCoef", "GetWinTileLimit", "CalculateCountryWinScore" } },
}

local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
GameModules.loaded = {}

function GameModules.loadModule(moduleName)
	local spec = MODULE_SPECS[moduleName]
	if spec == nil then
		return error(string.format("GameModules.loadModule: '%s' is not a known game module", tostring(moduleName)))
	end
	local moduleScript = Resolver.requireDescendant(replicatedStorage, spec.path)
	if not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return error(string.format("GameModules.loadModule: '%s' is a %s, not a ModuleScript", moduleScript:GetFullName(), moduleScript.ClassName))
	end
	local moduleValue = require(moduleScript)
	if type(moduleValue) ~= "table" then
		return error(string.format("GameModules.loadModule: '%s' returned %s, expected a table", moduleScript:GetFullName(), typeof(moduleValue)))
	end
	local missingMembers = {}
	for _, memberName in ipairs(spec.members) do
		if moduleValue[memberName] == nil then table.insert(missingMembers, memberName) end
	end
	if #missingMembers > 0 then
		return error(string.format("GameModules.loadModule: '%s' lacks %s (game update?)", moduleScript:GetFullName(), table.concat(missingMembers, ", ")))
	end
	GameModules.loaded[moduleName] = moduleValue
	Logger.info("Game.GameModules", "loaded", {
		module = moduleName, instance = moduleScript:GetFullName(), checkedMembers = spec.members,
	})
	return moduleValue
end

function GameModules.loadAll()
	for moduleName in pairs(MODULE_SPECS) do
		GameModules.loadModule(moduleName)
	end
end

Tracer.instrument("Game.GameModules", GameModules)

function GameModules.get(moduleName)
	local moduleValue = GameModules.loaded[moduleName]
	if moduleValue == nil then
		return error(string.format("GameModules.get: '%s' is not loaded (call GameModules.loadAll first)", tostring(moduleName)))
	end
	return moduleValue
end

return GameModules
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/MapGraph"] = [=[
local MapGraph = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")
