local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

local SEA_NAME_FRAGMENT = "Sea"

MapGraph.mapModel = nil
MapGraph.tiles = {}
MapGraph.tileOrder = {}
MapGraph.landCount = 0
MapGraph.seaCount = 0
MapGraph.edgeLengths = {}
MapGraph.edgeRivers = {}
MapGraph.tileColorChanged = Signal.new("Game.MapGraph.tileColorChanged")
MapGraph.tileAttributeChanged = Signal.new("Game.MapGraph.tileAttributeChanged")

local function isSeaName(tileName)
	return string.find(tileName, SEA_NAME_FRAGMENT, 1, true) ~= nil
end

function MapGraph.build(mapModel, maid)
	local TileGraph = GameModules.get("TileGraph")
	local MapData = GameModules.get("MapData")
	local orderedTiles, neighboursByTile = TileGraph.GetAsync(mapModel, nil)
	if orderedTiles == nil or neighboursByTile == nil then
		return error(string.format("MapGraph.build: TileGraph.GetAsync returned nil for '%s'", mapModel:GetFullName()))
	end
	table.clear(MapGraph.tiles)
	table.clear(MapGraph.tileOrder)
	table.clear(MapGraph.edgeLengths)
	table.clear(MapGraph.edgeRivers)
	MapGraph.landCount = 0
	MapGraph.seaCount = 0
	MapGraph.mapModel = mapModel
	local cityTiles = 0
	local coastalTiles = 0
	for _, tileInstance in ipairs(orderedTiles) do
		local tileName = tileInstance.Name
		local isSea = isSeaName(tileName)
		local cityEntry = MapData.tileNameToCity[tileName]
		local record = {
			name = tileName, instance = tileInstance, isSea = isSea,
			position = tileInstance.Position,
			terrain = tileInstance:GetAttribute("terrain"),
			airRegion = tileInstance:GetAttribute("airRegion"),
			cityName = cityEntry and cityEntry.name or nil,
			neighbours = {}, landNeighbours = {}, seaNeighbours = {}, isCoastal = false,
		}
		MapGraph.tiles[tileName] = record
		table.insert(MapGraph.tileOrder, tileName)
		if isSea then MapGraph.seaCount += 1 else MapGraph.landCount += 1 end
		if record.cityName ~= nil then cityTiles += 1 end
	end
	for _, tileInstance in ipairs(orderedTiles) do
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileInstance.Name]
		for _, neighbourInstance in ipairs(neighboursByTile[tileInstance]) do
			local neighbourName = neighbourInstance.Name
			local neighbourRecord = MapGraph.tiles[neighbourName]
			if neighbourRecord == nil then
				return error(string.format("MapGraph.build: neighbour '%s' of '%s' is not a map tile", neighbourName, record.name))
			end
			table.insert(record.neighbours, neighbourName)
			if neighbourRecord.isSea then
				table.insert(record.seaNeighbours, neighbourName)
			else
				table.insert(record.landNeighbours, neighbourName)
			end
		end
		if not record.isSea and #record.seaNeighbours > 0 then
			record.isCoastal = true
			coastalTiles += 1
		end
	end
	for _, tileName in ipairs(MapGraph.tileOrder) do
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]
		if not record.isSea then
			local tileInstance = record.instance
			maid:give(tileInstance:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Color"):Connect(function()
				MapGraph.tileColorChanged:fire(tileName)
			end))
			maid:give(tileInstance.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(attributeName)
				-- [FIX-D2] terrain/airRegion dibaca sekali saat build; refresh supaya tidak basi.
				if attributeName == "terrain" then
					record.terrain = tileInstance:GetAttribute("terrain")
				elseif attributeName == "airRegion" then
					record.airRegion = tileInstance:GetAttribute("airRegion")
				end
				MapGraph.tileAttributeChanged:fire(tileName, attributeName, tileInstance:GetAttribute(attributeName))
			end))
		end
	end
	Logger.info("Game.MapGraph", "built", {
		map = mapModel:GetFullName(), tiles = #MapGraph.tileOrder,
		land = MapGraph.landCount, sea = MapGraph.seaCount,
		cityTiles = cityTiles, coastalTiles = coastalTiles,
	})
end

function MapGraph.distance(fromName, toName) return MapGraph.edgeLength(fromName, toName) end
function MapGraph.riverModifier(fromName, toName) return MapGraph.edgeRiver(fromName, toName) end

function MapGraph.clear(reason)
	Logger.info("Game.MapGraph", "cleared", { reason = reason, tiles = #MapGraph.tileOrder })
	table.clear(MapGraph.tiles)
	table.clear(MapGraph.tileOrder)
	table.clear(MapGraph.edgeLengths)
	table.clear(MapGraph.edgeRivers)
	MapGraph.mapModel = nil
	MapGraph.landCount = 0
	MapGraph.seaCount = 0
end

Tracer.instrument("Game.MapGraph", MapGraph)

function MapGraph.tile(tileName)
	local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]
	if record == nil then
		return error(string.format("MapGraph.tile: unknown tile '%s'", tostring(tileName)))
	end
	return record
end

function MapGraph.isLand(tileName)
	local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]
	return record ~= nil and not record.isSea
end

function MapGraph.edgeLength(fromName, toName)
	local fromLengths = MapGraph.edgeLengths[fromName]
	if fromLengths == nil then
		fromLengths = {}
		MapGraph.edgeLengths[fromName] = fromLengths
	end
	local length = fromLengths[toName]
	if length == nil then
		local fromRecord = MapGraph.tiles[fromName]
		local toRecord = MapGraph.tiles[toName]
		if fromRecord == nil or toRecord == nil then
			return error(string.format("MapGraph.edgeLength: unknown tile in '%s' -> '%s'", tostring(fromName), tostring(toName)))
		end
		length = GameModules.get("Utils").GetDistanceBetweenTiles(fromRecord.instance, toRecord.instance)
		fromLengths[toName] = length
	end
	return length
end

function MapGraph.edgeRiver(fromName, toName)
	local fromRivers = MapGraph.edgeRivers[fromName]
	if fromRivers == nil then
		fromRivers = {}
		MapGraph.edgeRivers[fromName] = fromRivers
	end
	local modifier = fromRivers[toName]
	if modifier == nil then
		local fromRecord = MapGraph.tiles[fromName]
		local toRecord = MapGraph.tiles[toName]
		if fromRecord == nil or toRecord == nil then
			return error(string.format("MapGraph.edgeRiver: unknown tile in '%s' -> '%s'", tostring(fromName), tostring(toName)))
		end
		modifier = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").GetRiverModifierBetweenTiles(fromRecord.instance, toRecord.instance)
		fromRivers[toName] = modifier
	end
	return modifier
end

return MapGraph
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Ownership"] = [=[
local Ownership = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")
local Session = require("Game/Session")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

Ownership.countries = {}
Ownership.colorToCountry = {}
Ownership.tileOwner = {}
Ownership.countryTiles = {}
Ownership.countryTileCounts = {}
Ownership.unknownColors = {}
Ownership.countMismatchStreaks = {}
Ownership.ownerChanged = Signal.new("Game.Ownership.ownerChanged")

local function addTile(countryName, tileName)
	if countryName == nil then return end
	local tiles = Ownership.countryTiles[countryName]
	if tiles == nil then
		tiles = {}
		Ownership.countryTiles[countryName] = tiles
		Ownership.countryTileCounts[countryName] = 0
	end
	if not tiles[tileName] then
		tiles[tileName] = true
		Ownership.countryTileCounts[countryName] += 1
	end
end

local function removeTile(countryName, tileName)
	if countryName == nil then return end
	local tiles = Ownership.countryTiles[countryName]
	if tiles ~= nil and tiles[tileName] then
		tiles[tileName] = nil
		Ownership.countryTileCounts[countryName] -= 1
	end
end

local function countryForColor(color, where)
	local hex = string.lower(color:ToHex())
	local countryName = Ownership.colorToCountry[hex]
	if countryName == nil and not Ownership.unknownColors[hex] then
		Ownership.unknownColors[hex] = true
		Logger.warn("Game.Ownership", "unownedColor", { hex = hex, where = where, meaning = "no country has this colour" })
	end
	return countryName
end

local function refreshTile(tileName)
	local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]
	if record == nil or record.isSea then return end
	local previous = Ownership.tileOwner[tileName]
	local current = countryForColor(record.instance.Color, tileName)
	if previous == current then return end
	removeTile(previous, tileName)
	addTile(current, tileName)
	Ownership.tileOwner[tileName] = current
	Ownership.ownerChanged:fire(tileName, previous, current)
end

local function aliveByName(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.countries[countryName]
	return country ~= nil and country.Parent ~= nil and country:GetAttribute("isDead") ~= true
end

function Ownership.rebuildCountries()
	table.clear(Ownership.countries)
	table.clear(Ownership.colorToCountry)
	local MapData = GameModules.get("MapData")
	local disagreements = {}
	for _, country in ipairs(Session.countriesFolder():GetChildren()) do
		if country:IsA("ObjectValue") then
			local color = Resolver.requireAttribute(country, "color", "Color3")
			local hex = string.lower(color:ToHex())
			if Ownership.colorToCountry[hex] ~= nil then
				return error(string.format("Ownership.rebuildCountries: '%s' and '%s' share colour %s", country.Name, Ownership.colorToCountry[hex], hex))
			end
			Ownership.countries[country.Name] = country
			Ownership.colorToCountry[hex] = country.Name
			local staticName = MapData.colorToCountry[hex] or MapData.colorToCountry[string.upper(hex)]
			if staticName ~= nil and staticName ~= country.Name then
				table.insert(disagreements, { hex = hex, live = country.Name, mapData = staticName })
			end
		end
	end
	if #disagreements > 0 then
		Logger.warn("Game.Ownership", "colorTableDisagrees", { disagreements = disagreements })
	end
	Logger.info("Game.Ownership", "countriesRebuilt", { countries = #Session.countriesFolder():GetChildren() })
end

function Ownership.rebuildTiles()
	table.clear(Ownership.tileOwner)
	table.clear(Ownership.countryTiles)
	table.clear(Ownership.countryTileCounts)
	for _, tileName in ipairs(MapGraph.tileOrder) do
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]
		if not record.isSea then
			local owner = countryForColor(record.instance.Color, tileName)
			Ownership.tileOwner[tileName] = owner
			addTile(owner, tileName)
		end
	end
	Logger.info("Game.Ownership", "tilesRebuilt", { countries = Ownership.countryTileCounts })
end

function Ownership.attach(maid)
	Ownership.rebuildCountries()
	Ownership.rebuildTiles()
	maid:give(MapGraph.tileColorChanged:connect(refreshTile))
	maid:give(Session.countriesFolder().ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		Logger.info("Game.Ownership", "countryAdded", { country = child.Name })
		Ownership.rebuildCountries()
		-- [FIX-H9] warna yang tadinya unknown kini punya pemilik, jadi tile harus dipetakan ulang.
		Ownership.rebuildTiles()
	end))
end

function Ownership.isAI(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)
	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("Ownership.isAI: unknown country '%s'", tostring(countryName)))
	end
	return country.Value == nil
end

function Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, folderName)
	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)
	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("Ownership.diplomacyNames: unknown country '%s'", tostring(countryName)))
	end
	local diplomacy = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Diplomacy")
	local folder = Resolver.requireChild(diplomacy, folderName)
	local names = {}
	for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if aliveByName(child.Name) then names[child.Name] = true end
	end
	return names
end

function Ownership.enemiesOf(countryName)
	return Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Enemies")
end

function Ownership.friendsOf(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)
	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("Ownership.friendsOf: unknown country '%s'", tostring(countryName)))
	end
	local friends = { [countryName] = true }
	for name in pairs(Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Allies")) do friends[name] = true end
	for name in pairs(Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Puppets")) do friends[name] = true end
	local overlordName = country:GetAttribute("puppetOf")
	if overlordName ~= nil then
		if aliveByName(overlordName) then friends[overlordName] = true end
		for otherName, other in pairs(Ownership.countries) do
			if other:GetAttribute("puppetOf") == overlordName and aliveByName(otherName) then
				friends[otherName] = true
			end
		end
	end
	return friends
end

function Ownership.movementCountries(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)
	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("Ownership.movementCountries: unknown country '%s'", tostring(countryName)))
	end
	local allowed = { [countryName] = true }
	for name in pairs(Ownership.enemiesOf(countryName)) do allowed[name] = true end
	for name in pairs(Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Allies")) do allowed[name] = true end
	for name in pairs(Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Puppets")) do allowed[name] = true end
	local overlordName = country:GetAttribute("puppetOf")
	if overlordName ~= nil and Ownership.country(overlordName) ~= nil then
		allowed[overlordName] = true
		for name in pairs(Ownership.diplomacyNames(overlordName, "Puppets")) do allowed[name] = true end
	end
	return allowed
end

function Ownership.capitalTile(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)
	if country == nil then return nil, "unknown country" end
	local capitalName = country:GetAttribute("capital")
	if type(capitalName) ~= "string" then return nil, "no capital attribute" end
	-- [FIX-A1] FindFirstChild + guard: requireChild melempar error untuk negara tanpa folder Cities,
	-- padahal kontrak fungsi ini mengembalikan nil + alasan.
	local cities = country:FindFirstChild("Cities")
	if cities == nil then
		return nil, string.format("%s has no Cities folder", tostring(countryName))
	end
	local cityFolder = cities:FindFirstChild(capitalName)
	if cityFolder ~= nil then
		return Resolver.requireAttribute(cityFolder, "tileName", "string"), nil
	end
	local staticCities = GameModules.get("MapData").countryToCities[countryName]
	local staticCity = staticCities and staticCities[capitalName]
	if staticCity == nil or staticCity.tile == nil then
		return nil, string.format("capital '%s' is neither in Cities nor in MapData.countryToCities", capitalName)
	end
	return tostring(staticCity.tile), nil
end

function Ownership.verifyCounts()
	local persisted = 0
	for countryName, country in pairs(Ownership.countries) do
		if country.Parent ~= nil and country:GetAttribute("isDead") ~= true then
			local reported = country:GetAttribute("tileCount")
			local counted = Ownership.countryTileCounts[countryName] or 0
			if type(reported) == "number" and reported ~= counted then
				local streak = (Ownership.countMismatchStreaks[countryName] or 0) + 1
				Ownership.countMismatchStreaks[countryName] = streak
				if streak > 1 then
					persisted += 1
					Logger.error("Game.Ownership", "tileCountMismatch", {
						country = countryName, reported = reported, counted = counted, consecutiveChecks = streak,
					})
				end
			else
				Ownership.countMismatchStreaks[countryName] = nil
			end
		end
	end
	return persisted
end

Tracer.instrument("Game.Ownership", Ownership)

function Ownership.country(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.countries[countryName]
	if country ~= nil and country.Parent == nil then return nil end
	return country
end

function Ownership.isAlive(countryName)
	return aliveByName(countryName)
end

function Ownership.troopOwner(model)
	local color = model:GetAttribute("color")
	if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return nil end
	return countryForColor(color, model:GetFullName())
end

---[FIX-B3b] Negara yang berbatasan DARAT dengan kita, beserta border tile kita.
---Dihitung SATU pass atas tile kita lalu di-memo di tabel world (per siklus), menggantikan
---pemindaian tileOwner per-negara yang dipakai WarAdvisor.sharesBorder dan BorderPrep.scan.
---@param world table
---@return table name -> set of our border tiles
function Ownership.borderNeighbours(world)
	if world._borderNeighbours ~= nil then
		return world._borderNeighbours
	end

	local me = world.me
	if me == nil then
		-- Tanpa negara sendiri, tile tak bertuan (owner nil) akan salah dianggap milik kita.
		world._borderNeighbours = {}
		return world._borderNeighbours
	end

	local map = {}

	for tileName, owner in pairs(Ownership.tileOwner) do
		if owner == me then
			local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]
			if record ~= nil then
				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(record.landNeighbours) do
					local neighbourOwner = Ownership.tileOwner[neighbourName]
					if neighbourOwner ~= nil and neighbourOwner ~= me then
						local set = map[neighbourOwner]
						if set == nil then
							set = {}
							map[neighbourOwner] = set
						end
						set[tileName] = true
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	world._borderNeighbours = map
	return map
end

return Ownership
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Remotes"] = [=[
local Remotes = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

local REGISTRY = {
	MoveTroop = { path = "Player/MoveTroop", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(path: {tile BasePart}) source first", reply = "unit attributes (targetTile/currentPath) 0.21-0.23 s later" },
	HaltTroop = { path = "Army/HaltTroop", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(tile BasePart) of the own troop", reply = "unit attributes" },
	AddTroopFromTileToArmy = { path = "Army/AddTroopFromTileToArmy", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(tile BasePart, armyName string) toggles membership", reply = "OnClientEvent(inst, 'On'|'Off'|error)" },
	ToggleArmyReplenish = { path = "Army/ToggleArmyReplenish", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(army Folder)", reply = "army replenishTroops flips" },
	SetPriorityCity = { path = "Army/SetPriorityCity", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(cityName string)", reply = "Army.priorityCity" },
	TryIncreaseTarget = { path = "Army/TryIncreaseTarget", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(classFolder)", reply = "(ok, msg)" },
	TryBuyManpower = { path = "Army/TryBuyManpower", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "()", reply = "(ok, msg)" },
	SetProductionPercentage = { path = "Production/SetProductionPercentage", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(item ObjectValue, value 0..1)", reply = "item factoriesValue/factories" },
	PurchaseFactory = { path = "Cities/PurchaseFactory", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(city Folder)", reply = "OnClientEvent(ok, msg); failure opens a Robux prompt" },
	PurchaseDefense = { path = "Cities/PurchaseDefense", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(city Folder)", reply = "OnClientEvent(ok, msg); failure opens a Robux prompt" },
	TryPurchaseTech = { path = "Research/TryPurchaseTech", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(tech ObjectValue)", reply = "truthy when bought" },
	StartResearch = { path = "Research/StartResearch", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(tech ObjectValue) into a free slot", reply = "slot Value; both slots full fires OnAllSlotsOccupied" },
	TryStartFocus = { path = "Government/TryStartFocus", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(focus ObjectValue)", reply = "(ok, err)" },
	TryPurchaseShip = { path = "Economy/TryPurchaseShip", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(cityName string, shipType string)", reply = "falsy on failure" },
	TryExitPortWithFleet = { path = "Cities/Port/TryExitPortWithFleet", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(city Folder, {ship ObjectValue})", reply = "(true, seaTile) | (false, message)" },
	MoveFleet = { path = "Fleet/MoveFleet", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(path: {tile}) fleet tile first, sea tiles, optional land goal", reply = "fleet attributes" },
	LoadDivisionOnShip = { path = "Cities/Port/LoadDivisionOnShip", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(shipObjectValue from Port.DockedShips)", reply = "ship.loadedDivisions updates" },
	TryDeployPlanes = { path = "Planes/TryDeployPlanes", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(city Folder, AirMap region Part, planeData {6 keys})", reply = "(ok, _)" },
	TryMovePlanes = { path = "Planes/TryMovePlanes", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(source city Folder, destination city Folder, planeData)", reply = "(ok, err)" },
	TryRetreat = { path = "Planes/TryRetreat", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(AirMap region Part)", reply = "truthy on success" },
	-- [AIR-r5] Remotes baru untuk fitur airfield management.
	TryUpgradeAirfield = { path = "Planes/TryUpgradeAirfield", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(city Folder)", reply = "(ok, err)" },
	SetAirfieldPriority = { path = "Planes/SetAirfieldPriority", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(airfield Instance, enabled boolean)", reply = "airfield priority attribute" },
	TryUpgradeShipyard = { path = "Economy/TryUpgradeShipyard", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(city Folder)", reply = "(ok, err, nextTechName?) with err in 'No Money' | 'Research Required' | 'Max Level'" },
	-- [FIX-A2] Terverifikasi dari ctw_dump.txt (REMOTES) + ctw_ui_source.txt 12611/12647: arg = country ObjectValue.
	TryJustifyWar = { path = "Diplomacy/TryJustifyWar", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(country ObjectValue)", reply = "(ok, msg)" },
	TryDeclareWar = { path = "Diplomacy/TryDeclareWar", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(country ObjectValue)", reply = "(ok, msg)" },
	-- [FIX-FORT] Terverifikasi dari ctw_dump.txt (REMOTES) + ctw_ui_source.txt L21007.
	PurchaseFort = { path = "TileProjects/PurchaseFort", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(tile BasePart)", reply = "OnClientEvent(ok, err); tech Fortifications I/II/III gate level 1-3" },
	-- [FIX-GEN] Terverifikasi dari ctw_dump.txt + ctw_ui_source.txt L13612/L13631/L13347/L13142/L5754.
	TryUnlockGeneral = { path = "Army/TryUnlockGeneral", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(generalName string)", reply = "(ok, msg); costs PP, some need a gamepass" },
	TrySetArmyGroupGeneral = { path = "Army/TrySetArmyGroupGeneral", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(armyName string, generalName string; \"\" clears)", reply = "(ok, msg); msg == 'Not Enough PP' bila PP kurang" },
	ChangeArmyOrder = { path = "Army/ChangeArmyOrder", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(army Folder, \"Attack\"|\"Defend\")", reply = "army command attribute" },
	ChangeTargetCountries = { path = "Army/ChangeTargetCountries", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(army Folder, { countryName })", reply = "army targetCountries JSON" },
	AddArmy = { path = "Army/AddArmy", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "()", reply = "new army group folder (max 7)" },
}

Remotes.sendCounts = {}

function Remotes.entry(remoteName)
	local entry = REGISTRY[remoteName]
	if entry == nil then
		return error(string.format("Remotes: '%s' is not on the whitelist", tostring(remoteName)))
	end
	return entry
end

function Remotes.resolve(remoteName)
	local entry = Remotes.entry(remoteName)
	local remote = Resolver.requireDescendant(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"), entry.path)
	if remote.ClassName ~= entry.class then
		return error(string.format("Remotes: '%s' is a %s, the registry expects %s (game update?)", remote:GetFullName(), remote.ClassName, entry.class))
	end
	return remote
end

function Remotes.verifyAll()
	local verified = {}
	for remoteName in pairs(REGISTRY) do
		Remotes.resolve(remoteName)
		table.insert(verified, remoteName)
	end
	table.sort(verified)
	Logger.info("Game.Remotes", "verified", { remotes = verified })
end

function Remotes.send(remoteName, ...)
	local entry = Remotes.entry(remoteName)
	local remote = Remotes.resolve(remoteName)
	Remotes.sendCounts[remoteName] = (Remotes.sendCounts[remoteName] or 0) + 1
	if entry.class == "RemoteEvent" then
		remote:FireServer(...)
		return nil
	end
	return remote:InvokeServer(...)
end

function Remotes.replySignal(remoteName)
	local entry = Remotes.entry(remoteName)
	if entry.class ~= "RemoteEvent" then
		return error(string.format("Remotes.replySignal: '%s' is a %s and replies by return value", remoteName, entry.class))
	end
	return Remotes.resolve(remoteName).OnClientEvent
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Remotes", Remotes)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Session"] = [=[
local Session = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

local CTW_PLACE_ID = 14422394494
local MAP_MODEL_NAME = "Map"
local MAP_ATTRIBUTE = "map"

local players = game:GetService("Players")
local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local networkClient = game:GetService("NetworkClient")

Session.localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
Session.countryChanged = Signal.new("Game.Session.countryChanged")
Session.mapReplaced = Signal.new("Game.Session.mapReplaced")
Session.lastCountryName = nil

function Session.isCorrectPlace() return game.PlaceId == CTW_PLACE_ID end
function Session.countriesFolder() return Resolver.requireChild(replicatedStorage, "Countries") end

function Session.mapModel()
	local mapModel = Resolver.requireChild(workspace, MAP_MODEL_NAME)
	if not mapModel:IsA("Model") then
		return error(string.format("Session.mapModel: workspace.%s is a %s, expected Model", MAP_MODEL_NAME, mapModel.ClassName))
	end
	return mapModel
end

function Session.mapName()
	return Resolver.requireAttribute(workspace, MAP_ATTRIBUTE, "string")
end

function Session.replicatorPresent()
	return networkClient:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClientReplicator") ~= nil
end

function Session.ourCountry()
	local countryPointer = Resolver.optionalChild(Session.localPlayer, "Country", "the player has not joined a country yet")
	if countryPointer == nil then return nil, "LocalPlayer has no Country value" end
	local country = countryPointer.Value
	if country == nil then return nil, "no country selected (Country.Value is nil)" end
	if country.Parent ~= Session.countriesFolder() then
		return nil, string.format("Country.Value '%s' is not in ReplicatedStorage.Countries", country:GetFullName())
	end
	if country:GetAttribute("isDead") == true then
		return nil, string.format("%s is dead (annexed)", country.Name)
	end
	if country.Value ~= Session.localPlayer then
		return nil, string.format("%s is no longer controlled by the local player", country.Name)
	end
	local tileCount = Resolver.requireAttribute(country, "tileCount", "number")
	if tileCount <= 0 then
		return nil, string.format("%s owns no tiles (tileCount %s)", country.Name, tostring(tileCount))
	end
	return country, nil
end

function Session.attach(maid)
	local countryPointer = Resolver.optionalChild(Session.localPlayer, "Country", "the player has not joined a country yet")
	local function announceCountry(origin)
		local country, reason = Session.ourCountry()
		local countryName = country and country.Name or nil
		Logger.info("Game.Session", "country", {
			origin = origin, country = countryName,
			previous = Session.lastCountryName, reason = reason,
		})
		-- [FIX-H8] hanya pancarkan saat negara benar-benar berubah; Changed bisa terpicu berkali-kali.
		if countryName == Session.lastCountryName then return end
		Session.lastCountryName = countryName
		Session.countryChanged:fire(country, reason)
	end
	if countryPointer ~= nil then
		maid:give(countryPointer.Changed:Connect(function()
			announceCountry("LocalPlayer.Country.Changed")
		end))
	else
		maid:give(Session.localPlayer.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child.Name ~= "Country" then return end
			maid:give(child.Changed:Connect(function()
				announceCountry("LocalPlayer.Country.Changed")
			end))
			announceCountry("LocalPlayer.Country added")
		end))
	end
	maid:give(workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == MAP_MODEL_NAME and child:IsA("Model") then
			Logger.info("Game.Session", "mapModelAdded", { map = workspace:GetAttribute(MAP_ATTRIBUTE) })
			Session.mapReplaced:fire("workspace.Map added")
		end
	end))
	maid:give(workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal(MAP_ATTRIBUTE):Connect(function()
		Logger.info("Game.Session", "mapAttributeChanged", { map = workspace:GetAttribute(MAP_ATTRIBUTE) })
		Session.mapReplaced:fire("workspace map attribute changed")
	end))
	Logger.info("Game.Session", "attached", {
		placeId = game.PlaceId, correctPlace = Session.isCorrectPlace(),
		jobId = game.JobId, map = workspace:GetAttribute(MAP_ATTRIBUTE),
		replicator = Session.replicatorPresent(),
	})
	announceCountry("attach")
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Session", Session)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Tick"] = [=[
local Tick = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

local STAMP_ATTRIBUTE = "lastTimeSupplied"
local PASS_SEPARATION_FRACTION_OF_DAY = 0.5
local STALE_AFTER_PERIODS = 2
local MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR = 2

Tick.dayTick = Signal.new("Game.Tick.dayTick")
Tick.lastPassServerTime = nil
Tick.lastPassClock = nil
Tick.passCount = 0
Tick.intervals = {}
Tick.cachedPeriod = nil

Formula.define({
	id = "tick.periodSeconds",
	tier = "T2",
	source = "median interval between consecutive tick-pass stamp values measured this session (Game/Tick)",
	expression = "median(intervals)",
	unit = "s",
	inputs = { sortedIntervals = "array of s, ascending" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		local sorted = inputs.sortedIntervals
		local count = #sorted
		if count == 0 then return error("tick.periodSeconds: no interval measured yet") end
		local middle = (count + 1) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
		local lower = sorted[math.floor(middle)]
		local upper = sorted[math.ceil(middle)]
		return (lower + upper) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { sortedIntervals = { 3.056, 3.067, 3.084 } }, expected = 3.067, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { sortedIntervals = { 3.098, 3.117, 3.117, 3.163 } }, expected = 3.117, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

-- [FIX-H7] Window sampel dibatasi supaya memori & biaya tidak tumbuh tanpa batas.
local MAX_TICK_INTERVALS = 256

local function onStamp(tileName, stampValue)
	if type(stampValue) ~= "number" then return end
	local lastPass = Tick.lastPassServerTime
	local separation = GameModules.get("Utils").secondsPerDay * PASS_SEPARATION_FRACTION_OF_DAY
	if lastPass ~= nil and stampValue <= lastPass + separation then return end
	local nowClock = os.clock()
	if lastPass ~= nil then
		local interval = stampValue - lastPass
		-- [FIX-H7] batasi window, lalu urutkan salinan (bukan insert O(n) ke array tak terbatas).
		table.insert(Tick.intervals, interval)
		if #Tick.intervals > MAX_TICK_INTERVALS then
			table.remove(Tick.intervals, 1)
		end
		local sorted = table.clone(Tick.intervals)
		table.sort(sorted)
		Tick.cachedPeriod = Formula.evaluate("tick.periodSeconds", { sortedIntervals = sorted })
	end
	Tick.lastPassServerTime = stampValue
	Tick.lastPassClock = nowClock
	Tick.passCount += 1
	Logger.debug("Game.Tick", "dayTick", {
		pass = Tick.passCount, serverTime = stampValue, firstStampTile = tileName,
		arrivalLagSeconds = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - stampValue,
		intervalSeconds = lastPass and (stampValue - lastPass) or nil,
		measuredPeriodSeconds = Tick.cachedPeriod,
	})
	Tick.dayTick:fire({ pass = Tick.passCount, tickNumber = Tick.passCount, serverTime = stampValue })
end

function Tick.periodSeconds() return Tick.cachedPeriod end

function Tick.isFresh()
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()
	if period == nil or Tick.lastPassClock == nil then
		return false, string.format("tick period not measured yet (%d passes seen)", Tick.passCount)
	end
	local sinceLast = os.clock() - Tick.lastPassClock
	local bound = period * STALE_AFTER_PERIODS
	if sinceLast > bound then
		return false, string.format("no supply pass for %.2f s (bound %.2f s = %d periods of %.3f s)", sinceLast, bound, STALE_AFTER_PERIODS, period)
	end
	return true, string.format("last pass %.2f s ago", sinceLast)
end

function Tick.attach(mapGraph, maid)
	maid:give(mapGraph.tileAttributeChanged:connect(function(tileName, attributeName, value)
		if attributeName == STAMP_ATTRIBUTE then onStamp(tileName, value) end
	end))
	Logger.info("Game.Tick", "attached", { attribute = STAMP_ATTRIBUTE })
end

function Tick.reset(reason)
	Logger.info("Game.Tick", "reset", { reason = reason, passes = Tick.passCount, intervals = #Tick.intervals })
	Tick.lastPassServerTime = nil
	Tick.lastPassClock = nil
	Tick.passCount = 0
	Tick.cachedPeriod = nil
	table.clear(Tick.intervals)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Tick", Tick)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Units"] = [=[
local Units = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

local FLAG_CLEAR_UPPER_BOUND = 41.652
local DEAD_STATE = "Dead"
local RETREATING_STATE = "Retreating"
local DIGITS_PATTERN = "%d"
local LEVEL_PATTERN = "%d+"

Units.mapModel = nil
Units.records = {}
Units.byId = {}
Units.recordByModel = {}
Units.tileRecords = {}
Units.nextId = 0
Units.flagRecords = {}
Units.ourCountryName = nil
Units.unitAdded = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitAdded")
Units.unitMoved = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitMoved")
Units.unitRemoved = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitRemoved")
Units.unitChanged = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitChanged")

local function classAndLevel(troopName)
	local troopClass = string.gsub(troopName, DIGITS_PATTERN, "")
	local levelText = string.match(troopName, LEVEL_PATTERN)
	if levelText == nil then
		return error(string.format("Units: troop name '%s' carries no level digits", troopName))
	end
	return troopClass, tonumber(levelText)
end

local function tileList(tileName)
	local list = Units.tileRecords[tileName]
	if list == nil then list = {}; Units.tileRecords[tileName] = list end
	return list
end

local function detachFromTile(record)
	local list = Units.tileRecords[record.tile]
	if list == nil then return end
	local index = table.find(list, record)
	if index ~= nil then table.remove(list, index) end
	if #list == 0 then Units.tileRecords[record.tile] = nil end
end

local function isTroopModel(model)
	if not model:IsA("Model") then return false end
	local parent = model.Parent
	return parent ~= nil and parent.Parent == Units.mapModel and MapGraph.isLand(parent.Name)
end

local function updateFlagRecord(record, attributeName, value)
	if record.owner ~= Units.ourCountryName then return end
	local flag = Units.flagRecords[record.id]
	if attributeName == "state" and value == RETREATING_STATE and flag == nil then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = { sinceServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(), tile = record.tile }
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagRecorded", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, troop = record.troopName })
		return
	end
	if attributeName == "dieOnNextRetreat" and value == true and flag == nil then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = { sinceServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(), tile = record.tile }
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagRecorded", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, troop = record.troopName, origin = "live attribute" })
		return
	end
	if flag == nil then return end
	if attributeName == "dieOnNextRetreat" and value == false then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = nil
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagCleared", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, origin = "live attribute false" })
		return
	end
	if attributeName == "health" and type(value) == "number" and value >= FLAG_CLEAR_UPPER_BOUND then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = nil
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagCleared", {
			unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, health = value,
			bound = FLAG_CLEAR_UPPER_BOUND, origin = "health reached the recorded clear bound",
		})
	end
end

local function track(model, maid, origin)
	if Units.recordByModel[model] ~= nil then return end
	local tileName = model.Parent.Name
	local troopName = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "troopName", "string")
	local troopClass, level = classAndLevel(troopName)
	local owner = Ownership.troopOwner(model)
	local predecessor = nil
	for _, existing in ipairs(tileList(tileName)) do
		if existing.owner == owner and not existing.superseded then predecessor = existing end
	end
	local unitId
	if predecessor ~= nil then
		unitId = predecessor.id
		predecessor.superseded = true
	else
		Units.nextId += 1
		unitId = Units.nextId
	end
	local record = {
		id = unitId, model = model, tile = tileName, owner = owner,
		troopName = troopName, class = troopClass, level = level,
		superseded = false, addedServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
	}
	Units.records[record] = true
	Units.byId[unitId] = record
	Units.recordByModel[model] = record
	table.insert(tileList(tileName), record)
	local connections = {}
	table.insert(connections, model.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(attributeName)
		local value = model:GetAttribute(attributeName)
		updateFlagRecord(record, attributeName, value)
		Units.unitChanged:fire(record, attributeName, value)
	end))
	table.insert(connections, model:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
		local parent = model.Parent
		if parent ~= nil and parent.Parent == Units.mapModel and MapGraph.isLand(parent.Name) then
			local fromTile = record.tile
			detachFromTile(record)
			record.tile = parent.Name
			table.insert(tileList(parent.Name), record)
			Units.unitMoved:fire(record, fromTile, parent.Name)
			return
		end
		for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
		detachFromTile(record)
		Units.records[record] = nil
		-- [FIX-B2] hanya hapus kalau indeks masih menunjuk record ini (id bisa diwarisi penerus).
		if Units.byId[record.id] == record then Units.byId[record.id] = nil end
		Units.recordByModel[model] = nil
		local reason = parent == nil and "destroyed" or ("left the map for " .. parent:GetFullName())
		if not record.superseded then Units.flagRecords[record.id] = nil end
		Units.unitRemoved:fire(record, record.tile, reason)
	end))
	for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do maid:give(connection) end
	if predecessor ~= nil then
		Logger.debug("Game.Units", "unitReplaced", { unit = unitId, tile = tileName, from = predecessor.troopName, to = troopName })
	end
	Units.unitAdded:fire(record, origin)
end

function Units.reset(reason)
	local tracked = 0
	for _ in pairs(Units.records) do tracked += 1 end
	table.clear(Units.records)
	table.clear(Units.byId)
	table.clear(Units.recordByModel)
	table.clear(Units.tileRecords)
	table.clear(Units.flagRecords)
	Units.mapModel = nil
	Logger.info("Game.Units", "reset", { reason = reason, forgotten = tracked })
end

function Units.attach(mapModel, ourCountryName, maid)
	Units.mapModel = mapModel
	Units.ourCountryName = ourCountryName
	local tracked = 0
	for _, tileName in ipairs(MapGraph.tileOrder) do
		local tileRecord = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]
		if not tileRecord.isSea then
			for _, child in ipairs(tileRecord.instance:GetChildren()) do
				if isTroopModel(child) then
					track(child, maid, "initial scan")
					tracked += 1
				end
			end
		end
	end
	maid:give(mapModel.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
		if isTroopModel(descendant) then track(descendant, maid, "DescendantAdded") end
	end))
	Logger.info("Game.Units", "attached", { tracked = tracked, ourCountry = ourCountryName })
end

function Units.setOurCountry(countryName)
	if Units.ourCountryName ~= countryName then
		table.clear(Units.flagRecords)
	end
	Units.ourCountryName = countryName
end

function Units.unitOn(tileName) return Units.liveUnitOn(tileName) end

function Units.unitsOf(countryName)
	local result = {}
	for record in pairs(Units.records) do
		if record.owner == countryName and not record.superseded and record.model:GetAttribute("state") ~= DEAD_STATE then
			table.insert(result, record)
		end
	end
	table.sort(result, function(first, second) return first.id < second.id end)
	return result
end

function Units.isFlagged(record) return Units.flaggedNow(record) end
function Units.snapshot(record) return Units.snapshotNow(record) end

function Units.stats()
	local total = 0
	local byOwner = {}
	for record in pairs(Units.records) do
		if not record.superseded then
			total += 1
			byOwner[record.owner or "?"] = (byOwner[record.owner or "?"] or 0) + 1
		end
	end
	local flagged = 0
	for _ in pairs(Units.flagRecords) do flagged += 1 end
	return { total = total, byOwner = byOwner, ourFlagRecords = flagged }
end

Tracer.instrument("Game.Units", Units)

function Units.liveUnitOn(tileName)
	local list = Units.tileRecords[tileName]
	if list == nil then return nil end
	for index = #list, 1, -1 do
		local record = list[index]
		if not record.superseded and record.model.Parent ~= nil and record.model:GetAttribute("state") ~= DEAD_STATE then
			return record
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function Units.flaggedNow(record)
	return Units.flagRecords[record.id] ~= nil or record.model:GetAttribute("dieOnNextRetreat") == true
end

function Units.snapshotNow(record)
	local model = record.model
	local rawPath = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "currentPath", "string", "set only while an order has tiles left")
	local decodedDestination = nil
	if rawPath ~= nil and rawPath ~= "[]" and rawPath ~= "" then
		if record._decodedPathRaw == rawPath then
			decodedDestination = record._decodedPathDestination
		else
			local httpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
			local ok, decoded = pcall(httpService.JSONDecode, httpService, rawPath)
			if ok and type(decoded) == "table" and #decoded > 0 then
				decodedDestination = tostring(decoded[#decoded])
			end
			record._decodedPathRaw = rawPath
			record._decodedPathDestination = decodedDestination
		end
	end

	return {
		id = record.id, tile = record.tile, owner = record.owner,
		troopName = record.troopName, class = record.class, level = record.level, model = model,
		state = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "state", "string"),
		health = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "health", "number"),
		attack = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "attack", "number"),
		defense = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "defense", "number"),
		speed = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "speed", "number"),
		experience = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "experience", "number", "absent on a fresh Model before its first attack") or 0,
		targetTile = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "targetTile", "string", "set only while moving, waiting, attacking or retreating"),
		currentPath = rawPath,
		decodedDestination = decodedDestination,
		attackerCount = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "attackerCount", "number", "set only once the unit has been attacked"),
		noSupply = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "noSupply", "boolean", "written from the first supply pass on") == true,
		noMoney = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "noMoney", "boolean", "written from the first upkeep pass on") == true,
		liveFlag = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "dieOnNextRetreat", "boolean", "written from the first retreat on") == true,
		flagged = Units.flaggedNow(record),
		replenishConsumption = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "replenishConsumption", "number", "TroopCommon reads it with a default of 1"),
		replenishSuppliesName = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "replenishSuppliesName", "string", "set by the server with the base stats"),
		army = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "army", "string", "set only for members of an army group"),
	}
end

return Units
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["GUI/Rayfield"] = [=[
--[[
	GUI/Rayfield

	Memuat Rayfield Gen2 dari sirius.menu dan menyimpannya sebagai singleton,
	supaya Menu tidak perlu tahu URL-nya.
]]

local Rayfield = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

local RAYFIELD_URL = "https://sirius.menu/gen2"
local CHUNK_NAME = "=RayfieldGen2"

Rayfield.instance = nil

function Rayfield.load()
	if Rayfield.instance ~= nil then
		return Rayfield.instance
	end

	local fetchOk, body = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, RAYFIELD_URL)
	if not fetchOk then
		return error(string.format("Rayfield.load: download of '%s' failed: %s", RAYFIELD_URL, tostring(body)))
	end

	local chunk, compileError = loadstring(body, CHUNK_NAME)
	if chunk == nil then
		return error(string.format("Rayfield.load: '%s' failed to compile: %s", RAYFIELD_URL, tostring(compileError)))
	end

	local instance = chunk()
	if instance == nil then
		return error(string.format("Rayfield.load: '%s' returned nothing", RAYFIELD_URL))
	end

	Rayfield.instance = instance
	Logger.info("GUI.Rayfield", "loaded", { url = RAYFIELD_URL, bytes = #body })
	return instance
end

return Tracer.instrument("GUI.Rayfield", Rayfield)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Menu"] = [=[
--[[
	Menu â€” Rayfield Gen2 (sesuai dokumentasi resmi).

	=== BUGFIX-C5 (2026-10-01) ===
	User preferences persist across reloads via shared.CTW_WarAI_prefs.
	The old behaviour forced every toggle ON (except the two master switches) on every boot.
]]

local Menu = {}
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Rayfield = require("GUI/Rayfield")
local BorderPrep = require("Features/Military/BorderPrep")

local WINDOW_TITLE = "CTW War AI"
local WINDOW_SUBTITLE = "AI front & economy"
local STATUS_TAB_NAME = "Status"
local UI_SETTINGS_TAB_NAME = "UI Settings"
local SLIDER_STEP_BASE = 10
local HIDDEN_TABS = { Debug = true }
local FORCE_OFF_KEYS = { ["ai.enabled"] = true, ["ai.sendOrders"] = true }

local runService = game:GetService("RunService")

Menu.rayfield = nil
Menu.window = nil
Menu.unloading = false
Menu.toggleRefs = {}
Menu.pendingStatus = {}
Menu.statusStore = {}
Menu.statusText = nil
Menu.advisorText = nil
Menu.lastFlush = 0
Menu.selectedBorderTarget = nil
Menu.borderTargets = {}
Menu.borderDropdown = nil
Menu.borderInfoText = nil
Menu.borderStatusText = nil
Menu._prefUnsubs = {}

local function createSection(tab, title)
	for _, attempt in ipairs({
		function() return tab:CreateSection({ name = title }) end,
		function() return tab:CreateSection({ Name = title }) end,
		function() return tab:CreateSection(title) end,
	}) do
		if pcall(attempt) then return end
	end
end

local function buildStatusArea(tab)
	local placeholder = "Menunggu siklus pertama..."

	local ok, textHandle = pcall(function()
		return tab:CreateText({ name = "AI state", text = placeholder })
	end)
	if ok and textHandle ~= nil then
		Menu.statusText = textHandle
		print("[CTW_WarAI] status widget: CreateText OK")
		return
	end

	local ok2, consoleHandle = pcall(function()
		return tab:CreateConsole({ name = "AI state", height = 180, follow = true, maxLines = 500 })
	end)
	if ok2 and consoleHandle ~= nil then
		Menu.statusText = consoleHandle
		print("[CTW_WarAI] status widget: CreateConsole fallback")
		return
	end

	Menu.statusText = nil
	print("[CTW_WarAI] status widget: none -> console only")
end

local function roundingToStep(rounding)
	rounding = tonumber(rounding) or 0
	if rounding <= 0 then return 1 end
	return 1 / (SLIDER_STEP_BASE ^ rounding)
end

local function makeCallback(definition)
	return function(value)
		Settings.set(definition.key, value, "menu")
	end
end

function Menu.addControl(tab, definition)
	local onValue = makeCallback(definition)

	if definition.kind == "toggle" then
		local ok, toggle = pcall(function()
			return tab:CreateToggle({ name = definition.label, default = definition.default, callback = onValue })
		end)
		if ok and toggle ~= nil then
			Menu.toggleRefs[definition.key] = toggle
		end
		return
	end
	if definition.kind == "slider" then
		pcall(function()
			tab:CreateSlider({
				name = string.format("%s (%s)", definition.label, definition.unit),
				default = definition.default, min = definition.min, max = definition.max,
				step = roundingToStep(definition.rounding), callback = onValue,
			})
		end)
		return
	end
	if definition.kind == "dropdown" then
		pcall(function()
			tab:CreateDropdown({
				name = definition.label, options = definition.choices,
				default = definition.default, callback = onValue,
			})
		end)
		return
	end
end

local function notify(title, content)
	local rayfield = Menu.rayfield
	-- [FIX-H10] Rayfield Gen2 memakai window:Notify; library hanya mengekspos CreateWindow.
	local target = Menu.window or rayfield
	if target == nil then return end
	for _, attempt in ipairs({
		function() return target:Notify({ Title = title, Content = content, Duration = 5 }) end,
		function() return target:Notify({ title = title, content = content, duration = 5 }) end,
		function() return rayfield:Notify(title, content, 5) end,
	}) do
		if pcall(attempt) then return end
	end
end

local function setToggle(toggle, value)
	if toggle == nil then return end
	local ok = pcall(function() toggle:Set(value) end)
	if not ok then
		if type(toggle.SetValue) == "function" then
			pcall(function() toggle:SetValue(value) end)
		end
		pcall(function() toggle.Value = value end)
	end
end

function Menu.setBorderTargets(list)
	Menu.borderTargets = list or {}
	local names = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(Menu.borderTargets) do
		table.insert(names, entry.name)
	end
	if Menu.borderDropdown ~= nil then
		pcall(function() Menu.borderDropdown:Refresh(names) end)
	end
end

function Menu.updateBorderInfo(name)
	if Menu.borderInfoText == nil then return end
	local entry = nil
	for _, e in ipairs(Menu.borderTargets) do
		if e.name == name then entry = e; break end
	end
	if entry == nil then
		pcall(function() Menu.borderInfoText:Set("Target tidak ditemukan.") end)
		return
	end
	local verdict = entry.ratio >= 1.5 and "Strong" or "Weak - akan kirim semua"
	local text = string.format(
		"%s\nOur power: %.0f\nTheir power: %.0f\nRatio: %.2fx %s\nBorder tiles: %d",
		entry.name, entry.ourPower, entry.power, entry.ratio, verdict, entry.borderCount)
	pcall(function() Menu.borderInfoText:Set(text) end)
end

function Menu.setBorderStatus(text)
	if Menu.borderStatusText ~= nil then
		pcall(function() Menu.borderStatusText:Set(text) end)
	end
end

function Menu.build(_, onUnload)
	-- [FIX-D5] reset flag unload supaya build berikutnya tidak melewati tryUnloadRayfield.
	Menu.unloading = false
	Menu.rayfield = Rayfield.load()
	local rayfield = Menu.rayfield

	local window = rayfield:CreateWindow({
		name = WINDOW_TITLE,
		subtitle = WINDOW_SUBTITLE,
		sidebarLayout = true,
	})
	Menu.window = window

	local statusTab = window:CreateTab({ name = STATUS_TAB_NAME })
	createSection(statusTab, "AI state")
	buildStatusArea(statusTab)

	createSection(statusTab, "War Advisor â€” top 3")
	local ok_advisor, advisorText = pcall(function()
		return statusTab:CreateText({ name = "Rekomendasi", text = "(menunggu scan pertama...)" })
	end)
	if ok_advisor and advisorText ~= nil then
		Menu.advisorText = advisorText
	end

	for _, tabLayout in ipairs(Settings.layout()) do
		if not HIDDEN_TABS[tabLayout.name] then
			local tab = window:CreateTab({ name = tabLayout.name })
			for _, section in ipairs(tabLayout.sections) do
				createSection(tab, section.name)
				for _, definition in ipairs(section.definitions) do
					Menu.addControl(tab, definition)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local militaryTab = window:CreateTab({ name = "Military" })
	createSection(militaryTab, "Border Preparation")

	local ok_dd, dd = pcall(function()
		return militaryTab:CreateDropdown({
			name = "Target Country",
			options = { "â€”" },
			default = "â€”",
			callback = function(value)
				if value == "â€”" then return end
				Menu.selectedBorderTarget = value
				Menu.updateBorderInfo(value)
			end,
		})
	end)
	if ok_dd and dd ~= nil then Menu.borderDropdown = dd end

	local ok_info, info = pcall(function()
		return militaryTab:CreateText({
			name = "Target Info",
			text = "(menunggu scan pertama...)",
		})
	end)
	if ok_info and info ~= nil then Menu.borderInfoText = info end

	pcall(function()
		militaryTab:CreateButton({
			name = "Prepare Border",
			callback = function()
				local target = Menu.selectedBorderTarget
				if target == nil or target == "â€”" then
					notify("Border Prep", "Pilih target dulu.")
					return
				end
				BorderPrep.request(target)
				notify("Border Prep", "Request: " .. target .. " (diproses tick berikutnya)")
			end,
		})
	end)

	pcall(function()
		militaryTab:CreateButton({
			name = "Cancel Border Prep",
			callback = function()
				BorderPrep.cancel("user button")
				notify("Border Prep", "Cancelled.")
			end,
		})
	end)

	local ok_status, statusTx = pcall(function()
		return militaryTab:CreateText({
			name = "Border Status",
			text = "(tidak ada border prep aktif)",
		})
	end)
	if ok_status and statusTx ~= nil then Menu.borderStatusText = statusTx end

	createSection(militaryTab, "Idle Units")
	pcall(function()
		militaryTab:CreateToggle({
			name = "Gather idle units near capital",
			default = true,
			callback = function(value)
				BorderPrep.gatherEnabled = value
			end,
		})
	end)

	local uiTab = window:CreateTab({ name = UI_SETTINGS_TAB_NAME })
	createSection(uiTab, "Menu")
	pcall(function()
		uiTab:CreateButton({
			name = "Unload CTW War AI",
			callback = function()
				notify("CTW War AI", "Unloading...")
				pcall(onUnload)
			end,
		})
	end)

	-- [BUGFIX-C5] Persist user preferences across reloads.
	local prefs = shared.CTW_WarAI_prefs
	if prefs == nil then
		prefs = {}
		shared.CTW_WarAI_prefs = prefs
	end

	local onCount, offCount = 0, 0
	for key, toggle in pairs(Menu.toggleRefs) do
		local want
		if FORCE_OFF_KEYS[key] then
			want = false
		elseif prefs[key] ~= nil then
			want = prefs[key]
		else
			want = Settings.get(key)
		end
		Settings.set(key, want, "auto-boot")
		setToggle(toggle, want)
		prefs[key] = want
		if want then onCount += 1 else offCount += 1 end
	end

	-- Register change listeners so user choices persist.
	for key in pairs(Menu.toggleRefs) do
		if not FORCE_OFF_KEYS[key] then
			local unsubscribe = Settings.onChanged(key, function(value)
				prefs[key] = value
			end)
			table.insert(Menu._prefUnsubs, unsubscribe)
		end
	end

	task.delay(0.5, function()
		for key, toggle in pairs(Menu.toggleRefs) do
			local want = prefs[key]
			if want ~= nil then
				setToggle(toggle, want)
			end
		end
	end)

	notify("CTW War AI", string.format("Loaded. %d ON, %d OFF.", onCount, offCount))
end

function Menu.setStatus(key, text)
	Menu.pendingStatus[key] = text
end

function Menu.applyPending()
	if next(Menu.pendingStatus) == nil then return end
	for k, v in pairs(Menu.pendingStatus) do
		Menu.statusStore[k] = v
	end
	table.clear(Menu.pendingStatus)

	local keys = {}
	for k in pairs(Menu.statusStore) do table.insert(keys, k) end
	table.sort(keys)
	local lines = {}
	for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
		table.insert(lines, k .. ": " .. tostring(Menu.statusStore[k]))
	end
	local blob = table.concat(lines, "\n")

	if Menu.statusText ~= nil then
		local ok = pcall(function() Menu.statusText:Set(blob) end)
		if not ok then
			pcall(function() Menu.statusText:Append(blob) end)
		end
	end

	local now = os.clock()
	if now - Menu.lastFlush >= 15.0 then
		Menu.lastFlush = now
		print("[CTW_WarAI status]\n" .. blob)
	end
end

function Menu.startPump()
	return task.spawn(function()
		while Menu.window ~= nil do
			runService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			local ok = xpcall(Menu.applyPending, function(failure)
				Logger.error("Menu", "statusPumpFailed", { message = tostring(failure) })
				return failure
			end)
			if not ok then break end
		end
	end)
end

function Menu.setAdvisor(text)
	if Menu.advisorText == nil then return end
	pcall(function() Menu.advisorText:Set(text) end)
end

function Menu.notifyUser(title, content)
	local rayfield = Menu.rayfield
	-- [FIX-H10] Rayfield Gen2 memakai window:Notify; library hanya mengekspos CreateWindow.
	local target = Menu.window or rayfield
	if target == nil then return end
	for _, attempt in ipairs({
		function() return target:Notify({ Title = title, Content = content, Duration = 10 }) end,
		function() return target:Notify({ title = title, content = content, duration = 10 }) end,
		function() return rayfield:Notify(title, content, 10) end,
	}) do
		if pcall(attempt) then return end
	end
end

local function tryUnloadRayfield(rayfield, window)
	if rayfield ~= nil then
		if type(rayfield.Unload) == "function" then
			local ok = pcall(function() rayfield:Unload() end)
			if ok then return true, "rayfield:Unload" end
		end
		if type(rayfield.Destroy) == "function" then
			local ok = pcall(function() rayfield:Destroy() end)
			if ok then return true, "rayfield:Destroy" end
		end
	end

	if window ~= nil then
		local ok, sg = pcall(function() return window.screenGui end)
		if ok and sg ~= nil and typeof(sg) == "Instance" and sg.Parent ~= nil then
			pcall(function() window:Destroy() end)
			pcall(function() window:Close() end)
			pcall(function() sg:Destroy() end)
			return true, "window.screenGui destroyed"
		end
	end

	local destroyedNames = {}

	local function containsOurWindow(gui)
		if not gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then return false end
		if gui.Name == "AetherUI" then return false end
		for _, desc in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
			if desc:IsA("TextLabel") and desc.Text == "CTW War AI" then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	local containers = {
		game:GetService("CoreGui"),
		game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"),
	}

	for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
		if container ~= nil then
			for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
				if containsOurWindow(child) then
					table.insert(destroyedNames, child.Name)
					pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if #destroyedNames > 0 then
		return true, string.format("destroyed %d ScreenGui(s): %s",
			#destroyedNames, table.concat(destroyedNames, ", "))
	end

	return false, "no Rayfield ScreenGui matched"
end

function Menu.destroy()
	if Menu.window == nil and Menu.rayfield == nil then return end
	if not Menu.unloading then
		Menu.unloading = true
		local ok, how = tryUnloadRayfield(Menu.rayfield, Menu.window)
		print("[CTW_WarAI] rayfield unload:", ok, how or "(no detail)")
	end
	for _, unsubscribe in ipairs(Menu._prefUnsubs) do
		pcall(unsubscribe)
	end
	table.clear(Menu._prefUnsubs)
	Menu.window = nil
	Menu.rayfield = nil
	Menu.statusText = nil
	Menu.advisorText = nil
	table.clear(Menu.toggleRefs)
	table.clear(Menu.pendingStatus)
	table.clear(Menu.statusStore)
	-- [FIX-D6] referensi widget border juga harus dilepas saat unload.
	Menu.borderDropdown = nil
	Menu.borderInfoText = nil
	Menu.borderStatusText = nil
	Menu.borderTargets = {}
	Menu.selectedBorderTarget = nil
end

return Tracer.instrument("Menu", Menu)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/FileSink"] = [=[
local FileSink = {}
FileSink.__index = FileSink
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

function FileSink.new(filePath)
	writefile(filePath, "")
	if not isfile(filePath) then
		return error(string.format("FileSink.new: file '%s' could not be created", filePath))
	end
	local self = setmetatable({}, FileSink)
	self.filePath = filePath
	self.buffer = {}
	self.recordsWritten = 0
	self.bytesWritten = 0
	self.flushCount = 0
	return self
end

function FileSink:push(record)
	table.insert(self.buffer, Serializer.encode(record))
end

function FileSink:flush()
	if #self.buffer == 0 then return end
	local chunk = table.concat(self.buffer, "\n") .. "\n"
	appendfile(self.filePath, chunk)
	self.recordsWritten += #self.buffer
	self.bytesWritten += #chunk
	self.flushCount += 1
	table.clear(self.buffer)
end

function FileSink:stats()
	return {
		filePath = self.filePath, recordsWritten = self.recordsWritten,
		bytesWritten = self.bytesWritten, flushCount = self.flushCount,
		buffered = #self.buffer,
	}
end

return FileSink
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Formula"] = [=[
local Formula = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

local ALLOWED_TIERS = { T0 = true, T1 = true, T2 = true, T3 = true, T4 = true, T5 = true }

Formula.definitions = {}
Formula.logEveryEvaluation = false
Formula.loggedByTrace = {}
Formula.suppressedByTrace = {}

function Formula.define(definition)
	assert(definition.id, "Formula.define: id is required")
	assert(ALLOWED_TIERS[definition.tier], string.format("Formula.define: '%s' has no allowed derivation tier", tostring(definition.id)))
	assert(definition.source, "Formula.define: source of the inputs is required")
	assert(definition.expression, "Formula.define: symbolic expression is required")
	assert(definition.unit, "Formula.define: output unit is required")
	assert(definition.inputs, "Formula.define: input units are required")
	assert(definition.compute, "Formula.define: compute function is required")
	assert(definition.examples and #definition.examples > 0, "Formula.define: at least one worked example is required")
	if Formula.definitions[definition.id] then
		return error(string.format("Formula.define: '%s' is already defined", definition.id))
	end
	Formula.definitions[definition.id] = definition
	for exampleIndex, example in ipairs(definition.examples) do
		local produced = definition.compute(example.inputs)
		local passed
		if type(example.expected) == "number" then
			local tolerance = example.tolerance
			if tolerance == nil then tolerance = 0 end
			passed = type(produced) == "number" and (produced == example.expected or math.abs(produced - example.expected) <= tolerance)
		else
			passed = produced == example.expected
		end
		Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "example", {
			id = definition.id, expression = definition.expression,
			exampleIndex = exampleIndex, inputs = example.inputs,
			expected = example.expected, produced = produced,
			unit = definition.unit, passed = passed,
		})
		if not passed then
			return error(string.format(
				"Formula '%s' example %d drifted: expected %s, produced %s (%s)",
				definition.id, exampleIndex, tostring(example.expected),
				tostring(produced), definition.unit
			))
		end
	end
	return definition
end

function Formula.flushSuppressed(traceId)
	local suppressed = Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId]
	if suppressed ~= nil then
		Logger.debug("Utility/Formula", "suppressedEvaluations", {
			traceId = traceId, counts = suppressed,
			reason = "first evaluation per formula and trace is logged in full; enable formula.logEveryEvaluation to log all",
		})
	end
	Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId] = nil
	Formula.loggedByTrace[traceId] = nil
end

function Formula.describeRejected(candidates, winner, context)
	local rejected = {}
	for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
		if candidate ~= winner then
			table.insert(rejected, {
				id = candidate.id,
				reason = candidate.rejectReason and candidate.rejectReason(context) or "applies() returned false or ranked lower",
			})
		end
	end
	return rejected
end

function Formula.select(decisionName, candidates, context)
	for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
		if candidate.applies(context) then
			Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "branch", {
				decision = decisionName, chosen = candidate.id, reason = candidate.reason,
				rejected = Formula.describeRejected(candidates, candidate, context), context = context,
			})
			return candidate
		end
	end
	return error(string.format("Formula.select: no candidate applies for '%s'", decisionName))
end

function Formula.recordChoice(decisionName, ranked, decidingFact)
	if #ranked == 0 then
		return error(string.format("Formula.recordChoice: '%s' has no candidates", decisionName))
	end
	local losers = {}
	for index, entry in ipairs(ranked) do
		if index > 1 then
			table.insert(losers, { id = entry.id, score = entry.score, facts = entry.facts })
		end
	end
	Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "branch", {
		decision = decisionName, chosen = ranked[1].id, chosenScore = ranked[1].score,
		chosenFacts = ranked[1].facts, rejected = losers, reason = decidingFact,
	})
end

Tracer.instrument("Utility.Formula", Formula)

function Formula.evaluate(id, inputs)
	local definition = Formula.definitions[id]
	if not definition then
		return error(string.format("Formula.evaluate: unknown formula '%s'", id))
	end
	for inputName in pairs(inputs) do
		if not definition.inputs[inputName] then
			return error(string.format("Formula.evaluate: '%s' received undeclared input '%s'", id, inputName))
		end
	end
	for inputName in pairs(definition.inputs) do
		if inputs[inputName] == nil then
			return error(string.format("Formula.evaluate: '%s' is missing input '%s'", id, inputName))
		end
	end

	-- [PERF-FM-1] Fast path: TIDAK melakukan validasi input.
-- Hanya untuk hot path yang sudah dipercaya (mis. CombatModel.hitNumbers).
-- Menghemat 2 iterasi pairs() per panggilan. Formula.evaluate tetap dipakai
-- oleh tools dan caller eksternal yang butuh validasi.
function Formula.evaluateFast(id, inputs)
	local definition = Formula.definitions[id]
	if not definition then
		return error(string.format("Formula.evaluateFast: unknown formula '%s'", id))
	end
	return definition.compute(inputs)
end

	local value = definition.compute(inputs)
	if Logger.accepts("DEBUG") then
		local traceId = "none"
		if Logger.contextProvider then traceId = Logger.contextProvider() end
		local logged = Formula.loggedByTrace[traceId]
		if logged == nil then
			logged = {}
			Formula.loggedByTrace[traceId] = logged
		end
		if Formula.logEveryEvaluation or not logged[id] then
			logged[id] = true
			Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "formula", {
				id = id, expression = definition.expression, tier = definition.tier,
				source = definition.source, inputs = inputs, inputUnits = definition.inputs,
				output = value, unit = definition.unit,
			})
		else
			local suppressed = Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId]
			if suppressed == nil then
				suppressed = {}
				Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId] = suppressed
			end
			suppressed[id] = (suppressed[id] or 0) + 1
		end
	end
	return value
end

return Formula
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Logger"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Logger â€” no-op build (RAM-optimized).
]]

local Logger = {}

Logger.LEVELS = { "TRACE", "DEBUG", "INFO", "WARN", "ERROR" }
Logger.activeLevel = "ERROR"
Logger.counts = { TRACE = 0, DEBUG = 0, INFO = 0, WARN = 0, ERROR = 0 }
Logger.lastByLevel = {}
Logger.sinks = {}
Logger.pendingRecords = {}
Logger.contextProvider = nil

function Logger.accepts(_) return false end
function Logger.setLevel(_) end
function Logger.addSink(_) end
function Logger.removeSink(_) end
function Logger.flush() end
function Logger.write() end

function Logger.trace() end
function Logger.debug() end
function Logger.info() end
function Logger.warn() end
function Logger.error() end

return Logger
