MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Maid"] = [=[
local Maid = {}
Maid.__index = Maid
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

function Maid.new(ownerName)
	local self = setmetatable({}, Maid)
	self.ownerName = ownerName
	self.tasks = {}
	return self
end

function Maid:clean()
	local releasedCounts = {}
	local errors = {}
	for index = #self.tasks, 1, -1 do
		local ownedTask = self.tasks[index]
		local taskType = typeof(ownedTask)
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			if taskType == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				ownedTask:Disconnect()
			elseif taskType == "thread" then
				if coroutine.status(ownedTask) ~= "dead" then
					task.cancel(ownedTask)
				end
			elseif taskType == "Instance" then
				ownedTask:Destroy()
			elseif taskType == "function" then
				ownedTask()
			elseif type(ownedTask) == "table" and type(ownedTask.Destroy) == "function" then
				ownedTask:Destroy()
			end
		end)
		if not ok then
			table.insert(errors, tostring(err))
		end
		releasedCounts[taskType] = (releasedCounts[taskType] or 0) + 1
	end
	table.clear(self.tasks)
	if #errors > 0 then
		print("[Maid] clean errors:", table.concat(errors, " | "))
	end
	return releasedCounts
end

Tracer.instrument("Utility.Maid", Maid)

function Maid:give(ownedTask)
	local taskType = typeof(ownedTask)
	local supported = taskType == "RBXScriptConnection"
		or taskType == "thread" or taskType == "Instance" or taskType == "function"
		or (taskType == "table" and type(ownedTask.Destroy) == "function")
	if not supported then
		return error(string.format("Maid:give(%s): unsupported task type '%s'", self.ownerName, taskType))
	end
	table.insert(self.tasks, ownedTask)
	return ownedTask
end

function Maid:count() return #self.tasks end

return Maid
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Profiler"] = [=[
local Profiler = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

local PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX = 2
Profiler.stats = {}

function Profiler.run(label, callee, ...)
	local startClock = os.clock()
	local results = table.pack(pcall(callee, ...))
	local durationSeconds = os.clock() - startClock
	local labelStats = Profiler.stats[label]
	if labelStats == nil then
		labelStats = { count = 0, totalSeconds = 0, maxSeconds = 0, lastSeconds = 0, failures = 0 }
		Profiler.stats[label] = labelStats
	end
	labelStats.count += 1
	labelStats.totalSeconds += durationSeconds
	labelStats.lastSeconds = durationSeconds
	labelStats.maxSeconds = math.max(labelStats.maxSeconds, durationSeconds)
	if not results[1] then labelStats.failures += 1 end
	if Logger.accepts("DEBUG") then
		Logger.debug("Utility.Profiler", "profile", {
			label = label, durationSeconds = durationSeconds, succeeded = results[1],
		})
	end
	if not results[1] then
		return error(string.format("[%s] %s", tostring(label), tostring(results[PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX])), 0)
	end
	return table.unpack(results, PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX, results.n)
end

function Profiler.snapshot()
	local copy = {}
	for label, labelStats in pairs(Profiler.stats) do
		copy[label] = table.clone(labelStats)
	end
	return copy
end

return Profiler
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Resolver"] = [=[
local Resolver = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

Resolver.cache = {}
Resolver.cacheHits = 0
Resolver.cacheMisses = 0

function Resolver.requireService(serviceName)
	local serviceOk, service = pcall(game.GetService, game, serviceName)
	if not serviceOk or service == nil then
		return error(string.format("Resolver: service '%s' unavailable: %s", serviceName, tostring(service)))
	end
	return service
end

function Resolver.requireDescendant(root, path)
	local cacheKey = root:GetFullName() .. "|" .. path
	local cached = Resolver.cache[cacheKey]
	if cached ~= nil and cached:IsDescendantOf(game) then
		Resolver.cacheHits += 1
		return cached
	end
	Resolver.cacheMisses += 1
	local current = root
	for _, segment in ipairs(string.split(path, "/")) do
		local nextInstance = current:FindFirstChild(segment)
		if nextInstance == nil then
			return error(string.format("Resolver: missing '%s' under '%s' (stopped at '%s')", path, root:GetFullName(), current:GetFullName()))
		end
		current = nextInstance
	end
	Resolver.cache[cacheKey] = current
	Logger.debug("Utility.Resolver", "resolve", {
		root = root:GetFullName(), path = path,
		className = current.ClassName, cachedPreviously = cached ~= nil,
	})
	return current
end

function Resolver.awaitChild(parent, childName, timeoutSeconds, timeoutSource)
	local existing = parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
	if existing ~= nil then return existing end
	local arrivedSignal = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local arrived = nil
	local connection = parent.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == childName then
			arrived = child
			arrivedSignal:Fire()
		end
	end)
	local timeoutThread = task.delay(timeoutSeconds, function() arrivedSignal:Fire() end)
	arrivedSignal.Event:Wait()
	connection:Disconnect()
	if coroutine.status(timeoutThread) == "suspended" then
		task.cancel(timeoutThread)
	end
	arrivedSignal:Destroy()
	if arrived == nil then
		return error(string.format(
			"Resolver: '%s' did not appear under '%s' within %s s (timeout from %s)",
			childName, parent:GetFullName(), tostring(timeoutSeconds), timeoutSource
		))
	end
	return arrived
end

function Resolver.invalidate(reason)
	local droppedCount = 0
	for _ in pairs(Resolver.cache) do droppedCount += 1 end
	table.clear(Resolver.cache)
	Logger.info("Utility.Resolver", "invalidated", { reason = reason, dropped = droppedCount })
end

Tracer.instrument("Utility.Resolver", Resolver)

function Resolver.requireChild(parent, childName)
	local child = parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
	if child == nil then
		return error(string.format("Resolver: missing '%s' under '%s'", childName, parent:GetFullName()))
	end
	return child
end

function Resolver.optionalChild(parent, childName, absenceReason)
	assert(type(absenceReason) == "string" and absenceReason ~= "", "Resolver.optionalChild: absenceReason is required")
	return parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
end

function Resolver.requireAttribute(instance, attributeName, expectedType)
	local value = instance:GetAttribute(attributeName)
	if typeof(value) ~= expectedType then
		return error(string.format(
			"Resolver: attribute '%s' on '%s' is %s, expected %s",
			attributeName, instance:GetFullName(), typeof(value), expectedType
		))
	end
	return value
end

function Resolver.optionalAttribute(instance, attributeName, expectedType, absenceReason)
	assert(type(absenceReason) == "string" and absenceReason ~= "", "Resolver.optionalAttribute: absenceReason is required")
	local value = instance:GetAttribute(attributeName)
	if value ~= nil and typeof(value) ~= expectedType then
		return error(string.format(
			"Resolver: attribute '%s' on '%s' is %s, expected %s or absent (%s)",
			attributeName, instance:GetFullName(), typeof(value), expectedType, absenceReason
		))
	end
	return value
end

return Resolver
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Serializer"] = [=[
local Serializer = {}
local httpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

function Serializer.toJsonSafe(value, seen)
	local valueType = typeof(value)
	if valueType == "nil" or valueType == "boolean" then return value end
	if valueType == "number" then
		if value ~= value then return { ["$number"] = "NaN" } end
		if value == math.huge then return { ["$number"] = "Infinity" } end
		if value == -math.huge then return { ["$number"] = "-Infinity" } end
		return value
	end
	if valueType == "string" then
		if utf8.len(value) then return value end
		local hexBytes = string.gsub(value, ".", function(character)
			return string.format("%02x", string.byte(character))
		end)
		return { ["$bytesHex"] = hexBytes }
	end
	if valueType == "Instance" then
		return { ["$instance"] = value:GetFullName(), ["$class"] = value.ClassName }
	end
	if valueType == "Vector3" then return { ["$Vector3"] = { value.X, value.Y, value.Z } } end
	if valueType == "Vector2" then return { ["$Vector2"] = { value.X, value.Y } } end
	if valueType == "CFrame" then return { ["$CFrame"] = { value:GetComponents() } } end
	if valueType == "Color3" then return { ["$Color3"] = { value.R, value.G, value.B } } end
	if valueType == "BrickColor" then return { ["$BrickColor"] = value.Name } end
	if valueType == "EnumItem" then return { ["$Enum"] = tostring(value) } end
	if valueType == "UDim2" then
		return { ["$UDim2"] = { value.X.Scale, value.X.Offset, value.Y.Scale, value.Y.Offset } }
	end
	if valueType == "function" then
		local sourceName, lineNumber, functionName = debug.info(value, "sln")
		return { ["$function"] = { source = sourceName, line = lineNumber, name = functionName } }
	end
	if valueType == "table" then
		if seen[value] then return { ["$cycle"] = true } end
		seen[value] = true
		local keyCount = 0
		for _ in pairs(value) do keyCount += 1 end
		local isArray = keyCount == #value
		if isArray then
			for index = 1, #value do
				if value[index] == nil then isArray = false; break end
			end
		end
		local converted = {}
		if isArray then
			for index = 1, #value do
				converted[index] = Serializer.toJsonSafe(value[index], seen)
			end
		else
			for key, entry in pairs(value) do
				local keyText = type(key) == "string" and key or ("[" .. typeof(key) .. "]" .. tostring(key))
				converted[keyText] = Serializer.toJsonSafe(entry, seen)
			end
		end
		seen[value] = nil
		return converted
	end
	return { ["$" .. valueType] = tostring(value) }
end

function Serializer.encode(value)
	return httpService:JSONEncode(Serializer.toJsonSafe(value, {}))
end

function Serializer.decode(text, sourceName)
	local decodeOk, decoded = pcall(httpService.JSONDecode, httpService, text)
	if not decodeOk then
		return error(string.format("Serializer.decode: '%s' is not valid JSON: %s", sourceName, tostring(decoded)))
	end
	return decoded
end

return Serializer
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/IntelManager"] = [=[
local IntelManager = {}
IntelManager.__index = IntelManager

function IntelManager.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, IntelManager)
	self.sightings = {}
	self.threatMap = {}
	self.forceEstimate = {}
	self.pattern = { aggressiveness = 0.5, buildPhase = false, lastUnitCount = 0, unitGrowthRate = 0 }
	self.fogMemory = {}
	self.SIGHTING_EXPIRY = 45
	self.SIGHTING_LONG_EXPIRY = 180
	self.REGION_SIZE = 6
	self.THREAT_DECAY = 0.92
	self.MIN_THREAT = 0.01
	self.lastScanTime = 0
	self.SCAN_INTERVAL = 1.0
	self.lastPatternUpdate = 0
	self.PATTERN_INTERVAL = 5.0
	return self
end

function IntelManager:regionKey(tx, tz)
	return math.floor(tx / self.REGION_SIZE) .. "," .. math.floor(tz / self.REGION_SIZE)
end

function IntelManager:regionCenter(key)
	local rx, rz = key:match("(-?%d+),(-?%d+)")
	rx, rz = tonumber(rx), tonumber(rz)
	return rx * self.REGION_SIZE + 3, rz * self.REGION_SIZE + 3
end

function IntelManager:unitThreatValue(typeId)
	local tv = {
		[1] = 1.0, [2] = 1.5, [3] = 3.0, [4] = 2.0,
		[5] = 0.8, [6] = 1.2, [7] = 4.0, [8] = 2.5, [9] = 1.8,
	}
	return tv[typeId] or 1.0
end

function IntelManager:isInOurVision(tx, tz)
	if not warState or not warState.ourUnits then return false end
	local VR = 18
	for _, u in pairs(warState.ourUnits) do
		if u and u.Health > 0 then
			local ux, uz = getUnitTile(u)
			if distance(ux, uz, tx, tz) <= VR then return true end
		end
	end
	return false
end

function IntelManager:getVisibleEnemies()
	local list = {}
	if not warState or not warState.units then return list end
	for uid, u in pairs(warState.units) do
		if u.NationId ~= ourNationId and u.Health > 0 then
			local tx, tz = getUnitTile(u)
			if self:isInOurVision(tx, tz) then
				table.insert(list, {
					unitId = uid, tileX = tx, tileZ = tz,
					typeId = u.TypeId, hp = u.Health,
					maxHp = u.MaxHealth, nationId = u.NationId,
				})
			end
		end
	end
	return list
end

function IntelManager:scan()
	local now = tick()
	if now - self.lastScanTime < self.SCAN_INTERVAL then return end
	self.lastScanTime = now

	for _, e in ipairs(self:getVisibleEnemies()) do
		local old = self.sightings[e.unitId]
		if old then
			old.lastSeen = now
			old.tileX = e.tileX
			old.tileZ = e.tileZ
			old.hp = e.hp
			old.isStale = false
		else
			self.sightings[e.unitId] = {
				firstSeen = now, lastSeen = now,
				tileX = e.tileX, tileZ = e.tileZ,
				typeId = e.typeId, hp = e.hp, maxHp = e.maxHp,
				isStale = false, staleness = 0,
			}
		end
	end

	for uid, s in pairs(self.sightings) do
		local age = now - s.lastSeen
		if age > self.SIGHTING_LONG_EXPIRY then
			self.sightings[uid] = nil
		else
			s.isStale = age > self.SIGHTING_EXPIRY
			s.staleness = math.min(age / self.SIGHTING_EXPIRY, 1.0)
		end
	end

	self:rebuildThreatMap()
	self:estimateForces()
	self:updatePattern()
end

function IntelManager:rebuildThreatMap()
	local now = tick()

	for k, r in pairs(self.threatMap) do
		r.score = r.score * self.THREAT_DECAY
		r.enemyCount = r.enemyCount * self.THREAT_DECAY
		-- [FIX-H11] friendlyCount harus dihitung ulang tiap scan; kalau tidak, ia menekan threat ke 0.
		r.friendlyCount = 0
		if r.score < self.MIN_THREAT then self.threatMap[k] = nil end
	end

	for uid, s in pairs(self.sightings) do
		local k = self:regionKey(s.tileX, s.tileZ)
		if not self.threatMap[k] then
			self.threatMap[k] = {score = 0, enemyCount = 0, friendlyCount = 0, lastUpdate = now}
		end
		local r = self.threatMap[k]
		local w = s.isStale and (1.0 - s.staleness * 0.7) or 1.0
		r.score = r.score + self:unitThreatValue(s.typeId) * w
		r.enemyCount = r.enemyCount + w
		r.lastUpdate = now
	end

	if warState and warState.ourUnits then
		for _, u in pairs(warState.ourUnits) do
			if u and u.Health > 0 then
				local ux, uz = getUnitTile(u)
				local k = self:regionKey(ux, uz)
				if not self.threatMap[k] then
					self.threatMap[k] = {score = 0, enemyCount = 0, friendlyCount = 0, lastUpdate = now}
				end
				self.threatMap[k].friendlyCount = self.threatMap[k].friendlyCount + 1
			end
		end
	end
end

function IntelManager:estimateForces()
	local counts = {}
	for uid, s in pairs(self.sightings) do
		if not s.isStale then
			counts[s.typeId] = (counts[s.typeId] or 0) + 1
		end
	end

	for tid, count in pairs(counts) do
		if not self.forceEstimate[tid] then
			self.forceEstimate[tid] = {estimated = count, peak = count, trend = 0}
		end
		local e = self.forceEstimate[tid]
		e.peak = math.max(e.peak, count)
		e.estimated = math.max(count, math.floor(e.peak * 1.5))
	end

	local total = 0
	for _, c in pairs(counts) do total = total + c end

	local prev = self.pattern.lastUnitCount
	self.pattern.lastUnitCount = total
	self.pattern.unitGrowthRate = prev > 0 and ((total - prev) / self.SCAN_INTERVAL) or 0
	self.pattern.buildPhase = self.pattern.unitGrowthRate > 0.5

	return total
end

function IntelManager:updatePattern()
	local now = tick()
	if now - self.lastPatternUpdate < self.PATTERN_INTERVAL then return end
	self.lastPatternUpdate = now

	local offensive, total = 0, 0
	for uid, s in pairs(self.sightings) do
		if not s.isStale then
			total = total + 1
			if self:isNearOurTerritory(s.tileX, s.tileZ) then
				offensive = offensive + 1
			end
		end
	end

	if total > 0 then
		local ratio = offensive / total
		local growth = math.clamp(self.pattern.unitGrowthRate * 0.5, 0, 1)
		self.pattern.aggressiveness = math.clamp(
			ratio * 0.6 + growth * 0.2 + self.pattern.aggressiveness * 0.2, 0, 1
		)
	end
end

function IntelManager:isNearOurTerritory(tx, tz)
	if not warState or not warState.ourUnits then return false end
	for _, u in pairs(warState.ourUnits) do
		if u and u.Health > 0 then
			local ux, uz = getUnitTile(u)
			if distance(ux, uz, tx, tz) <= 15 then return true end
		end
	end
	return false
end

function IntelManager:getEffectiveThreat(key)
	local r = self.threatMap[key]
	if not r then return 0 end
	return math.max(r.enemyCount * 3.0 - r.friendlyCount * 0.5, 0)
end

function IntelManager:getHighestThreatNear(tx, tz, maxR)
	maxR = maxR or 30
	local best, bestScore = nil, 0
	for k, r in pairs(self.threatMap) do
		local rx, rz = self:regionCenter(k)
		local d = math.max(distance(rx, rz, tx, tz), 1)
		if d <= maxR then
			local ws = r.score / d
			if ws > bestScore then bestScore = ws best = k end
		end
	end
	if best then
		local rx, rz = self:regionCenter(best)
		return rx, rz, self.threatMap[best]
	end
	return nil, nil, nil
end

function IntelManager:getState()
	return {
		aggressiveness = self.pattern.aggressiveness,
		buildPhase = self.pattern.buildPhase,
		visibleEnemies = self.pattern.lastUnitCount,
	}
end

return IntelManager
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Humanizer"] = [=[
local Humanizer = {}
Humanizer.__index = Humanizer

function Humanizer.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, Humanizer)
	self.config = {
		minDelay = 0,
		maxDelay = 0,
		burstMinActions = 3,
		burstMaxActions = 7,
		burstPauseMin = 0.3,
		burstPauseMax = 1.8,
		idleChancePerCycle = 0.02,
		idleDurationMin = 2.0,
		idleDurationMax = 8.0,
		pathJitterChance = 0.15,
		pathJitterMax = 2,
		majorActionCooldown = 1.2,
		maxUnitsPerCycle = 8,
		unitSelectionVariance = 3,
		decisionDelayMin = 0.15,
		decisionDelayMax = 0.8,
	}
	self.burstCount = 0
	self.burstTarget = 0
	self.nextBurstPause = 0
	self.idleUntil = 0
	self.lastMajorAction = 0
	self.cycleCount = 0
	self.lastJitteredPositions = {}
	self.unitSeeds = {}
	return self
end

function Humanizer:rand(min, max)
	return min + math.random() * (max - min)
end

function Humanizer:randInt(min, max)
	return math.floor(min + math.random() * (max - min + 1))
end

function Humanizer:shouldPause()
	return false
end

function Humanizer:preOrder()
	self.burstCount = self.burstCount + 1
	self.lastMajorAction = tick()
	return 0
end

function Humanizer:executeOrder(orderFunc, ...)
	if self:shouldPause() then
		task.wait(self:rand(self.config.burstPauseMin, self.config.burstPauseMax))
	end

	local delay = self:preOrder()
	if delay > 0 then task.wait(delay) end
	return orderFunc(...)
end

function Humanizer:jitterPosition(unitId, targetX, targetZ)
	if math.random() > self.config.pathJitterChance then
		return targetX, targetZ
	end

	local jitter = self:randInt(1, self.config.pathJitterMax)
	local offset_x = self:randInt(-jitter, jitter)
	local offset_z = self:randInt(-jitter, jitter)
	local jx = targetX + offset_x
	local jz = targetZ + offset_z
	self.lastJitteredPositions[unitId] = {x = jx, z = jz}
	return jx, jz
end

function Humanizer:selectUnits(unitTable)
	local all = {}
	for k, v in pairs(unitTable) do
		table.insert(all, {key = k, value = v})
	end

	local count = math.min(
		#all,
		self:randInt(
			self.config.maxUnitsPerCycle - self.config.unitSelectionVariance,
			self.config.maxUnitsPerCycle + self.config.unitSelectionVariance
		)
	)

	for i = #all, 2, -1 do
		local j = math.random(1, i)
		all[i], all[j] = all[j], all[i]
	end

	local result = {}
	for i = 1, count do
		result[all[i].key] = all[i].value
	end
	return result
end

function Humanizer:beginCycle()
	self.cycleCount = self.cycleCount + 1
	self.lastJitteredPositions = {}
end

return Humanizer
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Signal"] = [=[
local Signal = {}
Signal.__index = Signal
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

local RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL = 2

function Signal.new(signalName)
	local self = setmetatable({}, Signal)
	self.signalName = signalName
	self.listeners = {}
	self.fireCount = 0
	self.failureCount = 0
	return self
end

function Signal:connect(listener)
	assert(type(listener) == "function", string.format("Signal(%s):connect needs a function", self.signalName))
	table.insert(self.listeners, listener)
	return function()
		local index = table.find(self.listeners, listener)
		if index ~= nil then table.remove(self.listeners, index) end
	end
end

-- [PERF-SG-1] table.clone hanya saat listener > 4. Untuk listener sedikit
-- (mayoritas signal di sini: 1-3), iterasi langsung tanpa alokasi. Risiko
-- re-entrant modify rendah di sinyal-sinyal tersebut.
local SIGNAL_CLONE_THRESHOLD = 4

function Signal:fire(...)
	self.fireCount += 1
	local count = #self.listeners
	if count == 0 then return end

	local signalName = self.signalName
	local listeners
	if count > SIGNAL_CLONE_THRESHOLD then
		listeners = table.clone(self.listeners)
	else
		listeners = self.listeners
	end
	for _, listener in ipairs(listeners) do
		local listenerOk = xpcall(listener, function(failure)
			Logger.error("Utility.Signal", "listenerFailed", {
				signal = signalName, message = tostring(failure),
			}, debug.traceback(tostring(failure), RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL))
			return failure
		end, ...)
		if not listenerOk then self.failureCount += 1 end
	end
end

function Signal:stats()
	return {
		signal = self.signalName, listeners = #self.listeners,
		fired = self.fireCount, failures = self.failureCount,
	}
end

function Signal:Destroy() table.clear(self.listeners) end

return Signal
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Stats"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Stats

	=== BUGFIX-C4 (2026-10-01) ===
	pushBounded is the shared helper used by every module that collects samples
	(hit intervals, break errors, reaction latencies, acceptance latencies,
	action history). It caps the list to `maxCount` by dropping the oldest entry.

	Without this, a long session grew those tables without bound.
]]

local Stats = {}
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

local MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR = 2
local DEFAULT_MAX_SAMPLES = 512

function Stats.median(samples)
	local count = #samples
	if count == 0 then return nil end
	local sorted = table.clone(samples)
	table.sort(sorted)
	local middle = (count + 1) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
	return (sorted[math.floor(middle)] + sorted[math.ceil(middle)]) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
end

function Stats.max(samples)
	local largest = nil
	for _, value in ipairs(samples) do
		if largest == nil or value > largest then largest = value end
	end
	return largest
end

-- [BUGFIX-C4] Push with a fixed upper bound.
function Stats.pushBounded(list, value, maxCount)
	maxCount = maxCount or DEFAULT_MAX_SAMPLES
	table.insert(list, value)
	local overflow = #list - maxCount
	if overflow > 0 then
		-- [FIX-D3] satu kali table.move, bukan table.remove(list, 1) berulang yang menggeser seluruh list.
		local oldLength = #list
		table.move(list, overflow + 1, oldLength, 1)
		for index = oldLength, oldLength - overflow + 1, -1 do
			list[index] = nil
		end
	end
	return #list
end

return Tracer.instrument("Utility.Stats", Stats)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Tracer"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Tracer â€” no-op build (RAM-optimized).
]]

local Tracer = {}

Tracer.traceCounter = 0
Tracer.currentTraceId = "none"
Tracer.frames = {}

function Tracer.context() return "none", 0 end
function Tracer.withTrace(_, callee, ...)
	local results = table.pack(xpcall(function(...)
		return callee(...)
	end, function(err)
		return debug.traceback(tostring(err), 2)
	end, ...))
	if not results[1] then
		error(results[2], 0)
	end
	return table.unpack(results, 2, results.n)
end
function Tracer.step() end
function Tracer.describeValue(v) return v end
function Tracer.describeValues() return {} end
function Tracer.wrapFunction(_, callee) return callee end
function Tracer.instrument(_, moduleTable) return moduleTable end

return Tracer
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["WarAI"] = [=[
--[[
	WarAI â€” versi ringan + push status dari Scheduler hooks.
]]

local WarAI = { attached = false }
local IntelManager = require("Utility/IntelManager")
local Humanizer = require("Utility/Humanizer")
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")
local Profiler = require("Utility/Profiler")
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")
local SettingsCatalog = require("Features/Core/SettingsCatalog")
local Rayfield = require("GUI/Rayfield")
local Menu = require("Menu")
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")
local Remotes = require("Game/Remotes")
local Session = require("Game/Session")
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")
local Units = require("Game/Units")
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")
local TechCatalog = require("Features/Economy/TechCatalog")
local EconomyPlanner = require("Features/Economy/EconomyPlanner")
local OrderExecutor = require("Features/Orders/OrderExecutor")
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")
local ArmyControl = require("Features/Orders/ArmyControl")
local AirPlanner = require("Features/Air/AirPlanner")
local Scorecard = require("Features/Review/Scorecard")
local PlanOverlay = require("Features/Review/PlanOverlay")
local WarAdvisor = require("Features/Diplomacy/WarAdvisor")
local AntiIdle = require("Features/Core/AntiIdle")
local Scheduler = require("Features/Core/Scheduler")
local Plan = require("Features/Front/Plan")
local PeacetimePrep = require("Features/Diplomacy/PeacetimePrep")
local BorderPrep = require("Features/Military/BorderPrep")
local ThreatHeat = require("Features/Front/ThreatHeat")

local RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL = 2
local STATUS_KEYS = {}

local maid = nil
local worldMaid = nil

function WarAI.defineCoreSettings()
	SettingsCatalog.defineAll()
end

function WarAI.buildWorld(reason)
	if worldMaid ~= nil then worldMaid:clean() end
	worldMaid = Maid.new("WarAI.world")
	Units.reset(reason)
	Tick.reset(reason)
	Resolver.invalidate(reason)
	-- [FIX-B3] state lintas-rebuild harus dibuang; kalau tidak, heat & lastStates menumpuk.
	ThreatHeat.reset()
	table.clear(Scheduler.lastStates)
	-- [FIX-P1-4/P1-5] state lintas-rebuild di modul diplomatik juga harus dibuang.
	WarAdvisor.lastRunTick = -100
	BorderPrep.reset(reason)
	PeacetimePrep.reset(reason)
	local mapModel = Session.mapModel()
	MapGraph.build(mapModel, worldMaid)
	Ownership.attach(worldMaid)
	local country = Session.ourCountry()
	Units.attach(mapModel, country and country.Name or nil, worldMaid)
	Tick.attach(MapGraph, worldMaid)
	Scheduler.refreshContext(country and country.Name or nil)
end

function WarAI.onCountryChanged(country, reason)
	local countryName = country and country.Name or nil
	Units.setOurCountry(countryName)
	OrderExecutor.reset("country changed")
	ActionDispatcher.reset("country changed")
	Resolver.invalidate("country changed")
	Plan.clearRecentlyCleared()
	Scheduler.refreshContext(countryName)
	WarAdvisor.notifiedCountries = {}
	if countryName ~= nil then
		Scheduler.requestTick({ reason = "country changed" })
	end
end

function WarAI.onTick(world, analysis, plan)
	EconomyPlanner.run(world, analysis)
	ArmyControl.run(world, analysis)
	AirPlanner.run(world, plan)
	-- [AIR-r5] Fitur airfield management baru.
	AirPlanner.runUpgrades(world, plan)
	AirPlanner.runReposition(world, plan)
	AirPlanner.runBuild(world)
	AirPlanner.runPriority(world, plan)
	AirPlanner.runNuke(world, plan)
	-- [TODO-NAVY] aktifkan saat fitur navy dipakai
	Ownership.verifyCounts()
	-- [FIX-H12] resolveStopped tidak pernah dipanggil: predictions menumpuk & attacksLost selalu 0.
	Scorecard.resolveStopped(world)

	local prepSummary = PeacetimePrep.summary()
	if prepSummary.active then
		if prepSummary.totalDeficit > 0 then
			Menu.setStatus("peacetime", string.format("%d borders, deficit %d: %s",
				prepSummary.borders, prepSummary.totalDeficit,
				table.concat(prepSummary.details, " ")))
		else
			Menu.setStatus("peacetime", string.format("%d borders, all guarded (4 each)",
				prepSummary.borders))
		end
	else
		Menu.setStatus("peacetime", "-")
	end

	local borderScan = BorderPrep.scan(world)
	Menu.setBorderTargets(borderScan)
	local borderPrepSummary = BorderPrep.summary()
	if borderPrepSummary ~= nil then
		Menu.setBorderStatus(string.format(
			"Active: %s â€” %d units, target power %.0f, %s",
			borderPrepSummary.target, borderPrepSummary.unitCount,
			borderPrepSummary.targetPower,
			borderPrepSummary.strong and "STRONG" or "WEAK"))
	else
		Menu.setBorderStatus("(tidak ada border prep aktif)")
	end

	local best = WarAdvisor.update(world, world.tickNumber)
	Menu.setAdvisor(WarAdvisor.formatTop3(WarAdvisor.recommendations, 3))
	if best ~= nil and not WarAdvisor.notifiedCountries[best.name] then
		WarAdvisor.notifiedCountries[best.name] = true
		Menu.notifyUser("War Advisor", string.format(
			"Consider justifying war on %s\nWin %.0f%% | Power %.2fx",
			best.name, best.winProbability * 100, best.powerRatio))
	end
end

function WarAI.attach()
	maid = Maid.new("WarAI")
	WarAI.defineCoreSettings()

	-- [FIX-GUI-ORDER] Muat GameModules + TechCatalog SEBELUM Menu.build().
	-- Menu.build() memanggil getAvailableTechOptions() yang membaca TechCatalog.entries;
	-- kalau TechCatalog belum di-build, dropdown "Add Tech to Queue" gagal dibuat dan
	-- hilang dari GUI tanpa pesan error (di-swallow pcall).
	if Session.isCorrectPlace() then
		local okModules, errModules = pcall(GameModules.loadAll)
		if okModules then
			pcall(Remotes.verifyAll)
			pcall(TechCatalog.build)
		else
			Logger.error("WarAI", "gameModulesLoadFailed", { message = tostring(errModules) })
		end
	end

	-- [FIX-GUI] Rayfield bisa gagal (fetch kosong / executor memblokir HttpGet). Dulu itu
	-- membatalkan SELURUH boot. Sekarang GUI opsional: AI tetap jalan tanpa GUI.
	local menuOk, menuFailure = pcall(Menu.build, STATUS_KEYS, WarAI.detach)
	if not menuOk then
		warn(string.format("[CTW_WarAI] GUI gagal dimuat, AI tetap jalan tanpa GUI: %s", tostring(menuFailure)))
		shared.CTW_GuiError = tostring(menuFailure)
	end

	maid:give(Menu.destroy)
	local pumpOk, pumpThread = pcall(Menu.startPump)
	if pumpOk and pumpThread ~= nil then
		maid:give(pumpThread)
	end

	if not Session.isCorrectPlace() then
		Logger.error("WarAI", "wrongPlace", { placeId = game.PlaceId })
		WarAI.attached = true
		return
	end

	for _, signal in ipairs({
		Session.countryChanged, Session.mapReplaced,
		MapGraph.tileColorChanged, MapGraph.tileAttributeChanged,
		Ownership.ownerChanged, Units.unitAdded, Units.unitMoved,
		Units.unitRemoved, Units.unitChanged, Tick.dayTick,
	}) do
		maid:give(signal)
	end

	maid:give(Session.countryChanged:connect(WarAI.onCountryChanged))
	maid:give(Session.mapReplaced:connect(function(reason)
		maid:give(task.spawn(function()
			local ok = pcall(WarAI.buildWorld, reason)
			if not ok then
				Logger.error("WarAI", "worldRebuildFailed", { reason = reason })
			end
		end))
	end))
	maid:give(function()
		if worldMaid ~= nil then worldMaid:clean(); worldMaid = nil end
	end)

	WarAI.buildWorld("attach")
	OrderExecutor.attach(maid)
	ActionDispatcher.attach(maid)
	Scorecard.attach(maid)
	AntiIdle.attach(maid)

	Scheduler.attach(maid, {
		onVerdict = function(verdict)
			Menu.setStatus("state", verdict.reason or "?")
			local c = verdict.country
			Menu.setStatus("country", c and c.Name or "none")
		end,
		onPlanned = function(world, plan, summary)
			Scorecard.recordLaunches(world, plan, summary.sent > 0)
			-- [FIX-D8] PlanOverlay tidak pernah dimuat; draw() sendiri yang mengecek ui.showPlans.
			PlanOverlay.draw(plan, worldMaid)
			local hpSum, woundedCount = 0, 0
			for _, u in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
				local h = u.health or 0
				hpSum += h
				if h < 60 then woundedCount += 1 end
			end
			local avgHP = #world.ourUnits > 0 and math.floor(hpSum / #world.ourUnits) or 0
			Menu.setStatus("units", string.format("ours %d (avg HP %d, wounded %d), enemy %d",
				#world.ourUnits, avgHP, woundedCount, #world.enemyUnits))
			Menu.setStatus("tick", string.format("%.3f s (%d passes)", Tick.periodSeconds() or 0, Tick.passCount))
			-- [FIX-D4] plan:summary() dipanggil sekali, bukan dua kali.
			local planSummary = plan:summary()
			Menu.setStatus("plan", string.format("%d orders, %d halts", planSummary.orders, planSummary.halts))
			Menu.setStatus("orders", string.format("sent %d, accepted %d, dry-run %d",
				OrderExecutor.stats.sent, OrderExecutor.stats.accepted, OrderExecutor.stats.dryRun))
			Menu.setStatus("scorecard", string.format("won %d, lost %d, open %d",
				Scorecard.summary().attacksWon, Scorecard.summary().attacksLost, Scorecard.summary().open))
			if EconomyPlanner.lastSummary then
				Menu.setStatus("economy", string.format("money %.0f, research %s",
					EconomyPlanner.lastSummary.money,
					EconomyPlanner.lastSummary.research and tostring(EconomyPlanner.lastSummary.research.chosen) or "-"))
			end
		end,
		onTick = WarAI.onTick,
	})

	Session.attach(maid)
	WarAI.attached = true
end

function WarAI.detach()
	if maid == nil then return end
	WarAI.attached = false
	print("[CTW_WarAI] detaching...")

	local ok, err = pcall(function() maid:clean() end)
	if not ok then
		print("[CTW_WarAI] detach error:", tostring(err))
	end
	maid = nil
	worldMaid = nil

	pcall(function()
		local Rayfield = require("GUI/Rayfield")
		Rayfield.instance = nil
	end)

	if shared.CTW_WarAI == WarAI then
		shared.CTW_WarAI = nil
	end
	print("[CTW_WarAI] detached")
end

function WarAI.status()
	return { attached = WarAI.attached }
end

return Tracer.instrument("WarAI", WarAI)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Navy/NavyFormulas"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Navy/NavyFormulas
	Declared navy arithmetic. Sources: NavyCommon.GetShipPrice (decompile),
	PortsMenuLocalScript.UpdatePortLevel (client mirror), verified against
	CTW_NavyDump.txt samples (45/39.6/35.36 TS, 60/52.8/47.14 DD+BB).
]]

local NavyFormulas = {}
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

local PORT_SPEED_BONUS_MAX = 1.5
local SHIP_BASE_DURATION = {
	["Transport"]  = 45,
	["Destroyer"]  = 60,
	["Battleship"] = 60,
	["Submarine"]  = 60,
}
NavyFormulas.SHIP_BASE_DURATION = SHIP_BASE_DURATION

Formula.define({
	id = "navy.shipPrice",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "NavyCommon.GetShipPrice (decompiled, line 14-16)",
	expression = "floor((tech.unitPrice or tech.price or 0) * Navy.<Type>BuildCostCoef)",
	unit = "money",
	inputs = { techUnitPrice = "tech unitPrice (nil when absent)", techPrice = "tech price fallback", typeBuildCostCoef = "Navy.<Type>BuildCostCoef" },
	compute = function(i) return math.floor((i.techUnitPrice or i.techPrice or 0) * i.typeBuildCostCoef) end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { techUnitPrice = nil, techPrice = 30000, typeBuildCostCoef = 1 }, expected = 30000, tolerance = 0 },
		{ inputs = { techUnitPrice = nil, techPrice = 55000, typeBuildCostCoef = 1 }, expected = 55000, tolerance = 0 },
		{ inputs = { techUnitPrice = nil, techPrice = 80000, typeBuildCostCoef = 1 }, expected = 80000, tolerance = 0 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "navy.shipBuildDuration",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "PortsMenuLocalScript.UpdatePortLevel:340, verified against CTW_NavyDump.txt",
	expression = "baseDuration / (1 + (clamp(level,1,hardCap)-1)/(hardCap-1)*1.5) / shipsBuildDurationMultiplier",
	unit = "days",
	inputs = { baseDuration = "TS=45, DD/BB/SS=60", portLevel = "Port.level", hardCap = "Techs.shipyardLevelHardCap", buildDurationMultiplier = "Techs.shipsBuildDurationMultiplier" },
	compute = function(i)
		local hardCap = math.max(i.hardCap, 1)
		local level = math.clamp(i.portLevel, 1, hardCap)
		local bonus = 1 + (level - 1) / math.max(hardCap - 1, 1) * PORT_SPEED_BONUS_MAX
		return i.baseDuration / bonus / math.max(i.buildDurationMultiplier, 0.01)
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { baseDuration = 45, portLevel = 1, hardCap = 12, buildDurationMultiplier = 1 }, expected = 45.0,  tolerance = 1e-3 },
		{ inputs = { baseDuration = 45, portLevel = 2, hardCap = 12, buildDurationMultiplier = 1 }, expected = 39.6,  tolerance = 1e-2 },
		{ inputs = { baseDuration = 45, portLevel = 3, hardCap = 12, buildDurationMultiplier = 1 }, expected = 35.36, tolerance = 1e-2 },
		{ inputs = { baseDuration = 60, portLevel = 1, hardCap = 12, buildDurationMultiplier = 1 }, expected = 60.0,  tolerance = 1e-3 },
		{ inputs = { baseDuration = 60, portLevel = 2, hardCap = 12, buildDurationMultiplier = 1 }, expected = 52.8,  tolerance = 1e-2 },
		{ inputs = { baseDuration = 60, portLevel = 3, hardCap = 12, buildDurationMultiplier = 1 }, expected = 47.14, tolerance = 1e-2 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "navy.portUpgradePrice",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "PortsMenuLocalScript.UpdatePortLevel:340, matched on 17/17 dump ports",
	expression = "shipyardPrice * (1 + shipyardPriceMultiplier * portLevel)",
	unit = "money",
	inputs = { shipyardPrice = "Techs.shipyardPrice", shipyardPriceMultiplier = "Techs.shipyardPriceMultiplier", portLevel = "Port.level" },
	compute = function(i) return i.shipyardPrice * (1 + i.shipyardPriceMultiplier * i.portLevel) end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { shipyardPrice = 5000, shipyardPriceMultiplier = 0.1, portLevel = 1 }, expected = 5500, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { shipyardPrice = 5000, shipyardPriceMultiplier = 0.1, portLevel = 2 }, expected = 6000, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { shipyardPrice = 5000, shipyardPriceMultiplier = 0.1, portLevel = 3 }, expected = 6500, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "navy.fleetCombatPower",
	tier = "T1",
	source = "proxy: sum(attack * health/maxHealth); not a server formula, only for fleet comparison",
	expression = "sum(ship.attack * ship.health / ship.maxHealth)",
	unit = "effective attack",
	inputs = { ships = "array of { attack, health, maxHealth }" },
	compute = function(i)
		local total = 0
		for _, s in ipairs(i.ships) do
			local r = s.maxHealth > 0 and (s.health / s.maxHealth) or 0
			total += (s.attack or 0) * r
		end
		return total
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { ships = { { attack = 0, health = 50, maxHealth = 50 }, { attack = 10, health = 120, maxHealth = 120 } } }, expected = 10, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { ships = { { attack = 7.2613, health = 54.2712, maxHealth = 120 }, { attack = 0, health = 50, maxHealth = 50 }, { attack = 0, health = 13.3322, maxHealth = 50 } } }, expected = 7.2613 * (54.2712 / 120), tolerance = 1e-4 },
	},
})

function NavyFormulas.baseDurationFor(classCode)
	local map = { TS = "Transport", DD = "Destroyer", BB = "Battleship", SS = "Submarine" }
	local key = map[classCode]
	if key == nil then return nil, nil end
	return key, SHIP_BASE_DURATION[key]
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Navy.NavyFormulas", NavyFormulas)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Navy/NavyModel"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Navy/NavyModel
	Pure read model. Snapshot ports + fleets + techs, no remote calls.
]]

local NavyModel = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

local SEA_NAME_FRAGMENT = "Sea"
local NAVY_ATTRIBUTE_KEYS = {
	"TransportAttackCoef", "TransportBuildCostCoef",
	"DestroyerAttackCoef", "DestroyerSpeedCoef", "DestroyerBuildCostCoef",
	"SubmarineAttackCoef", "SubmarineSpeedCoef", "SubmarineBuildCostCoef",
	"BattleshipAttackCoef", "BattleshipSpeedCoef", "BattleshipHealthCoef", "BattleshipBuildCostCoef",
	"shipSpeedCoef",
}
local TECH_KEYS = { "maxShipyardLevel", "shipyardLevelHardCap", "shipyardPrice", "shipyardPriceMultiplier", "shipsBuildDurationMultiplier" }

local function num(inst, key, default)
	local v = inst:GetAttribute(key)
	if type(v) == "number" then return v end
	return default
end

local function decodeLoaded(raw)
	if type(raw) ~= "string" or raw == "" or raw == "[]" then return {} end
	local ok, d = pcall(Serializer.decode, raw, "ship.loadedDivisions")
	if ok and type(d) == "table" then return d end
	return {}
end

function NavyModel._shipSnapshot(objectValue)
	local snap = {
		name = objectValue.Name, objectValue = objectValue,
		shipType = objectValue:GetAttribute("shipType") or "?",
		class = objectValue:GetAttribute("class") or "?",
		health = num(objectValue, "health", 0),
		maxHealth = num(objectValue, "maxHealth", 0),
		attack = num(objectValue, "attack", 0),
		maxAttack = num(objectValue, "maxAttack", 0),
		speed = num(objectValue, "speed", 0),
		AAPower = num(objectValue, "AAPower", 0),
		divisionCapacity = num(objectValue, "divisionCapacity", 0),
		buildDuration = num(objectValue, "buildDuration", 0),
		progress = num(objectValue, "progress", 0),
	}
	snap.loadedDivisions = decodeLoaded(objectValue:GetAttribute("loadedDivisions"))
	snap.loadedCount = #snap.loadedDivisions
	snap.healthRatio = snap.maxHealth > 0 and (snap.health / snap.maxHealth) or 0
	return snap
end

function NavyModel._buildQueueSnapshot(item)
	return {
		name = item.Name, objectValue = item,
		shipType = item:GetAttribute("shipType") or "?",
		progress = num(item, "progress", 0),
		buildDuration = num(item, "buildDuration", 0),
	}
end

local function fleetSnapshot(model, tileName)
	local e = {
		tileName = tileName, model = model,
		country = model:GetAttribute("country"),
		modelName = model:GetAttribute("ModelName"),
		state = model:GetAttribute("state"),
		currentPath = model:GetAttribute("currentPath"),
		AAPower = num(model, "AAPower", 0),
		ships = {}, shipCount = 0, totalHealth = 0, totalMaxHealth = 0, totalAttack = 0,
	}
	local shipsFolder = model:FindFirstChild("Ships")
	if shipsFolder ~= nil then
		for _, ship in ipairs(shipsFolder:GetChildren()) do
			local s = NavyModel._shipSnapshot(ship)
			table.insert(e.ships, s)
			e.shipCount += 1
			e.totalHealth += s.health
			e.totalMaxHealth += s.maxHealth
			e.totalAttack += s.attack
		end
	end
	return e
end

function NavyModel.snapshot(world)
	local state = {
		ports = {}, ourFleets = {}, enemyFleets = {},
		navyAttributes = {}, techs = {},
		shipsByPortName = {}, fleetsByTileName = {},
		totalOurShips = 0, totalOurTransports = 0, totalOurEscorts = 0,
	}
	local country = world.meInstance
	local navy = country:FindFirstChild("Navy")
	local techs = country:FindFirstChild("Techs")
	local cities = country:FindFirstChild("Cities")

	if navy ~= nil then
		for _, k in ipairs(NAVY_ATTRIBUTE_KEYS) do state.navyAttributes[k] = num(navy, k, 1) end
	end
	if techs ~= nil then
		for _, k in ipairs(TECH_KEYS) do state.techs[k] = num(techs, k, nil) end
	end
	if cities ~= nil then
		for _, city in ipairs(cities:GetChildren()) do
			local port = city:FindFirstChild("Port")
			if port ~= nil then
				local entry = {
					cityName = city.Name, cityFolder = city, portFolder = port,
					tileName = city:GetAttribute("tileName"),
					level = num(port, "level", 1), docked = {}, buildQueue = {},
				}
				local ds = port:FindFirstChild("DockedShips")
				if ds ~= nil then
					for _, s in ipairs(ds:GetChildren()) do table.insert(entry.docked, NavyModel._shipSnapshot(s)) end
				end
				local bq = port:FindFirstChild("BuildQueue")
				if bq ~= nil then
					for _, i in ipairs(bq:GetChildren()) do table.insert(entry.buildQueue, NavyModel._buildQueueSnapshot(i)) end
				end
				table.insert(state.ports, entry)
				state.shipsByPortName[entry.cityName] = entry
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(state.ports, function(a, b) return a.cityName < b.cityName end)

	local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
	if map ~= nil then
		for _, tile in ipairs(map:GetChildren()) do
			if string.find(tile.Name, SEA_NAME_FRAGMENT, 1, true) then
				for _, child in ipairs(tile:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Model") then
						local fleet = fleetSnapshot(child, tile.Name)
						if fleet.country == world.me then
							table.insert(state.ourFleets, fleet)
							state.fleetsByTileName[tile.Name] = fleet
							state.totalOurShips += fleet.shipCount
							for _, s in ipairs(fleet.ships) do
								if s.class == "TS" then state.totalOurTransports += 1
								else state.totalOurEscorts += 1 end
							end
						else
							table.insert(state.enemyFleets, fleet)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Navy.NavyModel", "snapshot", {
		ports = #state.ports, ourFleets = #state.ourFleets, enemyFleets = #state.enemyFleets,
		totalOurShips = state.totalOurShips, totalOurTransports = state.totalOurTransports,
		totalOurEscorts = state.totalOurEscorts,
	})
	return state
end

function NavyModel.portsByPriority(navyState)
	local sorted = table.clone(navyState.ports)
	table.sort(sorted, function(a, b)
		local aQ = #a.buildQueue > 0
		local bQ = #b.buildQueue > 0
		if aQ ~= bQ then return aQ end
		if a.level ~= b.level then return a.level > b.level end
		return a.cityName < b.cityName
	end)
	return sorted
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Navy.NavyModel", NavyModel)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Navy/NavyPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Navy/NavyPlanner
	Conservative v1: buy ships, upgrade shipyards, sortie with escorted transports.
	No invasion, no fleet movement (v2).
]]

local NavyPlanner = {}
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")
local NavyModel = require("Features/Navy/NavyModel")
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

local TIER = "N"
local SHIP_TECH_BY_CLASS = { TS = "Transport Ship I", DD = "Destroyer I", BB = "Battleship I", SS = "Submarine I" }
local SHIP_TYPE_BY_CLASS = { TS = "Transport Ship I", DD = "Destroyer I", BB = "Battleship I", SS = "Submarine I" }
local TYPE_KEY_BY_CLASS = { TS = "Transport", DD = "Destroyer", BB = "Battleship", SS = "Submarine" }
local TARGET_TRANSPORTS = 2
local TARGET_ESCORTS_PER_TRANSPORT = 2
local RESERVE_MONEY = 15000
local UPGRADE_RESERVE_MONEY = 20000

NavyPlanner.lastSummary = nil

local function moneyOf(world) return Resolver.requireAttribute(world.meInstance, "money", "number") end

local function techProgress(world, name)
	local techs = world.meInstance:FindFirstChild("Techs")
	if techs == nil then return 0 end
	local t = techs:FindFirstChild(name)
	if t == nil then return 0 end
	local p = t:GetAttribute("progress")
	return type(p) == "number" and p or 0
end

local function techPriceOf(world, name)
	local techs = world.meInstance:FindFirstChild("Techs")
	if techs == nil then return nil end
	local t = techs:FindFirstChild(name)
	if t == nil then return nil end
	return t, t:GetAttribute("unitPrice"), t:GetAttribute("price")
end

local function classIsUnlocked(world, classCode)
	local name = SHIP_TECH_BY_CLASS[classCode]
	if name == nil then return false end
	return techProgress(world, name) >= 1
end

local function chooseNextClass(world, navyState)
	local transports = navyState.totalOurTransports
	local escorts = navyState.totalOurEscorts
	for _, port in ipairs(navyState.ports) do
		for _, ship in ipairs(port.docked) do
			if ship.class == "TS" then transports += 1
			elseif ship.class == "DD" or ship.class == "BB" or ship.class == "SS" then escorts += 1 end
		end
	end
	if transports < TARGET_TRANSPORTS and classIsUnlocked(world, "TS") then
		return "TS", string.format("transports %d < target %d", transports, TARGET_TRANSPORTS)
	end
	if escorts < TARGET_TRANSPORTS * TARGET_ESCORTS_PER_TRANSPORT and classIsUnlocked(world, "DD") then
		return "DD", string.format("escorts %d < target %d", escorts, TARGET_TRANSPORTS * TARGET_ESCORTS_PER_TRANSPORT)
	end
	return nil, "soft cap reached"
end

local function priceOfClass(world, navyState, classCode)
	local name = SHIP_TECH_BY_CLASS[classCode]
	if name == nil then return nil end
	local techInstance, unitPrice, price = techPriceOf(world, name)
	if techInstance == nil then return nil end
	local coefKey = TYPE_KEY_BY_CLASS[classCode] .. "BuildCostCoef"
	return Formula.evaluate("navy.shipPrice", {
		techUnitPrice = unitPrice,
		techPrice = price,
		typeBuildCostCoef = navyState.navyAttributes[coefKey] or 1,
	})
end

local function choosePurchasePort(navyState)
	local sorted = NavyModel.portsByPriority(navyState)
	for _, port in ipairs(sorted) do
		if #port.buildQueue == 0 then return port end
	end
	return nil
end

function NavyPlanner.planPurchases(world, navyState, budget)
	local classCode, reason = chooseNextClass(world, navyState)
	if classCode == nil then return { skipped = true, reason = reason } end

	local price = priceOfClass(world, navyState, classCode)
	if price == nil then return { skipped = true, reason = "no price for " .. tostring(classCode) } end

	if budget.money < price + RESERVE_MONEY then
		return { skipped = true, reason = "money below price+reserve", price = price, money = budget.money }
	end

	local port = choosePurchasePort(navyState)
	if port == nil then return { skipped = true, reason = "every port has a build queue" } end

	local shipType = SHIP_TYPE_BY_CLASS[classCode]
	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "TryPurchaseShip",
		args = { port.cityName, shipType },
		key = "buyShip:" .. port.cityName .. ":" .. shipType,
		reason = string.format("buy %s at %s (L%d, %.0f money): %s", shipType, port.cityName, port.level, price, reason),
		tier = TIER,
	})
	budget.money -= price
	return { classCode = classCode, shipType = shipType, port = port.cityName, price = price }
end

function NavyPlanner.planUpgrades(world, navyState, budget)
	local hardCap = navyState.techs.shipyardLevelHardCap or 12
	local maxShipyard = navyState.techs.maxShipyardLevel or 6
	local effectiveCap = math.min(hardCap, maxShipyard)

	local candidates = {}
	for _, port in ipairs(navyState.ports) do
		if port.level < effectiveCap then
			table.insert(candidates, { port = port, hasQueue = #port.buildQueue > 0, level = port.level })
		end
	end
	table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
		if a.hasQueue ~= b.hasQueue then return a.hasQueue end
		if a.level ~= b.level then return a.level > b.level end
		return a.port.cityName < b.port.cityName
	end)

	local pick = candidates[1]
	if pick == nil then return { skipped = true, reason = "no port below cap" } end

	local price = Formula.evaluate("navy.portUpgradePrice", {
		shipyardPrice = navyState.techs.shipyardPrice,
		shipyardPriceMultiplier = navyState.techs.shipyardPriceMultiplier,
		portLevel = pick.port.level,
	})
	if budget.money < price + UPGRADE_RESERVE_MONEY then
		return { skipped = true, reason = "money below upgrade+reserve", price = price, money = budget.money }
	end

	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "TryUpgradeShipyard",
		args = { pick.port.cityFolder },
		key = "upgradePort:" .. pick.port.cityName .. ":" .. tostring(pick.port.level),
		reason = string.format("upgrade %s L%d -> L%d (%.0f money)", pick.port.cityName, pick.port.level, pick.port.level + 1, price),
		tier = TIER,
	})
	budget.money -= price
	return { port = pick.port.cityName, price = price, fromLevel = pick.port.level }
end

function NavyPlanner.planFormations(world, navyState)
	local bestPort = nil
	for _, port in ipairs(navyState.ports) do
		local hasT, hasE = false, false
		for _, ship in ipairs(port.docked) do
			if ship.class == "TS" then hasT = true
			elseif ship.class == "DD" or ship.class == "BB" or ship.class == "SS" then hasE = true end
		end
		if hasT and hasE then
			if bestPort == nil or port.level > bestPort.level then bestPort = port end
		end
	end
	if bestPort == nil then return { skipped = true, reason = "no port with transport+escort" } end

	local ships = {}
	for _, ship in ipairs(bestPort.docked) do table.insert(ships, ship.objectValue) end
	if #ships == 0 then return { skipped = true, reason = "port has no docked ships" } end

	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "TryExitPortWithFleet",
		args = { bestPort.cityFolder, ships },
		key = "exitPort:" .. bestPort.cityName .. ":" .. tostring(#ships),
		reason = string.format("sortie from %s (L%d, %d ships)", bestPort.cityName, bestPort.level, #ships),
		tier = TIER,
		onResult = function(ok, results)
			if not ok then Logger.warn("Features.Navy.NavyPlanner", "exitPortRefused", { port = bestPort.cityName, results = results }) end
		end,
	})
	return { port = bestPort.cityName, shipCount = #ships }
end

function NavyPlanner.run(world)
	if not Settings.get("navy.enabled") then return end
	if world == nil or world.meInstance == nil then return end

	local navyState = NavyModel.snapshot(world)
	local budget = { money = moneyOf(world) }
	local summary = {
		moneyAtStart = budget.money,
		ourFleets = #navyState.ourFleets,
		enemyFleets = #navyState.enemyFleets,
		ourShips = navyState.totalOurShips,
	}

	if Settings.get("navy.purchase") then
		summary.purchase = NavyPlanner.planPurchases(world, navyState, budget)
		summary.upgrade = NavyPlanner.planUpgrades(world, navyState, budget)
		summary.formation = NavyPlanner.planFormations(world, navyState)
	end

	summary.moneyAfter = moneyOf(world)
	NavyPlanner.lastSummary = summary
	Logger.info("Features.Navy.NavyPlanner", "planned", summary)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Navy.NavyPlanner", NavyPlanner)
]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Main"] = [=[
--[[
	Main â€” entry point.
]]

local WarAI = require("WarAI")
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
local Profiler = require("Utility/Profiler")
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

local BOOT_ERROR_PATH = "CTW_WarAI/logs/boot_error.json"
local BOOT_ERROR_TXT_PATH = "CTW_WarAI_boot_error.txt"
local RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL = 2

local function onInitializeError(failure)
	local stackText = debug.traceback(tostring(failure), RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL)
	Logger.error("Main", "initializeFailed", { message = tostring(failure) }, stackText)
	Logger.flush()
	if #Logger.sinks == 0 then
		pcall(writefile, BOOT_ERROR_PATH, Serializer.encode({
			message = tostring(failure), traceback = stackText, pendingRecords = Logger.pendingRecords,
		}))
	end
	pcall(writefile, BOOT_ERROR_TXT_PATH, tostring(failure) .. "\n" .. stackText)
	shared.CTW_BootError = { message = tostring(failure), traceback = stackText }
	pcall(function() WarAI.detach() end)
	warn(string.format("[CTW_WarAI] boot failed: %s\n%s", tostring(failure), stackText))
	return failure
end

local function initializeScript()
	if shared.CTW_WarAI then
		shared.CTW_WarAI.detach()
	end
	shared.CTW_BootError = nil
	shared.CTW_WarAI = WarAI
	WarAI.attach()
end

Profiler.run("Main_InitializeScript", function(...)
	return xpcall(initializeScript, onInitializeError, ...)
end)

return WarAI
]=]

-- ===============================================================================================
-- EPILOGUE LOADER
-- ===============================================================================================

local function readModuleSource(modulePath)
	local source = MODULE_SOURCES[modulePath]
	if source == nil then
		return error(string.format("Loader: module '%s' is not in the bundle", modulePath))
	end
	return source
end

local moduleCache = {}
local loadingStack = {}
local nativeRequire = require

local function requireModule(modulePath)
	if typeof(modulePath) == "Instance" then
		return nativeRequire(modulePath)
	end

	local cached = moduleCache[modulePath]
	if cached ~= nil then
		return cached.value
	end

	for _, loadingPath in ipairs(loadingStack) do
		if loadingPath == modulePath then
			return error(string.format("Loader: circular require of '%s' (stack: %s)", modulePath, table.concat(loadingStack, " -> ")))
		end
	end

	local source = readModuleSource(modulePath)
	local chunk, compileError = loadstring("return function(require) " .. source .. "\nend", "=" .. modulePath)

	if chunk == nil then
		return error(string.format("Loader: '%s' failed to compile: %s", modulePath, tostring(compileError)))
	end

	table.insert(loadingStack, modulePath)

	local loadOk, moduleValue = xpcall(function()
		return chunk()(requireModule)
	end, function(failure)
		return debug.traceback(tostring(failure), 2)
	end)

	table.remove(loadingStack)

	if not loadOk then
		return error(string.format("Loader: '%s' failed to load:\n%s", modulePath, tostring(moduleValue)), 0)
	end

	if moduleValue == nil then
		return error(string.format("Loader: '%s' returned nil", modulePath))
	end

	moduleCache[modulePath] = { value = moduleValue }

	return moduleValue
end

local bootOk, bootResult = pcall(requireModule, "Main")

if not bootOk then
	local text = tostring(bootResult)
	pcall(writefile, "CTW_WarAI_boot_error.txt", text)
	print("[CTW_WarAI] module load failed:\n" .. text)
	warn("[CTW_WarAI] boot failed:\n" .. text)
	error(bootResult, 0)
end

return bootResult
